“It looks like the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families are planning to join hands to take down the pirates in the Yangtze River?”

“Yes.”

“Haha... right. I would think that too.”

Shaolin and Wudang were now leading the Nine Great Sects. The Namgung family, who could be called the leader of the Five Great Families, joined forces with the two sects.

Of course, even if the strength of the three were combined, it would only be 30 percent of the power of the Justice faction of the Central Plains. Still, from the perspective of those watching, it would seem like all the Justice factions were joining in.

Chung Myung, who had been listening quietly, smiled.

“Actually, it would be good if they completely took down the Yangtze River. And even if not, would you say that those great sects will take advantage of the fact that they are in the Yangtze River and clean up all the unsightly people?”

“I-it could happen!”

Since not all sects move with one mind and goal, it was impossible to fully understand what would happen. But, looking at the situation, the reason for the move was clear.

Someone mumbled.

“But no matter what, nothing happened till now, and now suddenly....”

It wasn’t just one or two sects.

How many cities did Hong Dae-Kwang mention? This meant that war was happening in at least five places. If they included the small sects that weren’t mentioned because they weren’t important enough for the emergency report, it surely had to be around a dozen.

This meant that the war was spreading like wildfire.

“It’s all so different. They see things entirely differently now.”

“Because Shaolin and Wudang moved?”

“Not just those two, but even the Namgung family, which has not stepped out of their lands for decades till now.”

“... now that you say it.”

“It isn’t normal for a large sect to move. You couldn’t possibly realize how strange it is as Mount Hua decided to explode wherever it went....”

Chung Myung raised his eyes sharply.

“What? Are you saying we should light a fire on our butts and explode?”

“I-I didn’t mean that....”

“It did seem like it.”

Hyun Sang sighed, looking at Hong Dae-Kwang, who was starting to sweat.

“Branch leader. Chung Myung, ignore what he said, and branch leader, just say what you want to.”

“... I want to say, but I am scared of the consequences....”

“The sect leader will stop him.”

In the meantime, it was Hyun Sang who didn’t commit to stopping him.

“Well... since the Demonic Sect war, the major sects have rarely left their lands. Their movement may indicate that the peaceful kangho we’ve known so far will no longer continue.”

“Hmm.”

“Besides, isn’t the target of that movement the Evils Faction, not the Justice faction? History has shown that once the strength is sufficient, Evil factions get subjugated. So usually....”

“Haha. It starts with the bandits.”

“...”

Everyone’s eyes turned to Im So-Byeong. Clear tears flowed from his eyes.

“Just as expected! I knew it! There has already been news about three mountain bandits being attacked! Oh my, it must be so intense that the sect leaders are so driven now...”

“Stop crying and talk.”

“N-No. If you want to cry, cry, and if you want to laugh, laugh.”

“Laugh if I laugh...”

Im So-Byeong sighed as if the ground was sinking beneath him.

“First of all, I told everyone not to fight back and to run to the next mountain if someone attacks. It is a shame because the places where we worked gave advance notice to the surrounding bandits about the Justice factions, and we did some business with....”

“Business?”

“It is life! If you want to make a living, you have to do it!”

“...”

Bandits had come down the mountain and did business in the city?

“... what even is in your head...”

“Sect leader. You spoke out. You need to think.”

“Ahem. Apologies for that.”

Im So-Byeong shook his head.

“Surely I didn’t make transactions with bandits; we entrusted it to the merchants in the city. Anyway, those places will survive, but even those groups will be swept away in this wind, so I wonder how many more will get destroyed before...”

He trailed off and sighed. Chung Myung spoke with a sullen face.

“What are you sighing for? It is good if bandits get destroyed.”

“...I am a bandit, too. A bandit.”

“It is karma. Get rid of it now.”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who was listening to this nonsense, cleared his throat and continued.

“That movement alone would be enough to notice something strange, but there’s another side to this.”

“What now?”

“...Taking down the Evils Faction is the best. But now there are places that can be on par with the Five Great Families and Nine Great Sects, right?”

“Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“Mount Hua.”

“... that.”

Hyun Young sighed.

“So, to the other Justice faction sects, this looks like the Heavenly Friends Alliance, Five Great Families, and Nine Great Sects are collaborating to take down the Yangtze River 18 families?”

“Who wouldn’t say that?”

It was like that wherever you looked.

The members of Mount Hua and Heavenly Friends Alliance knew that their ties with the Nine Great Sects were probably worse than their ties with the Evils Faction. But no other sect would think that far.

Then, for sure, it would look like they joined forces to handle the Yangtze River pirates.

“Huge sects have an influence on those around them. The reason why a sect cannot set foot in a place that has a large sect isn’t because of its strength, but because it manages the land. This is because the local sects that support the big sects are destroying the major ones that have power over the land. Be it a Justice or Evil one.”

Everyone nodded.

The reason why there was no Evils Faction in the west of Shaanxi, where the Hwa-young clan held sway, was because there was Southern Edge. The sects that operated on the power of Southern Edge cut down the Evils Faction before they set foot close to it.

Wasn’t it because Southern Edge was visiting the place where the armed forces of the Ten Thousand People clan attacked Xi’an in the first place?

“And this is the same with the Evil Faction. In other words, until now, the sects around the Yangtze River were all tilted towards evil acts, riding on the power of the pirates. So, the justice factions endured it till now....”

“And they began to counterattack at the sight of an opening?”

“Yes. At least, I think so.”

“Hmm...”

Hyun Jong exhaled.

‘This is bad.’

The battle taking place there might not be a huge thing now. The problem is if the fight extends.

“Gang-buk, where most Justice faction sects are located, and Gangnam, where the Evil factions are also located, were divided by the Yangtze River.”

“Yes. This is a border between the two sides. But that border... is now being destroyed. They even went so far as to not care about anything....”

Everyone turned their heads to one person.

“Why?”

“....”

“No, who cares if the Justice faction is in the Center and the Evil faction in the Outskirts? They just want to make a living by doing business! In front of money, who cares if you are evil or right!”

Although it was true, it wasn’t something that a Taoist should say.

“Look, branch leader.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“What do you think will happen now? From the looks of it, it doesn’t seem like it will simply end like this. Maybe...”

Hyun Jong couldn’t even say anything out loud.

He was going to ask if there was a possibility of a major war between the sides, but the words felt too ominous to even be said out loud.

But Hong Dae-Kwang was quick to understand what he was trying to say.

“I don’t think it will go that far.”

“Why do you think so?”

“Because they are the Evil faction.”

“Hmm?”

“If an Evil faction attacks Wudang, Shaolin will help, and if Southern Edge is attacked, Mount Hua will....”

“Join to hit them more.”

“... that was a wrong example. Well, as is natural, Evil factions don’t help each other. If the pirates are attacked, rather than helping, there is a high chance that more will run to rip it down more.”

“Haha. They seem like idiots.”

“And the Evil faction isn’t?”

Hong Dae-Kwang nodded.

“Yes. So, in my opinion, there is a high chance that the bandits will be cleared out in Gang-buk, and the pirates will suffer damage, too. Is there any sect that would stand against the Shaolin, Wudang, or Namgung family?”

“...those are tough names to stand against.”

“Ugh! Why did you blow up Mount Hua!”

Hong Dae-Kwang smiled bitterly at Hyun Young’s joke.

“The problem is that because of that incident, the perception that the Justice Faction and Evil Faction are at war is spreading among people. Once that spreads widely, you never know when or where something shocking will happen.”

“Right. True. They will no longer tolerate things they tolerated in the past. Either this way or that.”

“Yes. That’s the problem... well, I don’t think this will spread further now.”

“Then we are lucky.”

But at that moment, Chung Myung crossed his arms and chuckled.

“You think that will happen?”

“Uh?”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who looked at him, was unsure, but Chung Myung didn’t bother explaining his thoughts.

‘In the end, it is all about the Evil Faction bastards moving...’

If one thought realistically, Hong Dae-Kwang’s words made sense.

But what if there was someone who predicted the entire situation from the start, and everyone just played into their hands?

Then, that person would take advantage of this situation.

“People will never do everything by common sense. Especially that guy.”

“That guy?”

Chung Myung smiled.

‘This is why people are scarier than ghosts or monsters.’

Chung Myung’s gaze turned to the south.

To that place where that person, who was like the embodiment of desires, would surely be watching this entire thing with a smile.

“The Water Dragon Pirates were attacked by Shaolin. I received word that Captain Sima has abandoned the pirates and is on the run!”

“It is said that the Wudang are advancing towards the Blue Wind Pirates! They have requested support.”

“The Namgung family head is moving to the North! W-we need to figure out something....”

A middle-aged man seated on a black throne looked down at the whole scene.

The throne he sat on was so black it felt like it could swallow you whole. His clothes were all black, and even his long beard and rough-growing hair were nearly as dark.

But what most resembled the color of darkness were his eyes. Those dark eyes stared at those waiting.

“Huh, Black Dragon King!”

Hearing the cry, the man’s eyebrows twitched.

The Yangtze River’s 18 Families dominated the river, and that was a supreme name that only one person who controlled the pirates could have.

No matter how strong the 18 River Families were, if they were caught between the Nine Great Sects and the Heavenly Friends Alliance, they were insignificant.

Shaolin and Wudang of the Nine Great Sects, the Namgung family of the Five Great Families, and even the Sichuan Tang of the Heavenly Friends Alliance could match up alone with the 18 River Families.

How could the 18 River Families of the Yangtze fight against such forces alone?

Considering that even the sects that had yet to join would inevitably do so, it was as if more than half the sects in the world were flocking to the Yangtze River.

“So...”

“Yes, Black Dragon King.”

“Measures?”

“...”

Everyone fell silent at the words of the Black Dragon King.

“I guess you didn’t mean to say that you people only have mouths and no solutions.”

“...”

“Come up with a solution.”

Those who were thinking glanced at each other.

This was like a bell tied around a cat’s neck. Even if they knew the answer, they didn’t dare to honestly speak to the Black Dragon King.

“You don’t answer?”

The Black Dragon King’s voice turned harsh.

“Normally, pirates run their mouths, but when it is time to open them, you close them. In that case, it can be said that both the mouth and head are useless.”

The faces of those thinking turned pale.

Could the Black Dragon King be anything else?

He was a man who wouldn’t even bat an eye if everyone there had their heads cut off.

In the end, one of them, unable to handle the pressure, cautiously opened his mouth.

“I am sorry to tell you...”

“Speak.”

“... there is a saying that one should avoid the falling rain. They say they are attacking the Yangtze River now... this is a subjugation. Considering where they are based, they wouldn’t be able to stay long in Yangtze.”

“... so?”

“We take refuge, order the subordinates to leave the Yangtze River and take shelter, and then return when the rain stops...”

Swish!

The person speaking fell with blood dripping from him.

Kwang!

But no one even looked in that direction. They simply bowed their heads, praying the wrath wouldn’t come to them.

The Black Dragon King, who had reduced a person to dead flesh with one gesture, stood up from the throne.

“You bastards are so good at saying such things! What? Abandon the Yangtze River and take refuge?! I! Are you saying that I, the Dragon of the River, should run from the bastards of the Justice faction? Me!?”

Everyone held their breath at the roar.

“Think who you are speaking to again! Say it again!”

It was said that when the dragon of the Yangtze River became angry, the Yangtze River became engulfed in a storm. The Black Dragon King’s anger was enough to cover their base with killing intent, if not the Yangtze River.

“B-Black Dragon King. Even if one is a tiger, the king of the living, one cannot handle a pack of wolves attacking and biting. If the crocodiles come in droves to bite, wouldn’t it be wise to run for now?”

Swish!

But this time, too, the person who spoke the idiom fell, blood leaking from his body.

“You idiots! How can you compare it to something like that? I am a dragon who doesn’t have to care about a crocodile!”

If one didn’t speak, their head would fall. If they did, they would be killed.

The reason why a treacherous person was placed next to a tyrant was simple. If one could not say something that suited the tyrant’s tastes, they would run.

Of course, if one looked at history, there were people who put their names on the line to rebuke tyrants. But it was unlikely that someone with such skill would come into a pirate group.

Normally, they would have said sweet things to his ears, but they couldn’t even find the right words now.

‘Damn it, if this continues, we will all be in danger.’

‘Dying at the hands of the Black Dragon King or dying at the hands of Shaolin are the same!’

“Are those proud pirates of the Yangtze River just planning to run away without even fighting? You cowards!”

Anger radiated from the eyes of the Black Dragon King.

“It is better to fight and die proudly than to run away and save your life! That is what those who bear the name of the Yangtze River 18 Families must do!”

Those who were lamenting sighed without being noticed.

‘Damn it.’

They all knew. The truth was that all the words the Black Dragon King was saying now were nothing more than bravado.

If the Black Dragon King had really been reckless enough to stand against the Justice faction sects without care and save his pride, he would have already turned into a cold corpse at the bottom of the Yangtze River even before he became the Black Dragon King.

In the world of the Evil Faction, surviving might be possible with strength alone, but in this place where all kinds of plans ran rampant, one needed to use their brains to rise to the top. So, in the end, it meant that the Black Dragon King was by no means a stupid man.

Nevertheless, the reason he continued to speak such nonsense was to avoid losing pride by making excuses.

At least he tried to fight back. But he had no choice but to look at his subordinates, blocking him with tears and retreat. And to leave with those words.

This obvious play wouldn’t end until most of them were beaten to death. Even though they knew that, they all played along.

“Who will it be? The one who says we should run? I will make it clear to you that the only way to escape from this river is to become a corpse! I will be at the forefront. Still, is there anyone who wants to talk about retreat?”

It was a situation where all they could do was sigh.

Even while this was happening, the pirates were being attacked. If they were aiming for a fight, they had to at least let one pirate troop survive.

If they didn’t, as soon as the Justice Faction left the Yangtze River, the Evil Faction would come behind their backs to feed on them.

It was just as they were about to open their mouths to play along.

“Who are you?”

“How dare you involve yourself here?”

There was a loud commotion at the entrance of the pirates. Those who were bowing down looked back in shock.

‘Justice Faction people?’

‘They came this far already?’

Fortunately, it wasn’t them. No matter how they looked at the entrance, it wasn’t Justice faction people.

The cold-looking man in the lead had his lips twitching as he said.

“There is no such thing as threatening a messenger. You couldn’t possibly be ignorant of such things, right?”

“You....”

The Black Dragon King, who had been watching quietly, frowned and asked loudly.

“What is it?”

Then, one of the subordinates approached him and said.

“... he says he wants to see the Black Dragon King.”

“Hmm? Who are they?”

“That....”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes cooled when he heard who they were.

“Let them in.”

“Yes!”

As soon as the pirates cleared, 5-6 envoys confidently walked inside. Then, they came up to the Black Dragon King, and all got down on one knee.

“I greet the Black Dragon King.”

“So polite... I see a precious sight.”

The Black Dragon King spoke with a sneer.

“I guess you thought about coming all the way here. Let us see what you wanted so badly to risk your life.”

The man kneeling at the front took out a sealed letter from his sleeve and held it out.

“This is the letter the clan leader told me to deliver.”

As the Black Dragon King just looked at the scene in silence, one of those who was down lamenting brought the letter and handed it to him.

“Hmmm.”

The Black Dragon King broke the seal on the letter and began to read it.

It didn’t seem to be a long letter, but the Black Dragon King’s expression changed several times in that short time.

“....”

The Black Dragon King finally opened his eyes and frowned. At the same time, the letter in his hand began to burn brightly.

“Do you know what is written here?”

“I do not.”

“Then do you know that the contents of the letter cannot guarantee your lives?”

“I do.”

“Then what courage do you have to come to me with such a letter? Fucking pigs!”

The Black Dragon King’s killing intent exploded.

Due to the enormous amount of killing intent, the bodies of those who brought the letter were drenched in cold sweat.

“Speak. Is delivering this letter more important than your lives?”

“That... I don’t know.”

“Then why did you come?”

The one in the lead spoke, gritting through his teeth.

“...If the clan leader commands me, I will go, even if it is to hell. So what is there to be afraid of?”

At that moment, the killing intent falling upon them vanished.

“... Haha.”

The Black Dragon King stood up and smiled, looking to the far south.

“Jang Ilso. Jang Ilso....”

His lips twisted.

“That brat in Guangxi has surely grown a lot. How dare he call me?”

“Black Dragon King?”

Black Dragon King looked back at the subordinates who were watching without understanding what was happening and scolded them.

“Dok Mang!”

“Yes! Black Dragon King!”

“Tell each of them that they will avoid fighting for a while and live for themselves.”

“T-that means...?”

“Look elsewhere. What did that sly bastard prepare for us?”

Black Dragon King walked without hesitation, waving the black cloak.

“Kyaa! It tastes good!”

A statement that came from the throat.

Even before the sun had set over Mount Seosan, the people gathered at its base were drinking heavily.

“I am really offended by those disgusting pirates!”

“That is right! Damn it, they are so stubborn they can’t see where they are going! Those who cannot even use their strength in front of Shaolin or Wudang!”

“That is why they are the Evil Faction!”

The drunken people shared their thoughts and a glass of joy.

Just because the toll that the pirates made them pay disappeared, it didn’t mean life was in bloom right away. However, the joy of being able to at least keep all the money they earned was a relief compared to meeting a knife-wielding robber on the vast river.

“Tsk. To put it conversely, doesn’t it mean that we haven’t done something that should have been done just once?”

“Yah! They say that if you save a drowning person, they give you a bundle of things. That is exactly what you are saying. These are all normal people! The work should be done now, too, so why look here for something?”

“Right! That is right!”

It was true that at first, there were people who looked at the Nine Great Sects and Namgung family who arrived at the Yangtze River, all belatedly chasing after Mount Hua, with suspicious eyes, but now it had all vanished.

Whatever the reason, whether they came early or late, wouldn’t it be good if the pirates were cleaned out?

“Well, I hope they drive out all the pirates like this.”

“Don’t expect too much. How can they drive out so many of them? That’s something even the officials cannot do.”

“The officials cannot? They aren’t doing it! Aren’t they just sitting by and watching us get attacked because they cannot use a ship? If a high-ranking official gets robbed by the Evil Faction, they will run to take down the pirates, right?”

“Well, that is too much. I wonder if they continue to reside in the Yangtze River.”

“That, too, remains to be seen. I heard that some of the Mount Hua sect is residing here?”

“Uh? What does that mean?”

“I just heard that the Mount Hua people are sailing ships around the place where they cleared out the pirates. A ship which goes across the river regularly.”

“Isn’t that a common occurrence until now?”

“I don’t know. Wasn’t it a daily occurrence for ships that operated so regularly to get robbed? Even the ships which come and go irregularly can easily be robbed, so a ship which departs with a set time and route is no different from a well-done meal.”

“Right.”

“But what kind of courage would the pirates have to mess with Mount Hua? It might be a long way from Guxiang, but now we don’t have to worry about being taken down by pirates when heading up the River!”

“R-right.”

“And in order to do that, Mount Hua people will probably stay in their positions for some time. Then maybe we won’t have to care about the pirates around here.”

“If only it were so!”

One by one, people began to grow hopeful.

If they no longer have to worry about being attacked by pirates, they wouldn’t just be able to make a little more money right away. As more money is made, more money can be spent, and when more money is spent, offsetting becomes active.

People shared a drink cheerfully, dreaming of a day when the Yangtze River would be filled with people and luggage.

But in such places, there was bound to be someone who blew out the candle.

“I don’t know. I wonder if it will turn out so well.”

“... what are you even saying?”

“If the pirates vanished like that, would there have been any cases of new pirates in the Yangtze River?”

“You, again....”

“The 18 Families. They are one of the Shenzhou Five clans. They will not simply suffer like that. And maybe this is a bad thing for us.”

“Why? Why is it bad news if that happens?”

“Isn’t the reason we’ve been able to travel along the water by paying tolls to them because they wanted money from us?”

“We know that.”

“But once something like this happens, those people can’t think of spending their lives plundering the Yangtze River. So, wouldn’t they just try to snatch the money in front of them before the subjugation comes again?”

“... that...”

The person who was speaking shook his head.

“If you touch a snake without safety, it will turn to poison for the hand. If they get upset and retreat, the anger of pirates will come for us. And will those noble bastards really protect the Yangtze till the end? Are they going to risk their lives protecting the pirates?”

“....”

Everyone closed their mouths.

They wanted to deny it, but there was definitely some truth to it.

“I don’t know if that will be the case. But I hope not.”

“Pathetic.”

“N-Now! Stop the gloom and drink! Drink!”

As the air cooled down, people became excited and raised their voices to drink again. However, the anxiety that had sunk in remained curled up in their hearts until the drinking party ended.

“Did you say the base was empty?”

“Yes!”

Beop Gye sighed.

“Tracking? Is it possible to track them?”

“It isn’t easy. Rather than fleeing to land like bandits, they took a ship and moved to the middle of the Yangtze....”

“...”

“We need ships to attack those in the Yangtze River. But fighting on them...”

“Must be difficult.”

“I guess so.”

“Right. There’s nothing you can do about it. First, look at the empty base and inspect it.”

“Yes, elder.”

Beop Gye’s eyes sank.

He was in no position to show it, so he pretended to be calm on the outside, but inside he was quite anxious.

‘This is bad.’

If the pirates escaped by ship again, Shaolin would be unable to pursue them. As he said a moment ago, engaging in war with pirates on the water was like inviting disaster onto themselves.

Of course, if the goal was to destroy them, even if it meant taking some damage, he would have gone ahead. But this fight wasn’t a fierce one where the pirates and Shaolin battle for their fate. Wasn’t the goal to achieve more results with less damage than other sects who came to the Yangtze River?

‘There is a higher chance that we stall and waste time.’

The Yangtze River was wide.

Even if they didn’t run away to the middle of the river, it would still be a tough task to sweep through the wide and long Yangtze River and eliminate the pirates. It would be impossible, no matter how great Shaolin, Wudang, Qingcheng, and Namgung that were all gathered at the Yangtze were.

Beop Gye looked at the Yangtze River, lost in thought.

‘Right now, we are attacking with one mind and one goal, but if even one of the four sects decides to withdraw...’

Then, the other sects would start to feel irritated.

‘No, that is still a long way off.’

For now, it was right to concentrate on the task at hand.

But just one thing.

‘Oddly, there is little resistance.’

Anyway, the opponent was the Yangtze River’s 18 Families. At this point, even the bandits would have moved...

‘I guess I am just aging. Amitabha.’

Beop Gye shook his head.

It was a movement as if he was trying to shake off the doubts filling his mind.

Hangzhou.

Above the sky was Heaven, and below it were Suzhou and Hangzhou.

Hangzhou, located by the West Lake, was one of the most beautiful cities in the entire Central Plains. Its outstanding scenery has inspired countless poets since ancient times.

But Hangzhou wasn’t just famous for its beautiful scenery.

A place where one could experience all the joys, luxuries, and pleasures in the world. The city best described as a night city was Hangzhou.

The time was three in the morning. Although not a single star could be found in the cloudy sky, the night in Hangzhou was brighter than the day. Lanterns lit up here and there, making the streets dazzlingly bright.

“Now then! We have amazing drinks and even more amazing snacks!”

“The best places on the top floor are empty! It is the perfect place for heroes!”

“It’s full of rare food brought in from Xi’an!”

The dazzling night streets were filled with the voices of people walking around and of those soliciting customers.

And among the night scenes of Hangzhou, there was a most notable place.

The tallest 10-story pavilion in Hangzhou, the Musk Incense Tower.

There, at the top floor window, a man was drinking alone. It was a strange sight.

The price of a place on the top floor of the tower was enough to cover the living expenses of an average family for months. If they drank alcohol, they would have to pay 10 times more than on the lower floor.

And yet, there was an empty place on the top floor of Musk Incense Tower.

This place, which overlooked the streets of Hangzhou and the West Lake through the open window, was the symbol of Hangzhou. It wasn’t just because the entertainers of Hangzhou were there, but also because the children of families who came to Hangzhou from the rumors somehow tried to leave behind memories of having visited the top floor of the tower.

But today, there was no one up there.

No, there was just one person seated there on the spacious floor. If someone who didn’t know saw that, they wouldn’t believe their eyes.

But if someone who knew saw the scene, they would nod like it was natural, and if someone who knew better saw this, they would immediately turn and run.

This was because the man enjoying the best seat there was none other than Jang Ilso, the Hegemony Lord.

“Hmm.”

He looked at the yellow alcohol in the glass and mumbled.

“Shaoxing wine.”

There were countless varieties of alcohol sold under the name Shaoxing all over the world.

But the true Shaoxing wine was made from the waters of Lake Gan in Zhejiang. In other words, one could only truly taste it if they went to that specific place.

Moreover, the alcohol that the Hegemony Lord was currently tasting, considered premium in Shaoxing, was of top quality and sold exclusively in the restaurant.

“Intoxicated by scent....”

The Hegemony Lord smiled and took a sip.

As expected.

The scent was indescribably deep and unique. The moment he sipped it, he felt as if his entire body was drenched in the aroma.

“How delightful.”

Of course, the alcohol of Guangxi, where his base was, and the alcohol of Guizhou were not inferior to the alcohol in Zhejiang.

But even if the alcohols were similar, the scent and feel were different. It was impossible to rank which one was the best.

The world was vast and diverse. Countless alcohols were made everywhere, each with its own unique characteristics.

An ordinary person would die without tasting even one-tenth of the alcohol produced in all parts of the world.

“Same here.”

Savoring Shaoxing wine wasn’t a tough task.

However, the true essence of local famous alcohol could only be understood when one went to that specific area and tasted it.

Could it be that the alcohol they drink in Guangxi and the alcohol they drink in Hangzhou are the same?

When one thinks about it that way, it feels boring, like being trapped. Then how many lands in this wide world could this person step on?

The Hegemony Lord gulped down another glass of the alcohol he loved and lightly rubbed his lips with the tip of his index finger.

‘But there isn’t much left.’

He would put the world under his feet.

He would have more land under his feet than anyone else and drink more alcohol than anyone else.

“This is Jang Ilso.”

At that moment, he began to hear footsteps from downstairs.

“Hmm.”

He turned his gaze to the stairs. Soon, a giant man, completely covered in black clothing, appeared.

“You little brat... How dare you ask me to come?”

“Welcome, Black Dragon King.”

Jang Il-so flashed a bright, triumphant smile.

Thud!

As the Black Dragon King climbed the last of the stairs, he glared at Jang Ilso with the intensity of an angry tiger.

“You little brat... look at this. I cannot believe I came all the way to Hangzhou to have fun.”

If it were an ordinary person, just looking into those eyes would make their heart stop. But the opponent here was Jang Ilso.

Drip.

Even after seeing the harsh gaze, he calmly filled the glass and smiled.

“Please sit down.”

“...”

“Wasn’t the reason you came this far to have a conversation?”

“Maybe I came to slit your throat?”

“Hahaha!”

Jang Ilso smiled broadly as he faced the Black Dragon King and slowly rubbed his neck. The jewelry hanging on his wrist jingled lightly.

“I never thought my neck would be worth this much. The Black Dragon King of the world personally coming for it. Seems like I, Jang Ilso, didn’t live in vain.”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes narrowed slightly at the sight of Jang Ilso speaking softly.

‘Fucking snake.’

Living as part of the Evil Faction meant dealing with all the situations of the world. That was because, unlike the Justice Faction, which tried to maintain at least a justifiable cause on the outside, the Evil Faction didn’t choose or care about the method to defeat an opponent.

Those who were the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans were people who had risen to their positions by breaking using their arms and legs through a world no ordinary person could imagine.

But...

The Black Dragon King’s eyes were cold as he looked at Jang Ilso.

This man was of a different class from those people.

The 18 River Families along the Yangtze River, the 72 Bandits of the Green Forest, and the Lower District Sect.

All of these sects had their own history. The leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans of the time were those who inherited or usurped power passed down from their predecessors. Of course, they would never say that the process was easy, but it was definitely different from climbing up from the bottom.

But that wasn’t Jang Ilso.

This man literally climbed up from the bottom, recruited 100,000 followers, and even raised the level of the Shenzhou Five Clans.

That was why, even though Jang Ilso was the youngest of the five leaders and lacked experience, no one could laugh at him or say anything.

He felt his stomach twist as he thought about what was being hidden behind the bright and smooth smiles.

“Now that I know your head is valuable, I wouldn’t feel particularly unfair if it were cut.”

“It isn’t that difficult to give my neck.”

“...”

Jang Ilso smiled and continued speaking.

“But how do you plan to stop those Justice Faction bastards who are destroying the Yangtze River by cutting off my head?”

“... you!”

Killing intent rose in the Black Dragon King’s eyes.

“True.”

He quickly turned his gaze to the voice that came from right next to him.

‘Just when...?’

A middle-aged man with an ordinary look had shown himself suddenly.

Although his goatee looked quite unique, it wasn’t particularly noticeable. A middle-aged man with the utmost ordinary appearance, someone you would pass by 10 times a day but never care about.

“Anyone can raise their voice. But solving the problem is a different matter.”

“... Thousand Faced Scholar.”

The Black Dragon King bit his lip.

No one knew this man’s real name.

No one even knew what the real face of this person was.

The face they were seeing now was one created by the man. If he put his mind to it, he could disguise himself as anything and even change his voice and body shape to whatever he wanted.

The reason was simple.

It was because this man, the Thousand Faced Scholar, was the leader of the Lower District sect.

“Can I get you a drink?”

Jang Ilso silently grabbed the empty glass next to him and placed it in front of him.

Swish.

He picked up the bottle and filled the glass, then lightly pushed it toward the Thousand-Faced Scholar.

Even though it moved through the air, not a single drop of alcohol spilled because of how he pushed it.

The Thousand-Faced Scholar, who received the cup, drank it down without delay and placed it back on the table with a loud sound.

“Good. Is it an alcohol of your choice?”

The Black Dragon King sneered softly,

“Normally, you hold your breath and pretend to be cautious, like a rat in a cat’s den, but you gulp down alcohol offered by others?”

“If it was a cup handed by the Black Dragon King, I might have thrown it.”

“... What?”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar smiled to make himself seem approachable.

“But you can trust the alcohol given by the Hegemony Lord. At least he isn’t someone who covets something small and loses big in return.”

“Thank you for the high regard.”

“It was nothing.”

“You people...”

The Black Dragon King, filled with anger, trembled from his beard to his gritted teeth.

The Thousand-Faced Scholar looked at him, still smiling.

“The Black Dragon King needs to tone down his anger a bit. Isn’t the person in the most urgent need of help the Black Dragon King?”

“Are you looking down at me now?”

“As if I would.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar shook his head.

At the same time, his expression changed. A face that looked so soft and generous a moment ago now had eyes sharpened like blades, focused on the Black Dragon King.

“It means that the situation isn’t that good. If one is going to act recklessly, don’t get in my way and just move out.”

“You fucking rat!”

At the moment when the Black Dragon King was about to burst into anger, the Thousand Faced Scholar spoke to Jang Ilso.

“The Green Forest King didn’t come... right?”

“He is useless.”

“Right.”

Thousand-Faced Scholar nodded as if he understood.

He was the leader of the Lower District Clan, and it was a place that divided the world’s information with the Beggars Union. There was no way someone like him didn’t know that the Green Forest had sided with the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Still, I thought it would be as such, but I didn’t think he would move like a dog on a leash. Isn’t it because the Hegemony Lord harassed him too much?”

This was a reference to the war between the Ten Thousand People Clan and Green Forest in the past.

“If that’s all he can do, it cannot be helped.”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“And it isn’t necessarily my fault. If the Thousand-Faced Scholar hadn’t pointed the dagger at my back, I wouldn’t have done that either.”

“If that is the case, we would have been the next ones. Or maybe the bandits would have moved to the South.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar chuckled.

The Evil Faction was that kind of place.

A free-for-all fight.

Yesterday’s enemy became today’s friend, and yesterday’s friend turned into today’s enemy.

To begin with, there were no friends in the Evil Faction. Even when they joined forces and fought, there was only one enemy and another enemy. If there was someone going to war, of course, one should target them. It wasn’t cowardice to aim for weakness; it was complacency not to do so.

Therefore, the five clans only fought at times while protecting their land. If you waged a war properly, someone would definitely come after you.

“Then where else?”

“I sent the letter.”

“Hmm.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar shook his head.

“I guess there is no point in waiting any longer. So, why did the Hegemony Lord call us?”

The Black Dragon King changed his look.

“I have never said I would listen.”

“Then go, Black Dragon King. We won’t stop you.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar clicked his tongue.

“There is no point in fighting for dominance here. Everyone’s feet are on fire right now. No, maybe it’s as if a dagger is already at their throats. And...”

The eyes of the Thousand Face Scholar looked at the Black Dragon King like daggers.

“The pirates know that better than anyone else.”

“...”

The Black Dragon King bit his lip.

“So? Are you saying those smart guys are going to get together and play?”

“Then it would be an invaluable position for us.”

The Black Dragon King turned as he heard this unfamiliar voice.

A person came walking up slowly.

There was no expression on the face that was soon revealed.

He couldn’t feel any emotion at all, and the skin was pale, not just bloodless. But rather, that placed pressure on them.

The man in the blue robe spoke in a cold voice as he reached the top floor.

“What’s important is what benefit you get from the position. If you aren’t going to play, it is meaningless.”

“... damn bastard.”

The Black Dragon King criticized, but the Thousand Face Scholar just nodded.

Of course, the benefits he thought of and the benefits the other thought of would be different. Because the one who divided everything in the world into benefits and losses had come.

Not everyone in the Evil Faction was faithful to their desires.

Sometimes they endured for something bigger, and sometimes they hid and disguised themselves to set a perfect trap.

But this man was different. In his mind, there was only gain.

Of course, countless people in the world were insane for money. Naturally, merchants were obsessed with money. Those people were willing to sell their souls for dazzling gold and were willing to go a thousand miles to get it done. Therefore, merchants were often called Money Ghosts.

But those who truly knew the inner thoughts of the world did not use the word ‘Ghost’ to refer to such people. Because there were people who were really insane for money.

Those who would do anything for money.

Those who followed the principles of the world risked their lives for gold, but those who mocked these principles risked their lives for real gold.

Gold was a luxury item.

The gold that truly controlled people wasn’t gold, but salt.

Therefore, in any country, it was common for merchants not to trade in salt. Because touching salt was considered treason.

Black ghosts risked all danger to touch that salt.

The Black Ghost Fort.

And this person was none other than the Desolate Gold Elder, Gong Ya-Wol.

Ten Thousand People Clan, Lower District Clan, Yangtze River 18 Families, and Black Ghost Fort.

The leaders of four of the five clans had gathered on the top floor of the tower in Hangzhou.

If anyone who knew of Kangho even slightly had seen this scene, they wouldn’t have been able to breathe.

“Money ghost...”

When the Black Dragon King was about to say something, the man briefly waved his hand.

“Stop talking nonsense, Black Dragon King. I am a busy person. Let’s hear the business first.”

“...”

“Hegemony Lord. Tell us why you called us here. If you speak nonsense, you will have to pay a heavy price. The sin of wasting my time is the greatest.”

The Hegemony Lord responded with an odd smile.

“If there is one thing that the elder lack, it is style and leisure.”

“Anything that doesn’t make money is of no use to me.”

“Please sit down.”

The elder glanced at Jang Ilso but didn’t object. He just sat across silently.

Thousand Face Scholar also hurried over and sat down in the place provided. The Black Dragon King, who was standing till the end, stomped over with an unhappy look and roughly pulled out the chair. He then growled before sitting down.

“I won’t leave you alone if you speak nonsense, Jang Ilso.”

“Nonsense...”

Jang Ilso smiled, lightly stroking the glass in front of him with a gentle hand.

“Face is important.”

His gaze landed on the Black Dragon King.

“Surely money is precious.”

This time, he looked at the elder.

“It is no exaggeration to say that there is nothing more important than information.”

Finally, Jang Ilso, who gave the Thousand Face Scholar a look, filled the glass.

Pour.

The sound of pouring alcohol spread around the quiet floor.

“But all of that....”

Jang Ilso, holding the glass, twisted his lips into a smile. It was a clear look of ridicule, just like before. Jang Ilso’s eyes, which drew a line, shone in the pale moonlight.

“Isn’t it a story about when the throat is being targeted?”

The atmosphere on the top floor suddenly turned cold as ice.

“This....”

Even so, the Black Dragon King, who was caught in the crosshairs of a deteriorating situation, was furious. Just as he was about to cause a scene, the Thousand Face Scholar entered with a calm voice.

“The head falls...”

He grinned.

“You’re saying that the Hegemony Lord is going to cut our heads?”

Jang Ilso responded with a smile.

“You’re saying such scary things. Is that even possible?”

The gentle eyes of the Thousand Face Scholar turned to Jang Ilso.

“Then, who is going to cut our throats?”

“You’re probably not asking because you don’t know. If you’re here without understanding that, there’s no reason for me to talk to you.”

“Hahaha....”

Although the laughter continued, killing intent spread from the Thousand Face Scholar’s eyes.

His face, which was nice and pleasant, was actually the scariest and most cruel of the Five Clan leaders. Due to his nature of handling information, he didn’t consider torturing and killing people to be sins.

“You’re being ridiculous, Hegemony Lord.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso silently poured another drink into his glass.

The moment the Thousand Face Scholar’s eyes narrowed at the act, which could be felt as disregard, Jang Ilso opened his mouth.

“First, the Yangtze River 18 Families.”

“....”

“Maybe the... Black Ghost Fort next.”

The Desolate Gold Elder’s eyes twitched next.

It wasn’t common for him, who was always emotionless, to show such emotion. That brief movement indicated how uncomfortable he felt.

“After that, the Ten Thousand People Clan. And the last would be the Lower District Sect.”

“...”

“They will be hunted one after another, and everyone will lose their heads. Or one can give up everything and become a prisoner.”

Thud!

The Black Dragon King struck the table.

“Who would dare do that?”

“The Nine Great Sects One Union.”

In response to the answer, which came out without hesitation, the Black Dragon King closed his mouth as if at a loss for words.

“...”

“And... maybe even the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Crack.

The Black Dragon King, whose face was contorted like an evil beast, glared at Jang Ilso and said,

“Let us say that is the case with the Nine Great Sects. But does that mean we need to discuss such nonsense?”

“Such nonsense?”

Jang Ilso smiled and looked straight at the Black Dragon King.

“Is there anyone who guarantees that any of these can take on the Heavenly Friends Alliance alone?”

“...”

“Mount Hua, the Sichuan Tang family, Nanman Beast Palace, and the North Sea Ice Palace... Moreover, who could deal with the Heavenly Friends Alliance, which the Green Forest has joined? The Yangtze River 18 families?”

“You...”

“Times have changed, Black Dragon King. The names of the Yangtze River 18 Families cause no fear in the world.”

“Hmm.”

The Desolate Gold Elder shook his head.

“It’s definitely impossible to handle the Heavenly Friends Alliance alone. However, it is an ideal theory that the Alliance can gather such strength. First, they are very far from one another and are loosely connected. It doesn’t happen that all of the Alliance’s strength is concentrated at once.”

“You are right. But...”

The corners of Jang Ilso’s mouth rose.

“The mere existence of the Heavenly Friends Alliance is a problem.”

“...”

“As you may know, if it wasn’t for the Heavenly Friends Alliance, Shaolin and Wudang would have never moved.”

The Desolate Gold Elder shook his head, unable to deny it.

“Right. There is nothing to be gained.”

Subjugating the Yangtze River was definitely something that would bring fame and favor to them.

Places like Shaolin, Wudang, and the Namgung family didn’t need more fame than they already had. Such recognition only mattered to those who hadn’t yet achieved it. There was no reason for them to seek new fame and favor from the public, as their reputations were already exceedingly high.

“But if they have the Heavenly Friends Alliance in there, things are different.”

It was as if a substitute had been created.

Without the Heavenly Friends Alliance, no matter how dirty and unpleasant things got, everyone ultimately had no choice but to cling to the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families. They were the only ones capable of handling the Evil Faction and stopping them.

But now, there was no need to be obsessed with them anymore. The Heavenly Friends Alliance had already demonstrated sufficient strength and proactivity.

“Murim always repeats the same cycle. When two strong factions compete for power, direct war doesn’t happen. For example, Wudang and Shaolin have never fought to the point of killing each other. Instead....”

Jang Ilso revealed it.

“They look for a game.”

“...”

“There are two ways to show that they are the stronger tiger. One is to fight and win, and the second is... to prove it by biting and killing more wolves.”

“... and we are the wolves.”

“Yes.”

Jang Ilso pushed the empty drinking glasses one by one.

“First, the Yangtze River pirates.”

Jang Ilso’s index finger gently pressed down on the glass in front of him.

Jang!

The glass cracked, making a noise like a sharp scream.

“Next?”

“....”

“There is no point in betting on whose head will be cut off first. If one is going to bet, something else is more fun to bet on. Which sect will take my head? Is it Wudang, Shaolin, Mount Hua, or the Tang family? Hehehe.”

Jang Ilso chuckled and slowly looked at them.

“I will bet on Shaolin. Yours?”

“...”

No one could bear to open their mouths at that.

This was because they knew that Jang Ilso wasn’t bluffing.

Not only the Black Dragon King, who was directly under attack but also those who were watching the entire process felt an extreme sense of crisis.

“So?”

The Desolate Gold Elder cut him off.

“Get rid of useless emotions. Because it is a waste of time. What can we do?”

“Join forces.”

“With this trash?”

The faces of the Black Dragon King and the Thousand Face Scholar became distorted when hearing the word “trash.”

“You better keep your mouth shut, elder. Unless you want to end up dead with your tongue cut out.”

“Or it would be better to cut off his limbs and make him dog food.”

Bloody voices filled with killing intent echoed, but the elder spoke without blinking.

“Anyone can think of a union. But no one can enforce it. There is no way we trust each other.”

“... that is right.”

“Just seeing your face makes my stomach turn.”

The Evil Faction hated the Justice Faction.

But more than that, they hated the Evil Faction too. This was because the people who fought, bit and raised swords much more than the Justice Faction were others from the same faction.

“It wouldn’t be a bad idea to take this chance to rip off all the heads and eat them.”

“That is a good opinion.”

The killing intent of the three people filled the room. It was a grueling intent that would not just cause trouble for normal people but even warriors.

At that moment.

“Hahahahahaha!”

Jang Ilso burst into laughter as the pavilion’s heavy atmosphere lifted.

“Ahahahahaah!”

The three people’s fierceness disappeared in an instant as he hit the table and shed tears laughing. Everyone’s eyes moved to Jang Ilso.

“... what is so funny to you?”

When the question came up, Jang Ilso raised his hand and gently wiped away the tears from the corners of his eyes.

“Ah... ah. I am sorry. Ugh... it is too funny to hold back.”

“What is so funny?”

“Wouldn’t it be weird if it wasn’t funny?”

Jang Ilso said with a smile still on his face.

“How can I not laugh at the bug-like beings who growl and fight with one another, not even realizing the mess they have stepped into? Such pathetic ones are the leaders of the Shenzhou Five clans? Hahaha!”

“...what did you say just now?”

Jang Ilso’s red lips twisted.

“Why? Did I say something wrong?”

“...”

“Even animals, when their lives are in danger, unite regardless of their natural enemies. But do people bring personal grudges into matters where their lives are at stake?”

“...”

Even the tone of voice he held till then had vanished, and Jang Ilso bared his white teeth.

“Resentment, hatred, and all the trivial emotions they enjoy exist because of that neck attached to the body. Once you die, the grudges have no meaning. The most important thing for humans isn’t resentment, respect, or use.”

No one could refute those words and just continued to look at him.

“Survival.”

“...”

“There is life to live. Nothing means anything if you don’t survive. I will do anything to live, even if it means rolling in shit, even if it means licking the feet of the enemy!”

These weren’t words that should come out of the mouth of someone with a high status as a clan leader of the Five Clans.

But maybe that was why his sincerity was greatly felt.

“Compared to that, protecting the back of someone who was pointing a knife at you until yesterday is nothing special.”

Jang Ilso, who poured the drink from a new bottle, looked at everyone with a strange smile and softly asked,

“Am I wrong?”

“Hmm.”

A groaning sound escaped from the mouths of the Black Dragon King and the Thousand Face Scholar.

They definitely hated the people in front of them more than the Justice Faction. It was no exaggeration to say that their history was a history of killing each other.

But no matter what, they were the Evil Faction. They could not join hands with the Justice Faction. The only way to match the Justice Faction was to move in earnest with one another.

“For now...”

The Desolate Gold Elder opened his mouth.

“If this is something that only ends temporarily, isn’t it all just meaningless discussion? It will end with damage to the pirates.”

“It wouldn’t be that simple.”

Jang Ilso spoke clearly.

“If the Heavenly Friends Alliance doesn’t stop, the Nine Great Sects won’t stop either. They might be stronger, but the initiative is already in the hands of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“...”

“And Mount Hua is the head of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, which won’t stop. They are greedy. As much as me.”

Jang Ilso’s smile grew bigger. There was a glimpse of madness in his eyes.

“One way or another, they will push to the south of the Yangtze River. And we cannot just sit and relax until then, right?”

“For the sake of our pride?”

“That is true. But precisely, it might be because we cannot stand by and watch as the Heavenly Friends Alliance gradually absorbs the land south of the Yangtze into their own.”

The Desolate Gold Elder nodded as if he was thinking.

“We have to unite to fight against such forces, the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families.”

“Right. If not, you have to choose one of the two.”

Jang Ilso, who suddenly lost his smile, spoke quietly as if growling.

“Either live like a dog or die like a wolf.”

“...”

“...”

“But what I want is to live as a wolf. The reason I can treat others as prey is when I am confident that I can hunt them down without taking damage. If one is prepared to get their arms and organs ripped out as a reward for hunting, then one shouldn’t dare call it hunting.”

Jang Ilso said, holding his face with one hand.

“Just attach any grandiose word you want.”

The sight of his eyes shining between his fingers was terrifying.

“No matter what the cause, the goal is survival. Surviving. If one misses the right time, even if they come together, they get bitten. I will definitely live. I will survive and enjoy everything I gain without missing a single thing.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar, who had been listening quietly, put one hand on his forehead and laughed.

“So upfront and low quality that I cannot say anything about it.”

“...”

“But...”

His eyes shone blue.

“There is nothing wrong with it. I am not a person who is anxious to die, either. If that is the only way to live, then that is what we should do. I will agree with the Hegemony Lord.”

Jang Ilso gently smiled.

“From this moment on, there are no Shenzhou Clans.”

The loud declaration came.

“What remains are just four clans, and the union, where the Shenzhou vanishes, only the Four Precepts Alliance remains!”

A name that would take the world by storm.

This was the moment when the existence of the Four Precepts Alliance first came from Jang Ilso’s mouth.

“It is called the Four Precepts Alliance...”

The Desolate Gold Elder mumbled quietly with a cracked tone.

This wasn’t normal.

Especially when considering the impact the creation of this Alliance would have on the Central Plains.

‘It is dangerous.’

Of course, as Jang Ilso said, a union of this kind might be needed to fight against the Justice Faction, which was running rampant. But, if they thought about it, there was a high chance that the creation of this new Alliance would instead create a steadfast enemy for them.

Kukuku.

At that moment, Jang Ilso scratched the table with his fingertips. The shape of the Central Plains was drawn on the table along the tip of his finger.

“This is the current strength of the world. First, the influence of the Nine Great Sects.”

The Nine Great Sects are scattered all over the world.

Considering the Southern Island sect was on an island in the South Sea, Qingcheng in Yunnan, and Kunlun at the end of Cheon province, it might be unreasonable to limit the sphere of influence of the Nine Great Sects.

But they couldn’t say it was completely wrong.

In the end, the center of the Nine Great Sects was Shaolin and Wudang. Since the two sects were centered around the Hunan region, the Northern Plains and the surrounding areas were guarded by Southern Edge, Kongtong, and the Beggars Union. The center of the Nine Great Sects was clearly Hunan and the Northern Plains.

“And the Heavenly Friends Alliance of the West.”

“Hmm.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar’s face flinched slightly.

Although he really didn’t want to admit it, he couldn’t deny that the Heavenly Friends Alliance was currently exerting enormous influence on the western side of the Central Plains.

‘It isn’t even funny.’

To the west of the Central Plains were the Kunlun Sect, Diancang, and Emei Sect, along with Qingcheng.

Of course, they were not gathered together like the Nine Great Sects in the center, nor were they sects that actively participated in the affairs of the Central Plains.

But even so, the Nine Great Sects were the Nine Great Sects. One couldn’t ignore the Heavenly Friends Alliance, which built a solid sphere of influence to keep those forces in check.

‘Southern Edge’s luck must be great. To begin with, Southern Edge is located west of Mount Hua. If Southern Edge had continued to be active, the Heavenly Friends Alliance wouldn’t have even started.’

Whether it was Mount Hua that caused Southern Edge to close its gates or whether Southern Edge had found a gap to withdraw, it was clear that their resourcefulness was beyond average.

This was clearly visible in the eyes of the Thousand Face Scholar.

From the North Sea Ice Palace in the north to the Nanman Beast Palace in the South, it was one long line.

‘If they can maintain that line until the end, instead of the Nine Great Sects, the Heavenly Friends Alliance will become the force that represents the world one day.’

But...

There were a lot of unsettling factors, obviously.

“And what is left?”

Tak!

Jang Ilso stretched out his hand and hit the table. The place his hand touched was none other than the provinces south of the river.

“The Southlands.”

“...”

The influence of the officials hadn’t reached it yet. Therefore, the sects directly South of the Yangtze River gathered to avoid the oppression of the Nine Great Sects and officials.

“The key is the Yangtze River! If the sects in the Southlands can work together to protect the Yangtze River, they will not be able to move south. Then, first, we can firmly obtain the land in the South.”

“Then it is no different than before?”

“The world has changed.”

Crack.

Jang Ilso grabbed the table. A handful of wood was torn by his hand.

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance brought together people who couldn’t relate to each other. And the existence of that Alliance made the Nine Great Sects, which were fighting one another, unite again. Now, the world of people sticking their necks up and protecting their own sects is done. What remains now is which of them will survive!”

“Hmm.”

“The world will be reorganized into three factions.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar laughed as if it were a joke.

“The world is divided into three already. Does that resonate well?”

“Shouldn’t it?”

“Hehehe.”

After a bizarre laugh, he nodded.

“Well, that’s good. Because I decided to agree with Hegemony Lord’s thoughts from the start. If one looks at the current situation, it is clear that if things continue like this, everyone will be ruined.”

When it came to handling information and analyzing the situation, the Lower District was second to none. The words that came from his mouth were surely powerful.

Even Black Dragon King seemed intrigued by it.

“Are you planning to make the Yangtze River the core?”

“I cannot say it is the core. But one could say that it is the most important place right now. If the Yangtze River is washed out, there will be no way to divide the Southlands and the Northern Plains. The key is to firmly instill the thought of both sides enduring danger when it comes to crossing the Yangtze River.”

“...you mean to rip off the arms, then rip off the stomach and eat the internal organs?”

The Black Dragon King pondered those words and smiled.

Actually, there was nothing bad about that. In order for the Nine Great Sects to target the Evil Faction in the Southlands, they needed to attack the pirates and open the way. If that happened, the first to fall would be the Yangtze.

In such a situation, why would they refuse to take the initiative and provide support?

“Kuahahahah! Now, this weird guy is finally saying something worth listening to. If so, I will also join the Four Percept Alliance!”

The eyes of the Black Dragon King shone.

“It would be better to abandon the idea of using the 18 River Families as a shield. If something like that happens, I will definitely cut off your heads.”

“Such a useless worry. Because we also need the Yangtze River to live.”

Jang Ilso lightly waved his white hand and clearly expressed his intention.

Tak. Tak. Tak.

The Desolate Gold elder, the last man left, was resting his chin on one hand and tapping the table with the other. He saw them mumbling, and it seemed like he was calculating the profits and losses.

Tak!

As if he had reached a conclusion, he frowned and tapped his finger on the table.

“It doesn’t make sense.”

“The reason?”

“It isn’t enough.”

He crossed his arms and explained.

“When hundreds of clans are scattered, the level of strength isn’t important because all kinds of circumstances come into play. But when those forces are merged into two, the only question that remains is who is stronger.”

“....”

“Even if the four sects in the Southlands unite and gulp up all smaller sects to merge, they will not have enough strength. If we aren’t careful, we could fail sooner because of the Alliance.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar frowned and refuted.

“If you take advantage of the ties between the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Nine Great Sects....”

“That sounds very much like a dream. The center of the Heavenly Friends Alliance is the Sichuan Tang family and Mount Hua. Justice faction. No matter how bad their ties are, if they are from the same faction, they can join hands at any time. Even if they don’t join, they won’t attack each other by fighting the Evil Faction.”

“....”

“If you don’t have the strength to single-handedly stop the Nine Great Sects, it is like jumping into the flame with firewood in hand. We won’t have any trace left. And this is impossible without the Green Forest. And haven’t they sided with the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

Jang Ilso nodded his head.

“Reasonable words.”

“Hmm?”

To Jang Ilso’s unexpectedly obedient attitude, the elder frowned.

“Is there a way to make up for the lack of strength? Even if we attract all sects in the Southlands, and not replace the Green Forest?”

“Yes.”

Jang Ilso shook his head and then lifted the glass to take a sip.

Tak.

And he put it down with a smile on his lips. His red lips were wet, and he looked more magical.

“If it is the Southlands, yet. But... there is no need to gather strength just in the Southlands.”

“...If not the Southlands?”

“Wouldn’t that be a sufficient answer?”

When Jang Ilso pointed to the back, everyone turned their heads with surprised faces.

‘Who?’

‘I felt nothing!’

In particular, the look of shock couldn’t be hidden on the faces of the Thousand-Faced Scholar and the Desolate Gold Elder. They never thought there could be someone who could approach them without being detected.

“...”

There was someone standing in the dark corner where the lamp light didn’t reach.

The moment they saw him, wrapped from head to toe in dark red, blood-red long cloths, everyone’s spines went cold, and their hair stood on end.

“...Who is that?”

“The Blood Palace Lord?”

“The B-Blood Palace?”

In the end, a cry of shock came from the Black Dragon King’s mouth.

The Blood Palace.

The most secret and mysterious of the Five Palaces. The place whose base wasn’t even known.

Power and stealth.

A place where those two incredible features combined. The Blood Palace Lord, who was said to be the strongest among the Five Palaces, had appeared here.

“The... Palace....”

A creepy voice, as if someone were scratching metal with a blade, came from the darkness.

“The Ten Thousand People... clan leader agreeing with... all....”

The Black Dragon King and the other two all looked at each other.

“...If the Blood Palace Lord is with us...”

“It is a different story.”

When it came to the Blood Palace, it wasn’t a place that was inferior to the Green Forest. No, if they looked at strength alone, they might be so strong as to be compared with the Green Forest.

Didn’t the force that emanated from the Blood Palace Lord prove that now?

The Desolate Gold Elder quickly flicked his head to think.

“There are already enough numbers. What is lacking is the number of skilled people. In that case, the Blood Palace is a much better option than Green Forest.”

The calculation was done.

He smiled.

“Enough. The Black Ghost Fort will share the goal with the Alliance.”

“The Yangtze River, too, will join with the Alliance.”

As everyone’s eyes turned to the Blood Palace Lord, he too nodded, as if there was no need to speak.

“The name is the Four Percept Alliance, and the clans are five...”

“Symbolism is important. The Blood Palace Lord doesn’t seem unhappy either.”

“More sects will gather under the Alliance. It is better if it would not be Four and Evil.”

Tak. Tak. Tak.

Jang Ilso wordlessly placed the five empty drinking glasses in front of them and began to fill them.

“That is a great decision.”

“But!”

At that moment, Desolate Gold Elder raised his hand.

“Before we share a glass, there are things we must make clear.”

“Please speak.”

“Who does the work?”

“...”

Everyone went silent without an answer.

“There is no point in maintaining this Alliance with this current system. I don’t know if the Justice factions can run like that, but we cannot. Clear coordination and a firm system are needed.”

“Hahahaha! That is right. But there is no need to worry about that. I will become the leader and guide you!”

“It is too much for you, Black Dragon King. Don’t step out of your place.”

“Thousand Faced Scholar! Do you wish to die right now?”

A loud voice erupted, but the Thousand-Faced Scholar ignored it and let out a crooked laugh.

“Information is power. I am suitable because I know the circumstances of all clans.”

“Even if you have power, you cannot do anything without money. The one who provides the money is the one who holds the power, right?”

“You people...”

It was as if three people exchanged cold glances with each other.

“You don’t have to worry about that.”

Jang Ilso poured the drink into the glass and shook his head lightly.

“Do you have different thoughts?”

“The only person who can become the leader of the Alliance is me, Jang Ilso.”

A flame burned brightly in the Black Dragon King’s eyes.

“This damned brat... in the end, it was a clever move.”

“Claims need evidence.”

“I also know what areas will definitely benefit me.”

Jang Ilso smiled and pushed a glass of alcohol in front of everyone, one by one.

“The Black Dragon King’s mastery is excellent, but the power of harmony is lacking. There will definitely be friction elsewhere. Moreover, the Black Dragon King cannot leave the Yangtze River. If one has a regiment on the front line, won’t everything end the moment the front line is pushed back?”

“... hmm.”

The Black Dragon King let out a sigh as he looked at the glass in front of him.

It was annoying, but it wasn’t wrong.

“Information is extremely important, but sometimes it can be harmful. No one wants to follow someone who knows their weaknesses and secrets.”

“... is it a fundamental problem? Tsk.”

The Thousand-Faced Scholar also accepted the drink held out in front of him. He, too, had no choice but to agree with it.

“No one could ignore the strength of money, but this place is Kangho. And being sensitive to profit means that the person involved has personal gain. Isn’t the leader of the Alliance a suitable position for someone who can sacrifice his or her own interests for the sake of everyone?”

“... that is the truth.”

The Desolate Gold Elder also nodded. When the interests of the Alliance and the interests of his clan conflict, he cannot choose the former.

Jang Ilso picked up one of the two remaining glasses of drink and threw it at the Blood Palace Lord.

“It goes without saying that there are other sects.”

Tak.

The Blood Palace Lord, who received the drink, nodded silently.

“Not just that...”

Jang Ilso’s eyes shone with madness. Everyone who saw those eyes couldn’t help but flinch.

There were people who all had negative views of the world, but there was something about Jang Ilso that made them sick of it. It was as if the initiative had been passed to him.

“Those who are confined to their own land and only look at the path ahead cannot become the alliance leader. The person who becomes the leader must be greedier than anyone else. Because of that, they must have greater greed than anyone.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes turned, and he grabbed the side of the Northern Plains on the map he had drawn before.

Crack.

He clenched it hard enough to tear off the entire part. The shattered wood turned into powder and flew.

“I don’t just want to occupy the Southlands and stay in that barren place, pretending to be king. I don’t have any thoughts about it. I want that place more than anyone else. Just looking at it makes me thirsty and hungry. It is a hunger that will not be relieved by a thousand glasses of alcohol or grains.”

“...”

“You share whatever you can get from the land. Neither people nor money nor land mean anything to me. There is just one thing I need: to subdue everything in the world in the name of Jang Ilso.”

Everyone held their breath.

Jang Ilso’s eyes were filled with madness, which proved that the words weren’t lies.

“Only he is worthy.”

“...”

“Follow me. Then I will guide you. Not to this smelly corner of the Southlands, but to the Northern Plains, the central region!”

“Hahahahah!”

The Thousand Face Scholar laughed loudly and shook his head.

“Really, I cannot stand it. This Lower District clan leader greets the Alliance Leader.”

As the Thousand Face Scholar said this, the Desolate Gold Elder also sighed and nodded.

“That would be the most profitable path. Desolate Gold Elder of the Black Ghost Fort greets you.”

“... damn it.”

The Black Demon King didn’t seem to like it until the end, but he couldn’t back out now.

“Do not expect politeness from me.”

“Of course.”

“... the Black Dragon King of the 18 River Families accepts Jang Ilso as the leader of the Alliance!”

“Drink.”

Jang Ilso raised his glass first, and everyone followed suit, lifting their glasses high.

“As of this time, we are brothers. Drink! A sword to those who betray!”

Bang!

The drinking glasses clashed roughly in the air.

Eventually, everyone emptied their glasses in one go.

The Four Precepts Alliance of the Evil Faction.

Finally, the Alliance that would turn the world upside down was born.

“Thank you, clan leader.”

“Hmm.”

Ho Ga-Myung smiled as he saw Jang Ilso lightly nodding his head.

“No, I guess I will have to call you Alliance leader now.”

“What are you doing with that face! Forget it.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand shyly, saying he hated grandiose titles, but Ho Ga-Myung still had a smile on his face.

“Please go up.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso walked leisurely and climbed into a four-horse carriage tied to four white horses. The carriage, decorated extravagantly as expected for him, was as luxurious on the inside as it was on the outside.

As Jang Ilso got into the carriage, Ho Ga-Myung looked around and followed him. As soon as the two got into the carriage, the horses started galloping away.

He opened his mouth with a flushed face.

“Fortunately, it doesn’t seem like there were any major problems.”

“We got lucky.”

“How can one be that lucky?”

Ho Ga-Myung knew just how much Jang Ilso worked to create this plan.

The effort put in over that long time had finally paid off.

“This is just one step.”

But Jang Ilso waved his hand covered in ornaments and said,

“On the outside, they lower their heads and pretend to agree, but there is no way those snakes will follow me that politely. On the inside, they are only planning to put me and the Ten Thousand People clan to the front to pursue their own interests.”

That’s what it meant to be an Alliance leader.

The position of the leader of an alliance, which had already been created, was a position where power and strength could be gained simultaneously, and it left a huge mark. On the other hand, if one was a first-time alliance leader, one would lose money alone.

In order to maintain the alliance, everyone wouldn’t bend their position during the inception. In the end, it was clear that everyone would have to bear the loss.

The fact that they stepped forward to become the Alliance leader over the others was a sign of shame. If they had supported each other, everyone would have waved their hands.

This position of union was made of twisted poisonous snakes. It was obvious that if there was a moment of chance, extremely poisonous teeth would bite into their body.

“But isn’t that also what the clan leader wanted?”

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, clan leader.”

“I told you before, right? If you don’t take risks, you won’t gain anything.”

Jang Ilso laughed as his mouth twisted.

“For those pigs who are full, just throw in the leftover rice to work. But a hungry wolf is never satisfied with the leftover things. Hunting involves risk at all times.”

Jang Ilso traced his lips with his index finger. His blood-red lips gave off this weird glow.

“If you want to gain something as important as life, it is natural for one to risk their life.”

“Fair point.”

Ho Ga-Myung looked at Jang Ilso with a newfound respect.

Wonderful.

Since the war against the Demonic Sect in the past, the Evil Faction never united to form one union. The Evil Faction was reborn again after 100 years. Jang Ilso and the Ten Thousand People clan took place at its center.

Jang Ilso was the youngest and least experienced compared to the other clan leaders. It wwas a natural thing for Ho Ga-Myung, but it would never be expected by others.

But, Jang Ilso didn’t look happy even after accomplishing so many things. He just seemed calm, as if it was simply what he deserved.

Jang Ilso’s eyes were already looking at the next thing. He didn’t pay attention to what he had in hand. Jang Ilso was a person who searched for the next move before tearing off the flesh of the animal he killed.

“Thanks to the Mount Hua ones, the plan came 5 years early... no, 10 years early.”

“Right.”

“Do they think that I want to sincerely congratulate them for the founding of the Heavenly Friends Alliance? Hahahaha!”

Jang Ilso held his stomach and laughed.

Wasn’t it like slapping someone who wanted to cry?

Kangho was like stagnant water. No one lacked anything, yet no one risked anything.

That Heavenly Friends Alliance broke the balance of Kangho, which had been peaceful.

If things had happened according to plan, it would have taken more than 10 years. But the Heavenly Friends Alliance... no, thanks to Mount Hua, all this could be achieved at once.

“It is a truly happy and joyful thing, but... if you think back, it is dangerous. I couldn’t let go of the chance, so I moved, but I am not fully ready.”

“The more the danger, the greater the gain.”

“Right. True. As expected, Ga-Myung, you know my heart.”

He had four snakes in his stomach.

If he didn’t provide them with comfort, those snakes would bite his stomach and jump out. In that case, Jang Ilso would give up his life without hesitation.

But...

‘I will find out soon.’

Jang Ilso’s stomach acid was melting the snakes.

“It remains to be seen who will wield it.”

Jang Ilso’s blood-red lips opened slightly, revealing pure white teeth. They were frighteningly sharp.

“Let’s go.”

“Are you planning on going in person?”

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, clan leader.”

“People should never forget a grudge. But that also applies to grace.”

Ho Ga-Myung looked at Jang Ilso with puzzled eyes.

“If someone has shown favor, isn’t it also natural for those who have received it to repay the favor back?”

“... you are right... then?”

“I need to pay back. I don’t know if it is enough, but I will have to pay it back.”

Jang Ilso laughed.

“Of course, it remains to be seen if they will like my gift, but in any case, it is meaningful in expressing sincerity.”

Ho Ga-Myung, seeing Jang Ilso’s smile, nodded.

Sometimes, his position as general felt meaningless.

He was unrivaled when it came to using his brains, no matter where he went, and had the confidence to shake anyone with his words. But only one person, Jang Ilso, was inscrutable to him.

However, Ho Ga-Myung didn’t feel anxious about it.

Jang Ilso was Jang Ilso. He wasn’t someone that normal people could judge.

Ho Ga-Myung glanced at Jang Ilso, who had already closed his eyes and was deep in thought. Ho Ga-Myung held his breath so as not to disturb his thoughts.

Jang Ilso closed his eyes and pressed his temples with his fingers.

‘Now is the start.’

It was just one step. There was still a long way to go. And the road ahead would continue to be a difficult and thorny one. Just walking would tear your flesh, and thorns would get stuck in your bones.

However, when he thought about what he would gain by walking there, Jang Ilso was able to walk with a smile, not only on a thorny path but one with fire too.

A hungry person never stopped.

And the day would never come when his hunger vanished.

“No fight?”

“It seems like it.”

Chung Myung suddenly grabbed Im So-Byeong by the collar and shook him.

“What do you mean? If your dirty bastards and vile Justice faction people gather, everyone will not care and just fight! Why aren’t they fighting! Why! I paid down the full plan!”

“Ugh! Leave me alone!”

“No! Don’t you people fight with each other and die too! What is with you!”

Baek Cheon sighed as he heard those words.

“Chung Myung...”

“Uh?”

“You should say things like that where no one can hear.”

“Ah.”

Even Baek Cheon was still unable to handle the fact that a Taoist was saying such a thing.

‘You seem like the apostle.’

Dirty bastards and the vile Justice faction? Did this guy even think before speaking?

“Cough! Cough! That... it seems like the pirates ran without care when they saw the Justice Faction!”

“Can’t they chase? Don’t the Shaolin bastards have footwork? Where are they? This guy and that guy, if they look for them, they will find them all!”

“Footwork is meaningless. They are on ships. So how do they follow?”

“Then get on a ship and chase them!”

“... then won’t the pirates attack the ship? Then what will happen?”

“What happens? What happens then? Good! The bastards are all going to fall into the water; it is a feast!”

“....”

Im So-Byeong, who looked at Chung Myung with blank eyes, helplessly turned his gaze to Baek Cheon.

Baek Cheon, unable to bear it, lowered his head.

‘I am sorry.’

A bandit who lived off the people was questioning the character of a disciple of a Taoist sect. Of course, it was nonsense, but the person who couldn’t refute such nonsense was Chung Myung.

“Anyway... no, leave that aside. Anyway... it seems like they abandoned the pirate base and went to the Yangtze River.”
