“Think about it. They must be pissed, those bastards!”

Flames rose in his eyes.

‘Even in simple missions, they fought all day and night over which sect to deploy. The damn bastards!’

The more he thought about it, the more his teeth were clenched.

At that time, he should have just ripped the heads off those bastards without care! Why did he put up with it? To enjoy wealth and fame? Only to end up like this?

It would have been alright if everyone else was defeated, regardless of whether his sahyungs and sajaes would stop him.

“... I don’t know why people are fussing around like that.”

“Ugh.”

Chung Myung sighed deeply.

“They will come to a conclusion soon.”

“Uh? What?”

“In the end, they will be fighting over who will lead the way.”

“... Is it that important?”

“It is.”

Chung Myung shook his head firmly.

“Being a leader is a position where you make the greatest contribution and at the same time take the greatest risk. I am saying this because it is a delicate, complicated, and ambiguous position, which feels strangely awkward for them to step up to. Still, it is also a shame to give it to someone else.”

“What is he even saying now? Insane bastard.”

“... what does sasuk even know?”

“...”

Looking at the shocked Baek Cheon, Chung Myung clicked his tongue and gestured to the front.

“Don’t worry. As I said, this is a matter with a definite conclusion. No matter how much controversy there is, there is just one place that will be at the top.”

“Shaolin?”

“No, the Namgung family.”

“Uh?”

Everyone who heard the unexpected words was shocked. Jo Gul spoke on behalf of everyone’s feelings.

“Uh... no, of course, the Namgung family isn’t one with low status....”

“There was Shaolin and Wudang, so why would the Namgung family be at the forefront?”

“Uh.”

It was a question worth asking. Chung Myung explained with a small smile.

“If there was just Shaolin or Wudang, the Namgung family wouldn’t have stood at the forefront. But if they both are there, they can step forward. If Qingcheng is also there, there is no one but Namgung to stand at the front.”

“What does that mean?”

No matter how he thought of it, Jo Gul couldn’t understand it at all.

However, Baek Cheon frowned slightly as if he understood what Chung Myung meant.

“... are you saying that they would rather hand over the credit to the Five Great Families than see one of the Nine Great Sects lead it?”

“Oh. Dong-Ryong’s head still works, huh?”

Baek Cheon burst out laughing as if it was absurd. But Yoon Jong asked back as if he didn’t understand at all.

“No matter what, aren’t Shaolin, Wudang, and Qingcheng part of the same group? Of course, they claim to be close to the Five Great Families, but they can’t be compared to the Nine Great Sects One Union, right? So why come to that conclusion?”

Chung Myung looked at Yoon Jong as if he were impressed.

“Wow, our sahyung is so kind. How can you be this naïve?”

“... just curse at me, Chung Myung.”

“It seems like sahyung doesn’t know much about it, but it’s normal for people to dislike seeing others succeed. If a complete stranger buys the land, it might be okay, but if your own cousin buys it, you’ll feel like your stomach is turning.”

“Isn’t that a bit too harsh a way of looking at the world?”

“Then watch.”

Chung Myung gestured to the front again.

“Perhaps I might be wrong.”

“...”

Yoon Jong looked in the direction Chung Myung pointed with doubtful eyes.

At that moment, those who had formed a camp began to move.

“Who is it?”

With everyone paying attention, those in light blue robes rushed to the riverside at once.

“It is the Namgung family!”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung with slightly shocked eyes.

‘How does this guy manage to get it right?’

“What? Is it interesting?”

“...”

“There is nothing to be so surprised about. If you understand what kind of people they are, anyone can guess it.”

Chung Myung twitched his lips mockingly and drank again.

“That is alright, but... it seems like the Namgung family head is also a person with great merit. I see he cornered them into a hard place to give him the spot. But... since the Tang family has broken away from the Five Great Families, they will have to show that the Five Great Families are still strong. Hehe.”

Baek Cheon sighed softly as he listened to him.

‘Anyway, this guy is truly unpredictable.’

Everyone in Mount Hua knew Chung Myung was a lot smarter than they had first thought. But even so, he never imagined that even the heads of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families would move according to his predictions.

Even as they were talking, the troops were advancing.

The disciples of the Namgung family, who rushed to the river, quickly boarded the ship anchored there. The process from completing boarding to retrieving the anchor passed by literally in the blink of an eye.

‘He is good.’

Baek Cheon was deeply impressed.

It might be possible to somehow imitate that speed, but that unerring consistency was something that Mount Hua never dreamed of. It was a sight that made one guess how strictly they lived under control and how hard they trained.

‘That Namgung family....’

They seemed to prove that the reputation of being the leader of the Five Great Families wasn’t something just anyone could earn. The ships carrying the Namgung family quickly moved ahead to confront the pirate ships occupying the other side of the river.

Jo Gul shouted, flustered.

“A head-on breakthrough?!”

“Even so, they are pirates!”

The other disciples of Mount Hua also looked at the rushing ship of the Namgung family with startled eyes.

The number of ships captured by the pirates was greater than expected. But even that was just a handful compared to the numerous pirate ships that could be filled on the other side.

“Will it be alright? Isn’t the quality of the troops different?”

“Ah, no, but if they attack from underwater, there is nothing they can do to stop it, right?”

Perhaps they had the same thought as Baek Cheon when they saw the approaching ship and began to jump into the river. It was clear that the intention was to reach down and make a hole in the bottom of the ship.

But Namgung Hwang wasn’t without a plan.

At the very front of the ship moving ahead, a person stood on top of the lion-shaped figurehead.

Srng.

The Namgung family head drew his sword and stared ahead with an angry look.

“These insignificant pirates!”

He raised the sword above his head.

Wooong!

The qi that rose from his sword raged like a storm. Soon, a milky sword light bloomed from the blade and enveloped it.

“Taaat!”

The sword was then brought down in a single slash.

At the same time, a sword the size of a house, surrounded by qi, shot out like a cannon, an unbelievable sight.

Slassshhhh!

The moment this enhanced sword, which could barely even be described as simply formidable, touched the river, the flowing water exploded in all directions. In an instant, the entire river was pushed to the left and right, splitting into multiple sections.

“W-what is that!”

“Fuck!”

The disciples of Mount Hua were shocked and exclaimed loudly.

“Splitting a river with a sword? For real?”

It was such a spectacular sight.

The milky sword qi moved forward without losing strength, even after splitting the river. In an instant, it crashed into the ship ahead and camped in front.

A ship made of planks was too weak and insignificant to withstand the enhanced sword that split it.

Kwaang!

The ship was cut and exploded, with debris flying everywhere.

“Ackkk!”

“Ackkk!”

Those who were swept away in the aftermath, screaming and flying away, were in a better situation. Those who were hit directly by the sword were shredded to pieces and died without knowing how they died.

Kwang! Kwaang!

As if completely destroying a ship wasn’t enough, the enhanced sword destroyed two ships in succession and only stopped after destroying half of another ship.

One sword slash.

With a single slash, he shattered as many as three ships, which were now sinking.

“Ackkkkk!”

“W-what is that!”

The screams came not just from those on the ship.

The shock of those who jumped into the water was the same.

The destructive strength of Namgung Hwang’s single slash, which disrupted the water, was astonishing. Those caught in the suddenly formed vortex were swept away without a chance to react, finding themselves in an unimaginable situation where their bodies were pushed atop the water.

What awaited them as they fell was the deep riverbed, a sight they never thought they would encounter in their lives.

Thud! Thud! Thud!

Soon, a massive amount of water crashed down on the pirates lying on the riverbed. The divided river returned to its original course, immediately crushing the pirates with its immense pressure.

Namgung Hwang, who orchestrated this situation beyond normal comprehension, where the pirates on the Yangtze River drowned and perished, roared like an enraged tiger.

“Strike! Let those insignificant pirates know the name of Namgung!”

“Yes!”

Upon Namgung Hwang’s command, the disciples of the Namgung family leaped from the ship without hesitation, diving into the swirling Yangtze River.

Everyone who witnessed the scene couldn’t help but shudder.

“...That is the Namgung family.”

“Namgung Hwang... Sword Emperor.”

It was a moment when the disciples of Mount Hua could witness firsthand the majesty of a truly powerful man who commanded the world.

Hyun Jong’s eyes were filled with shock.

‘Namgung Hwang.’

To be honest, the way he behaved at the gathering of the leaders a little while back was something to be frowned upon.

Of course, it wasn’t that he didn’t understand the resentment he might have towards Mount Hua. Still, even so, he clearly didn’t fit the status of the Namgung family head, which can be called the best in the world.

But what Namgung Hwang showed now was enough to blow those thoughts from Hyun Jong’s mind.

‘A reason?’

It seemed like Namgung Hwang was speaking to him.

In a world of tolerance, flammability and other trivial things aren’t important. What the head of the Namgung family needs is the strength to carry the name on his back. That is enough.

“...The absolute warrior.”

Baek Cheon’s palms turned damp with cold sweat.

‘How does he compare to the high priest?’

The comparison felt difficult to make.

The high priest was truly a strong and terrifying being. But at that time, Baek Cheon couldn’t even judge the high priest. He had no choice but to rush ahead with all his strength, ready to die and leave the rest to Chung Myung.

But that wasn’t the case now.

As he was coldly and calmly witnessing with his own eyes the strength radiating from this absolute warrior from a distance, his spine felt tingly, and his hands were shaking.

‘The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.’

Baek Cheon realized at that moment.

How ridiculously he had looked at the name all this time.

“Grrr!”

The blue water of the Yangtze immediately turned red.

The Blue Sky sword troops had jumped into the water and began to kill the pirates.

Although their swords, which were lesser in strength, couldn’t split the river like Namgung Hwang, they split the current easily. Moreover, the strength they had built up since childhood allowed them to maintain their movements in the water the same as outside.

Then, all that remained was the enormous gap in skill.

Slash!

The sword that sliced through the water pierced the chest of the pirates. Those who approached quickly were repelled even faster and sank into the water.

“Huh!”

Namgung Hwang observed the scene and swung his sword.

“With peace enduring, we see how audacious the pirates are to attack the Namgung family, even with us present!”

He should let them know.

The reason they had only been observing the bastards of the Evil faction until now wasn’t due to a lack of power.

Wooong!

Namgung Hwang’s sword began to gather tremendous strength again. It was such an incredible force that one could hardly imagine a person holding it within their dantian.

“That... that...”

As Baek Cheon, who was watching, spoke in astonishment, Chung Myung spoke coldly.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“Open your eyes and look.”

Chung Myung gestured toward the ship Namgung Hwang was on.

“Do you know why the Namgung family is considered the strongest in the world?”

“... why?”

At that moment, several thoughts crossed Baek Cheon’s mind. Because their strength is immense, their children are born with extraordinary qualities, they can create spiritual pills with their wealth, and they can focus on training in a stable environment...

But Baek Cheon knew none of these were the answers Chung Myung sought.

Chung Myung said quietly.

“The Namgung family is full of idiots. In fact, if they want to pursue victory, it is better to wield the enhanced sword rather than the blade. Right, just like the Hubei Peng family.”

Baek Cheon nodded at those words.

If one was going to use an enhanced sword, it was much easier to wield a thick and strong sword than a thin and cumbersome blade.

“But those people pursued the blade yet never let go of their swords. They are greedy individuals who hold two things that are difficult to coexist—qi and sophistication—with both hands and eventually succeed in making them compatible.”

“...”

“If you are a swordsman, Sasuk, too, keep a close eye on that person’s sword. You will see a sword that is different from Mount Hua’s.”

“I see.”

Baek Cheon’s face became serious. He nodded and intently looked at Namgung Hwang. The other disciples must have heard their conversation, and all of them looked at Namgung Hwang seriously.

Chung Myung, feeling satisfied with the sight, nodded.

‘Nice.’

In fact, the reason Chung Myung thought about retreating wasn’t just because he didn’t want to fight with the pirates.

What the disciples of Mount Hua lacked was experience.

But the experience didn’t just mean fighting with their hands and experiencing with their bodies. It is also a great experience to see how others fight and what martial arts they use with our own eyes.

Mount Hua, Wudang, Namgung family.

During the time when Chung Myung was the Plum Blossom Sword Saint, the three groups were collectively called the best sword sects in the world. And wasn’t the highest leader of each sect referred to as one of the World’s Three Greatest Swordsmen?

Mount Hua had the Beautiful Sword. The Wudang has the Soft Sword, and the Namgung family pursues the powerful Enhanced Sword and broken swords, which were difficult to find among sword weapons.

‘The sword of Mount Hua is the best.’

But that didn’t mean it was perfect. If something more could be obtained from Namgung Hwang’s sword, the swords of the Mount Hua disciples would become more perfect.

“Taaat!”

Namgung Hwang swung his sword again.

His Enhanced Sword surged left and right. It looked as if some dragon that lived in the Yangtze River was playing the role of a dragon.

His Enhanced Sword flew across the river and swallowed a huge ship.

Kwaang!

It was a destructive power that couldn’t be compared to a cannon. It blew up the entire ship and then destroyed several ships in succession.

A straight path began to emerge between the densely packed pirate ships.

Quite literally, the Emperor’s Sword.

The Emperor only moved forward. They knew how to advance, but had no idea how to retreat or choose a different path.

And Namgung Hwang’s sword was the Emperor’s Sword, which destroyed everything in its way.

“Kill them all! Make those who dare to stand in Namgung’s way regret not knowing their place!”

“Yes!”

The ships flanking Namgung Hwang’s vessel on the left and right increased their speed and surged forward.

Srng.

Namgung Hwang slowly raised his head and turned it gradually.

“Huh.”

Initially, the pirates blocking the path were not even aware. What he was watching closely were the Wudang, Qingcheng, and Shaolin ships trailing behind him.

Namgung Hwang, staring at them with cold eyes, gestured forward.

He could see the Mount Hua sect observing them with wide eyes.

‘Huh!’

The shock on their faces was unmistakable.

‘What a bunch of morons.’

They were fools who believed they had achieved something just because they had some fame. That was why Namgung Hwang didn’t like Mount Hua. A truly strong person doesn’t act carelessly and must build their strength.

“Watch carefully. The strength of the Namgung family.”

“Hmm.”

Venerable Heo Do slightly twitched his lips and let out a laugh.

“It looks like the Namgung family is quite excited.”

“Must be so, right? A warrior who has no use for a sword is meaningless.”

“Yes.”

Peace had lasted too long.

Enough to replenish the strength exhausted during the past war with the Demonic Sect... no, enough time had passed to gather even more qi.

Those who have gathered strength always wanted to prove it. But there was no way or chance to do so in Kangho until now. Ultimately, the proof of strength was possible only when there was an enemy. They couldn’t run out onto the street and perform a sword dance to prove their level of martial arts, was it?

“Anyway, the strength of the Namgung family is a lot more than I expected.”

“Right. This means that Namgung Hwang also gritted his teeth like us.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone coldly. He felt his stomach churn. The perfection of the sword was out of the question. Still, it was an exciting and eccentric sword that had a way of stimulating people.

As expected.

“Am-itah-bha!”

A loud roar was heard from the ship on the right, and a golden fist rushed ahead.

The immense strength of this intangible fist ran down the water! The pirate ship caught in it was instantly crushed, losing its shape.

It wasn’t an attack that broke and destroyed things like Namgung Hwang’s sword. But it was a blow to the ship that crushed it with its enormous weight.

The method might be different, but the strength it carried was in no way inferior to that of Namgung Hwang’s sword.

“It seems like the Buddhist monk’s cultivation is as deep as Beop Gye’s.”

“Even if he is a monk, he is a warrior too. The spirit of winning is bound to be in him.”

Venerable Heo Do laughed softly.

If the abbot were here, he would have smiled kindly watching Namgung Hwang run wild. But, no matter how great Beop Gye was in the world, he was just an elder. There was bound to be impatience that Shaolin, whom he led, shouldn’t fall behind Namgung.

“What do you think?”

“It isn’t the right stage yet.”

Venerable Heo Do let out a bitter laugh.

It was by no means lacking, but the form of martial arts felt different. No matter how hard they tried, it would be difficult to keep up with the impact of the sword.

‘There is nothing wrong with overdoing, but there is no need to do it now.’

This wasn’t the stage meant for them. The real stage was right inside the valley.

“Namgung and Shaolin will open the path. As soon as we enter, we disembark and drive away the pirates.”

“Yes, sect leader!”

Just in time, Namgung Hwang’s ship, which had taken the lead, finally cleared the path in a straight line.

The pirate ships, which had been firmly positioned with confidence in their numbers, became frightened and began to turn their bows to the left and right. Of course, they probably knew that if they lost the Black Dragon pirates’ base, there would be no future for them. However, the force of Namgung Hwang, who was using the enhanced sword right before their eyes, was enough to blow away their reason and instill fear within.

Just two sword strikes.

With just those two, Namgung Hwang succeeded in creating a huge path between the gathered ships, so much so that water couldn’t be seen.

“Don’t worry, bastards! The following sects will handle things. We need to clear the path to Black Dragon Palace!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang smiled.

‘You gave me the lead?’

They had their own calculations, so they obediently placed the Namgung family at the forefront...

‘Your miscalculation is that you underestimated the strength of the Namgung family.’

There was absolutely no intention that the name and fame that follow will give credit. As long as they were there, the escape route was blocked. They would enter at once and destroy everything in one swoop.

May only the name of Namgung resound on the Yangtze River.

“Do-wei!”

“Yes, lord!”

Namgung Hwang glanced back at Namgung Do-wei. His face was red and ready to fight.

“When we enter, take the lead. Regain the lost fame with your own hands!”

“I will surely do so.”

“Nice!”

Now, the path leading to the valley was stretched out before the eyes of Namgung Hwang.

“Speed up! Enter at once! Don’t worry about traps! I will crush them with my hands!”

“Yes!”

The warriors of the Namgung family, whose morale was soaring, shouted in unison, causing the entrance to the valley to resound.

Numerous ships were rushing through the Yangtze River’s currents into a deep, dark valley.

“S-stop them! Don’t let them get in!”

The leaders of the pirates, who had gathered at the Black Dragon Pirates’ meeting, shouted. But no matter how much they screamed or struggled, nothing worked.

The pirates, completely terrified, were busy shooting their bows in panic. They were desperately trying to escape from the Namgung family’s ship, which was rapidly approaching with every passing moment.

“You damn bastards! Do you not hear me?”

If this were land, they would have run immediately and broken the heads of those not listening to them. Unfortunately, this was the Yangtze River. No matter how talented they were at slaying dragons, they couldn’t possibly run to another ship, right?

“Come back, right now! You fucking bastards!”

The leader of the White Land Pirates, who was scolding them, gritted his teeth, his face turning red.

“Shoot! Shoot! Fucking shoot!”

The ship he was on was still under control. The pirates clung to the railing and fired the harpoons.

Tuung! Tunng!

Large iron harpoons, the length of a human body, flew fiercely at the Namgung family ship. But not a single one reached its target.

Kaang!

The warriors of the Namgung family, who were guarding the ship and sniping at them, jumped out of the water and knocked away the harpoons. Those on the ship neatly swung their swords, completely deflecting the harpoons.

The sight of harpoons flying with terrifying force, being hit by those thin swords and bouncing back helplessly, was unbelievable.

“This...”

“Ah, no! Captain! Harpoons don’t work, and those approaching in the water are being killed! There is no way!”

Crack.

The captain’s eyes turned red as they became bloodshot.

“Go ahead.”

“Uh?”

“If the ships, which are now running away in fear, join and attack from all around, no matter how great they are, they cannot stop all of them! If we take the lead and charge ahead bravely, the other ships will also change their minds!”

“Ha! But! Captain! You just saw them! They...”

“Shut up! No matter how great the Namgung family is, we are the 18 Pirates! Are you telling me to be scared of some Justice faction who is on the Yangtze River now?”

“...”

“If the harpoon doesn’t work, just go for the ship! Just blow a hole in the ship! No matter how great they are, they don’t have water arts! And they are nothing without a ship.”

“C-captain... Please think again...”

Chang!

The captain pulled out his large spear.

“You, if this spear hits your mouth, will you obey me then? Will you not turn the wheel?”

“I-I understand!”

The subordinate moved immediately, and the captain’s face contorted.

‘These bastards have no loyalty.’

Actually, it was understandable for his subordinate to just run, but he wasn’t allowing it now.

It was acceptable to abandon the other pirates and join the Black Dragon pirates. But the situation wasn’t great. And abandoning the Black Dragon pirates wasn’t something that could be forgiven.

‘The Black Dragon King will not leave me alive!’

Even considering the Black Dragon King’s persistent nature, there was no way his safety could be guaranteed.

“Whether you die this way or that way, it is the same! So move! Hit them from the side!”

“Yes!”

The pirates had to die one way or another. If they rejected the order now, they would have spears pierced their chests.

Gritting their teeth, they turned the rudder and started rowing. The ship cut through the current and rushed toward the Namgung family.

“Hit!”

It was at that moment that the evil-stricken pirates let out the shout.

Paat!

White sword qi spread out from the Namgung ship at the forefront and engulfed their ship.

Kwang!

The captain was shocked.

Due to the nature of the ships, which often collided with each other, they were made of wood that was twice as hard as ordinary wood. But this sword qi split the wood like tofu.

“This...”

The deck was split in half. As he looked at the black hole, cold sweat ran down his spine.

But that wasn’t the end.

Paat! Paat!

White Sword qi poured out one after another. The people standing on the railing swung their swords simultaneously with expressionless faces, and each time a sword was swung, a half-moon-shaped sword qi emerged.

Kwang! Kwang! Crack!

The pirate ship lost its momentum in an instant and turned into a rag.

“What nonsense!”

The captain screamed.

After all, sword qi wasn’t something anyone could just perform. In particular, someone who could project sword qi this far away and use it to cut the ship was worthy of being called an expert.

But how could something like this happen as if many experts were on one ship?

‘T-that Namgung family?’

The pirate’s face turned white.

‘Is this strong?’

He only realized it when he saw it with his own eyes.

The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families. These were people they were familiar with, and it was natural for them to be called strong.

But that was also why they were underestimated.

Only now was he able to clearly see with his own eyes why they could dominate Kangho for so long.

“C-captain!”

“T-the ship is sinking! Run!”

“It is already filled with water...”

Slash!

But before he could finish speaking, a white sword qi came and cut his throat. Blood splashed all over the deck. At the same time, the barely holding-on ship tipped over and became useless. The blue water rose above the deck at an incredible speed.

“Those monsters....”

The pirate captain was devastated and looked at the Namgung family ship with exhausted eyes.

What was even more humiliating than this defeat was that the Namgung ship didn’t care about them anymore. The Namgung family ships were entering the valley, leaving them alone for the other ships to handle.

“C-captain!”

“Abandon the ship and jump into the water! Join the ships behind!”

The captain who gave the order looked down at his hands.

He was shaking so badly that he seemed pathetic.

No matter how fierce and vicious the snakes were, in a place where tigers and dragons ran rampant, they were nothing more than a meal. Realizing his own limitations, he closed his eyes and turned away in despair.

“Catch up!”

Beop Gye spoke in a loud voice.

His dull eyes were fixed on the ship of the Namgung family ahead.

‘Emperor’s Sword Namgung Hwang. I thought he had grown a bit, but...’

Of course, at best, they were dealing with just novices. But just by looking at it, the Namgung family was clearly showing the strength they possessed.

“Amitabha.”

As the Namgung family was now demonstrating, the place where the strength of the sect was originally supposed to be displayed was none other than Shaolin. However due to the checks between Wudang and Qingcheng, in the end, Namgung was gaining more for its reputation.

‘How greedy can one be!’

Although it was a matter of defeating the Yangtze River pirates, they were only seeking the safety of the sect.

He was finally able to understand the words of the abbot, advising him not to meddle in Kangho’s affairs.

“Speed up! The distance cannot be widened!”

“But, Elder, shouldn’t we keep a reasonable distance? If the back is surrounded...”

“Surrounded.”

Beop Gye frowned.

“Is being blocked at the back being surrounded? No matter how much they try to surround us, will they be able to stop us?”

“That...”

Hae Jin, unable to answer, simply mumbled.

From the standpoint of war, a route of retreat had to be secured in any situation. But now the statement wasn’t wrong. This was the path that the Namgung family cleared in one swoop. There was no way Shaolin wouldn’t be able to break through the path, right?

This wasn’t just about building pride. That was the way it was. It was true that the Namgung family showed great strength, but this was Shaolin, not some other place.

“All those remaining behind need to do is secure retreat...”

At that moment, Beop Gye stopped talking and shut his mouth.

The Wudang ships, which were advancing alongside them, suddenly increased their speed and were moving quickly. It looked like they were done thinking.

“This!”

The voice of Beop Gye grew louder.

“Qingcheng will handle the back! Hurry up! We cannot fall behind Wudang!”

“Yes!”

When the name Wudang came up, Hae Jin’s face became stiff.

Giving the vanguard to the Namgung family wasn’t something that could be helped. But no matter what, there was no way the merit could be taken by Wudang.

In that case, wouldn’t it be better for the Namgung family to monopolize the merit?

‘Venerable Heo Do!’

Beop Gye’s face contorted.

They were just a little quick to judge. However, thanks to this, the Wudang ships were already ahead of them by a dozen meters.

‘If the abbot were here, he might have tolerated their actions, but I will not sit idly!’

Beop Gye lightly clenched his fists and gritted his teeth.

The Wudang ships came close behind the ships of Namgung, who were in the lead. After that, the Shaolin and Qingcheng ships increased their speed as well.

It would have been spectacular in its own way if the urgent change of formation was done in a neat and orderly manner, but at first glance, it was more chaos than grandeur.

It felt so odd for the sects belonging to the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.

The Mount Hua disciples who were watching this were at a loss for words.

“... Chung Myung.”

“What?”

Baek Cheon looked ahead with curiosity and asked.

“...It was truly impressive to see the Namgung family move ahead with majesty and the pirates retreat. But in the end, if they do that, won’t all the pirates on the river run?”

“Yes.”

“If that happens, the pirates’ numbers wouldn’t exactly decrease. In that case, wouldn’t it be right for at least two sects to wait and sort those pirates out? In any case, just two sects can handle the Black Dragon pirates.”

“Right, right.”

“Then why are they doing that?”

Chung Myung laughed.

“Who will even stay and handle them?”

“Anyone can do it. They are pirates, and they are the Nine Great Sects.”

“Is there any way to gain merit by catching those who are running?”

“...”

“They have come a long way, but no one wants to pass up the merit to gain. Regardless of whether there are any pirates left on the Yangtze River or not, all they have to do is cut off the Black Dragon King’s head or destroy the Black Dragon pirates.”

Baek Cheon’s face went stiff.

“You are saying that they came to help the civilians and gain fame?”

“Sasuk.”

“... Yes?”

“Watch carefully. This is the nature of those who claim to be the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families. If only one sect had come, they would have handled it, cleared the pirates, and prepared themselves before entering.”

“...”

“But the moment two or more sects gather in one place, whether it’s a plan or not, they will never give up.”

Chung Myung’s mouth twitched.

A sight he had grown tired of seeing.

‘But I cannot call them entirely wrong either.’

He and Mount Hua, weary of that nature, fought against the Demonic Sect without seeking gain or fame. And what was the price of that?

If Chung Myung hadn’t come back, Mount Hua would have eventually collapsed into debt. Everyone else would have monopolized the merits and benefits. Still, fearlessly making a name for themselves as one of the world’s prestigious sects.

Being first when no one recognized it?

Such nonsense. Now he knew.

The graves of those who died without anyone knowing were just weeds to be pulled out. Chung Myung never intended to follow in the footsteps of the past.

“... they are cold and harsh. And they are the Nine Great Sects.”

“And fierce. Still, don’t criticize them because their acts are ridiculous. The order of precedence is wrong. Even though they are part of the Great Sects, it isn’t like they are not stubborn. They are stubborn in their way because they are part of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.”

“...”

Baek Cheon nodded as if he understood something.

“But you told us to watch closely. If they go inside, we won’t be able to see what’s happening, right?”

“Eh, come on. There’s a way.”

“What?”

“There it is.”

Chung Myung gestured to the side.

On the other side of the scattered pirate ships, some new ships appeared.

“... what are they?”

“Our ship.”

“Huh?”

Baek Cheon was shocked.

“Our ship? That one?”

“Yes. Just in case, I told them to bring an extra ship. I thought I wouldn’t be able to use it because we left late, but thanks to those idiots fighting and taking more time, we got to use it.”

“...”

Chung Myung wore a victorious grin.

“Let’s go. We need to see with our own eyes whether they went into a place of treasure or the mouth of a tiger.”

“... seriously, I cannot understand you.”

Baek Cheon shook his head and looked at the ships entering the valley. His expression gradually hardened.

Strangely, the scene seemed more ominous to him...

“Yah! Bring some extra alcohol!”

“...”

No.

It was all just an illusion.

“Hmm!”

Namgung Hwang frowned as he entered the valley.

The terrain felt strange.

The long path filled with water between the cliffs was wide enough for two ships to pass side by side. It couldn’t be called narrow, but it wasn’t exactly wide, either.

‘There is no better place than this for the entrance of the water through which ships must pass.’

Moreover, the cliff was quite high.

As they ventured further into the valley, the surroundings grew darker. When he raised his head and looked up, it seemed like a long blue line was drawn in the black sky.

“Don’t let your guard down!”

Namgung Hwang shouted, his voice echoing around.

“A lion does his best to catch a rabbit too. The opponent is the Evil Faction! We don’t know what they will do. There is no shame in losing due to skill, but death due to inattention is an even greater shame!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang checked the surroundings with sharp eyes.

It was his first time leading so many people against such pirates. Even though he was overly excited, it wasn’t a particularly strange situation.

But as the Namgung family was being led by him, his eyes turned colder as if to prove that the position of the head wasn’t a given.

“Father.”

“Call me lord!”

“Yes, lord!”

Namgung Dowi quickly corrected himself.

“The road is narrow, and the currents are strong. If the ship is attacked here...”

“Don’t worry.”

Namgung Hwang spoke firmly.

“To prepare for that, I had the Qingcheng Sect escort the ship. There is no way that insignificant pirates would be able to throw a harpoon on the path and hit us.”

Namgung Dowi nodded.

It might sound arrogant at first glance, but Namgung Do-wei knew. This was not arrogance. The arrogance displayed by someone qualified to be arrogant should be called confidence.

“Instead...”

Namgung Hwang’s gaze turned upward.

His lips curled into a mocking smile.

“They don’t even know the place. If they are pirates, we just ram through them.”

At that, Namgung Do-wei’s gaze turned to the place where Namgung Hwang’s eyes were focused.

“Here they come!”

People wearing black robes were fiercely charging towards them, climbing the cliffs that lined up like screens to the left and right.

‘They are different!’

A moment of anxiety appeared on the face of Namgung Do-wei.

Running down that steep cliff as if it were flat wasn’t something possible with the usual footwork. Just looking at them, it was clear that the black-robed men were on a different level from the pirates they had faced until then.

“Well, is this the Black Dragon pirates?”

Namgung Hwang chuckled.

Before he could even give the order, the Blue Sky swordsmen on board the ship rose up like a large bird soaring on the wing and ran to the cliff.

The two groups became entangled on the sheer cliff with no room to step, an extremely steep slope.

“Hit them!

“Kill them all!”

Sword qi was all around.

Sharp swords pierced the bodies of the pirates, and the long spears thrown by the pirates split the bodies of the swordsmen.

Slash!

A pirate with a long cut across his chest fell into the river without even being able to scream.

Plop! Plop!

Anyone could see that the Blue Sky swordsmen were winning. Although it was unlikely they had trained to fight on cliffs, their martial arts skills, steadily built over a long time, ensured their victory.

“This cannot be all of them.”

Namgung Hwang looked up.

“Shoot!”

Sure enough, the sky visible above the narrow canyon was suddenly filled with black arrows.

“Right.”

This was a very natural form of attack.

However, contrary to the visible intimidation, it was nothing more than a useless attack.

Kakang!

Namgung Hwang’s sword struck down the flying arrows.

If this were a normal battlefield where soldiers clashed with one another, the rain of arrows would have been threatening. Even if those that were hit were from proper sects, they would have taken significant damage.

But those now moving were none other than the Blue Sky swordsmen of the Namgung family.

Ordinary arrows would not even inflict a single wound on them, even if shot all day long.

It would certainly have been dangerous to fire an arrow from a short distance with qi on it. Still, it was impossible for an arrow to be shot with such a difference in distance.

In other words, the cliffs, which were too high, were a hindrance for the opponents too.

“Lord! Do we head up?”

“Forget it. Ignore it!”

Namgung Hwang shouted loudly.

“Don’t get caught up with those idiots! The ones following behind will handle it!”

“Yes!”

The ship, which had hesitated for a moment, began to move quickly again. The pirates fighting on the cliff had jumped to block the ship, but none of them could even set foot on the ship Namgung Hwang was on.

“Kuaak!”

“Ack!”

The elders of the Namgung family, who climbed onto the railing as if guarding the ship, cut down the pirates at once.

“Do not dare interfere, you bastards!”

“How dare these stupid evil faction morons aim for us!”

Although they were advancing while being hindered at the front, the Namgung family ship was moving ahead, widening the distance with Wudang, which was following.

The term “handle the tangled with grace and confuse others” suited this setup.

Slash!

The ship sailed through the current. It seemed like no one could stand in front of Namgung Hwang.

Namgung Do-wei was thrilled to see Namgung Hwang standing at the figurehead, which was lion-shaped.

The pirates driven out by the elders were rushing to climb the cliff, and the arrows rained down. And now, not only arrows but also rocks the size of humans were being thrown.

It was literally a war zone.

But the back of Namgung Hwang, who was moving through the center, didn’t waver one bit.

‘Namgung family lord.’

Emperor’s Sword.

Blue Sky Namgung Family Lord.

Namgung Do-wei once again realized the power of his family to a greater extent. He, too, must one day lead the Namgung family in the same way Namgung Hwang was doing.

‘One day, Shaolin and Wudang will kneel at the feet of the Namgung family.’

At the moment when Namgung Do-wei struck down the flying arrow and made the promise, Namgung Hwang shouted.

“I see it!”

Finally, the end of the long canyon ahead was visible. The narrow valley widened greatly, and the innermost part of the valley became clearly visible.

It was huge.

‘It is like the top of a big volcano.’

The water coming in through the narrow channel spread out in a fan shape and reached the land. The terrain there was, so to speak, a vast basin surrounded by steep cliffs.

“I am glad they settled down in a place like this.”

This place was surely a natural fortress.

The entrance was excessively narrow, so multiple ships couldn’t enter, and to get in, one would have to take a lot of damage because they had no choice but to be defenseless against attacks from the top of the cliff.

But that was the story for normal people. This didn’t apply to warriors. No matter what they did up there, if one was a skilled warrior, they could avoid it.

Namgung Hwang smiled.

“If the opponent was us, wouldn’t they be like rats caught with poison?”

Namgung Hwang drew his sword.

“Now! It will hit at once!”

“Yes!”

Several large pavilions were built on the land.

Because the pirates move their ships and bases frequently, they don’t build grand structures. But, the buildings around were huge and magnificent at first glance.

Although other pirates could be thrown away and run away at any time, they could clearly feel the spirit. The determination that the Black Dragon pirates were showing was different.

‘Right, these are the pirates!’

Namgung Hwang stepped firmly onto the figurehead and rose up.

Kuung!

He flew about 20 meters in one leap and landed on the ground.

“DIEEEEE!”

As soon as Namgung Hwang touched the ground, more than a dozen pirates attacked him simultaneously. Their weapons varied, but their force was uniformly sharp.

“So trivial!”

Thud!

Namgung Hwang stepped on a pillar and swung his sword.

Kwaang!

The sword, charged with tremendous qi, cut down all who approached him at once.

No, it was a blow more appropriately described as crushing than cutting.

Those who faced the sword qi, laden with immense power, paid the expected price. Those who were thrown back like cannonballs died before their bodies hit the ground.

“Get lost, you idiots!”

Namgung Hwang roared like a lion.

“Where is the Black Dragon King? I am the Emperor’s Sword, Namgung Hwang!”

With him leading, the swordsmen of the Namgung family leaped to land one after another.

“Lord!”

“Give us the order!”

Namgung Hwang frowned and shouted.

“Kill them all! Find the Black Dragon King and bring him to me!”

“Yes!”

The swordsmen, having received the Lord’s command, rushed forward like the wind.

“Stop them!”

“Those damn bastards!”

The pirates of the Black Dragon also gritted their teeth and blocked the swordsmen.

There was no way out.

Their natural fortress didn’t allow escape, even for the pirates who owned it. If the pirates were invaded, there was just one option left. All they could do was fight to the end.

Among the 18 River Families along the Yangtze River, the Blood Pirates were the most vicious and cruel. The countless sins they committed could not be forgiven by surrendering and begging for their lives.

Then, all that was left was to fight!

“DIE!”

“Kuak!”

The sound of metal clashing against metal rang out like screams.

The skills of each individual were clearly superior among the Namgung swordsmen. With the elders of the Namgung family joining in, no matter how many Black Dragon pirates there were, their strength would be limited.

However, the number was definitely higher on the side of the Black Dragon Pirates.

It was difficult to understand where such a large number of people were coming from, as pirates dressed in black emerged like ants.

“Shoot!”

Kwang!

Kwaang!

Large iron harpoons were fired one after another from the cannons installed in the palace-like place.

The swordsmen of Blue Sky struck down the flying harpoons. But when the direction of the wind changed, one was embedded in the body of a swordsman on the right.

Crush!

“Kuakk...”

If the distance had been longer, they might have been able to respond, but they, too, had no experience in such fights. The swordsman fell down with blood dripping and slowly lost his life.

“Those bastards!”

Namgung Hwang shouted loudly and rushed ahead.

Kwaaang!

The sword qi that erupted from the tip of his sword struck the enemy.

“Show the spirit of the Namgung family!”

“Yes!”

As the Namgung family reached the peaks, the Wudang disciples disembarked from the ships and stood on land.

Venerable Heo Do, who had just disembarked, took the lead into the battle line.

“T-this damn justice...”

Slash!

Venerable Heo, unable to retreat, pierced the head of the hesitating pirate at once and spoke coldly.

“Do not fall behind. We make up for the loss in an instant!”

“Yes!”

Namgung and Wudang.

The two swords, which represented the world, began to kill the pirates as if competing with each other.

“Elder!”

“I know!”

Beop Gye unusually raised his voice.

Shaolin was a sect that taught its disciples to always maintain a steady mind. Beop Gye was also not someone unfamiliar with the importance of steadfastness.

But the scene unfolding before his eyes was enough to instantly undo the long-standing cultivation of Beop Gye.

Blood splattered, and screams echoed around them.

Those who were alive just moments ago suddenly turned into cold corpses.

“Elder! We need to go!”

“Move!”

Beop Gye leaped from the ship and landed on the ground, shouting.

The thick, bloody smell assaulted his nose. In addition, the scent of gunpowder, clouds of dust, and other unidentifiable odors mixed together, making everyone dizzy.

The faces of not only the head monk but also other Shaolin disciples contorted.

“Hurry up! We cannot just have it said that Shaolin merely watched and then returned!”

“Yes!”

As Beop Gye ordered, the Shaolin monks rushed forward all at once. The sight of Shaolin monks in yellow robes performing footwork simultaneously and charging ahead like a hailstorm aroused both admiration and fear.

But just one person.

Hae Yeon didn’t join them.

He closed his eyes tightly as he watched the backs of his sahyungs moving forward.

‘No.’

This wasn’t it.

At least Shaolin shouldn’t be like this. The Shaolin he knew wouldn’t act this way.

Fighting bravely against pirates?

This was expected, and that fight also needed to have a reason.

However, the Shaolin monks, who now rushed ahead fiercely, had no desire to protect the civilians. They were just filled with a sense of pride, not wanting to be seen as inferior to other sects.

‘Why does it feel so empty?’

Hae Yeon bit his lip.

One person’s face came to his mind.

A person who always shouted that it was about reason, selfishness, and profit, and he couldn’t bear to see another sect gain fame.

But in the end, that person’s actions benefited everyone.

‘So different.’

Chung Myung talked about profit, but does justice, while Shaolin speaks of righteousness but pursues profit?

Of course, if Chung Myung had heard this, he would have laughed, but at least that was how Hae Yeon saw it.

The difference made Hae Yeon’s feet heavy to move.

Compared to the time he spent with them in Shaolin, the time he spent in Mount Hua wasn’t much. Nevertheless, Mount Hua played a large role in him.

“Amitabha.”

He took a deep sigh and looked back.

Since they had come this far, there was no way he could see it. The only thing in front of him was the high cliff and the Qingcheng ship entering the valley.

The person who always ran ahead was nowhere to be seen, and the people who supported his back were also nowhere to be seen.

‘Let us not think of it.’

Hae Yeon told himself.

Even if this situation made him suffer, he couldn’t just let his sahyungs fight.

“Amitabha!”

Hae Yeon forced himself to shake off the delusions in his mind and ran ahead.

“Tsk! Damn it!”

“These dirty bastards! Four more are coming! And the Justice Faction at that!”

“D-do not back down!”

The direction of the battlefield was decided in an instant.

Of course, not all the forces of each sect came for this attack. There were quite a few people left in each sect, and there were people who weren’t brought along since the ship couldn’t support the number.

But that applied to the Black Dragon Pirates too.

All the strength of the 18 River Families of the Yangtze River, if gathered, would be comparable to that of one sect. Even if the Black Dragon pirates accounted for about half of the total strength in the Yangtze River families, it would be difficult for them to deal with all the sects gathered.

But now, with as many as four sects forming an alliance against them?

From the beginning, it was a fight that the Black Dragon Pirates had no chance of winning.

Woong!

Paaat!

The majestic sword of the Namgung family and Wudang’s disciples, filled with gentleness and seriousness, fiercely took down the pirates as if competing.

“Amitabha!”

And Shaolin was truly skilled.

Kuang!

A golden fist collided with the pirates, blowing them away.

Shaolin wasn’t a sect that just used its fists. All Shaolin monks were familiar with the 19 forms of martial arts to some extent. Nevertheless, the reason they insisted on fighting in this manner was that there was no better method when fighting to subdue an opponent without killing them.

Tung!

Beop Gye, who defeated the pirate in front of him, erupted into a roar.

“The path of Buddhism isn’t just to look after all living things! It is also the role of a Buddhist to remove the demons and open the way to rebirth! Don’t give mercy to anyone!”

“Yes!”

The blue, white, and yellow troops devoured the Black Dragon pirates ravenously.

‘What about the Black Dragon King?’

‘Where is the pirate leader?’

The situation had escalated to such a point that it seemed it would no longer matter how many more pirates were dealt with. Then, there was only one thing left: who would defeat the Black Dragon King, the leader of the Yangtze pirates?

“Push in!”

“Cut them down!”

The Black Dragon King would naturally be inside the Palace. In that case, the decision will depend on who breaks through there and gets in.

‘I need to go first!’

Namgung Hwang’s eyes shone.

The Black Dragon King is clearly a formidable person who rules over the pirates and has an overwhelming reputation even among the numerous people of the Evils Faction.

But he was Namgung Hwang.

There was no way he would lose against the Black Dragon King.

But the problem was that there were too many people here who thought the same. And so the orders went through, but neither Venerable Heo Do nor he would give up the Black Dragon King.

‘One cannot make porridge only to give it to a dog!’

If they cut off the head of the Black Dragon King, wasn’t it obvious that the achievements of the Namgung family, who stood at the forefront and paved the way, would fade?

Kwaang!

The sword qi wielded by Namgung Hwang exploded like a bomb and swept across the front lines.

“Lord! Save your strength!”

“We will clear the path!”

Namgung Do-wei moved to the front. Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth at the sight.

He felt anxious, but the Black Dragon King was by no means an easy opponent. If he wasted his strength there, the worst could happen, and they might be defeated by the Black Dragon King.

Namgung Hwang suppressed his hasty feelings and looked to the side.

Woong!

Elder Heo Do, with white sword qi wrapped around his sword, swung it gently.

It was such an odd sight.

Smooth and fast were concepts that could never coexist. But in that sword, those two things clearly coexisted.

The sword seemed to have been swung lazily and slowly, but it quickly pierced the enemy’s gap and cut into the vital point.

‘Heo Do!’

The desire to win the battle was welling up in the heart of Namgung Hwang.

One was Namgung Hwang, known as the leader of the strongest sword family in the world. And the other person was Wudang’s sect leader, who was called the greatest swordsman.

Also, at the same time, the two sects were competing for the position of the best swordsmen in the world.

But, despite having such deep and complex relationships, the two never clashed with each other.

It wasn’t like their ties were bad enough to risk a spar with lives on the line, and they had too much to lose to decide the winner with a clumsy fight.

The moment Namgung Hwang’s eyes became sharp, Venerable Heo Do naturally turned his head to look at him.

Namgung Hwang read the calmness in the look and clenched his mouth.

‘Are you still taking your time after seeing my sword?’

Namgung Hwang held the sword tightly.

“Okay. Let’s see how long that leisure lasts!”

Namgung Hwang, whose desire to win was at its peak, rushed forward first.

“Lord!

“You need to conserve your strength!”

“Get out of my way!”

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang, who once again swung the sword and sent the pirates flying, shouted.

“What is the need to save strength when I am only dealing with someone like the Black Dragon King? I am Namgung Hwang, the Emperor’s sword!”

It would have been extremely arrogant if those words came out of someone else’s mouth, but Namgung Hwang clearly demonstrated his worth.

Kwaang!

Once again, the pirates were reduced to flesh by the sword of Namgung Hwang. The force of the swordsmen guarding his sides grew more intense, like piercing the sky.

“Follow the lord!”

“Make sure that the Lord doesn’t stop!”

A Martial Sect and a Martial Family.

Although there were pros and cons, loyalty to the Lord of the family and solidarity with one another were clearly advantages that a Martial Family had. The power of a Family, which was closely tied to blood, was being displayed now.

Kwaang!

“Hmm!”

Venerable Heo Do frowned as he watched Namgung Hwang attack with a sudden change of force.

‘Not even a wild boar rushes in like that.’

A sword wasn’t meant to be used as such.

‘But even so...’

Venerable Heo Do lightly glanced at his own face. Even at such an age, it seemed like the feeling of victory still remained in his heart. He saw the heart beating.

And.

“Do not get too excited.”

He spoke softly. The voice was clearly heard in the ears of the Wudang disciples following him.

“When a chance comes, you just need to grab it. Don’t lose your composure!”

Wudang’s disciples, who were feeling impatient as the Namgung family was attacking the pirates, managed to calm their hearts after those words. Then, they took a short breath and, with a stronger stance, attacked the pirates.

‘Abbot. This is your mistake.’

Whether it was Namgung Hwang or the Qingcheng sect leader, the abbot would have been able to lead Shaolin through this. But since he wasn’t, Shaolin remained inactive.

“Go ahead and go in!”

As Namgung and Wudang simultaneously increased their force, the pirates barely maintained their defense line.

“N-no...”

“We cannot hold on any longer!”

Venerable Heo Do and Namgung Hwang seized the opportunity.

“Get out! You bastards!”

Namgung Hwang exploited the gap, while Venerable Heo Do soared like a crane, leaping over the pirates’ heads and into the palace.

“That!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes gleamed.

“Taat!”

Having crushed the pirates in one swoop, Namgung Hwang pursued Venerable Heo Do like a hungry tiger.

“Oh my, damn it!”

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang, who broke down the Palace door in one go, scanned left and right with sharp eyes.

‘Where are you?’

His body moved before his mind could process it. He immediately dashed like a beam of light and burst through the broken door in the center of the pavilion.

“Black Dragon King!”

Namgung Hwang’s feet, which had jumped in with great force, suddenly stopped.

What he saw was a large inner room, a luxurious and dignified chair in the center, and Venerable Heo Do staring at the chair with a stiff face.

“Where is the Black Dragon King?”

“... He’s not here.”

“W-what?”

Namgung Hwang’s face contorted with bewilderment.

This place was like the heart of the 18 River Families. No matter how powerfully the Black Dragon King had absorbed the pirates into his own, he couldn’t flee before the pirates lost.

So, did it make sense that the Black Dragon King, who should protect the Black Dragon pirates more than anyone, wasn’t there?

Beop Gye, who arrived late, looked at them with a blank expression.

“You are saying the Black Dragon King isn’t here?”

“... seems like it, monk.”

“But how...”

Beop Gye appeared equally flustered.

“The Black Dragon King isn’t here?”

Venerable Heo Do’s expression continued to shift with the repeated use of the words. As he turned his head to look, his eyes widened upon seeing the face of the person who had asked the last question.

“Didn’t the Qingcheng decide to handle the rear?”

The presence of Byeok Hyun indicated that the disciples of Qingcheng were also present.

“What are you doing here? Why?”

“E-even if you say that... from the start, didn’t we decide to handle the pirates on the river and support those who guard the top? Everyone went in without a word, so I naturally expected the strategy to have changed.”

“This...”

Venerable Heo Do’s face showed a look of disappointment.

‘Such a shameless lie!’

It was clear that, out of a desire for recognition, the man had broken the plan and followed them in. But now wasn’t the time to discuss it.

“What is the escape route?”

“It was easy to break through, but is there a need to have an escape route?”

But Elder Heo Do’s face turned cold as if he wore iron armor.

“Then... are you saying everyone is inside now? In this dock?”

Only after the word ‘dock’ was mentioned did the color drain from the faces of the sect leaders.

“It cannot be....”

“No! No way! The Black Dragon pirates are there! They aren’t just the limbs of pirates; they are the head and heart! Who would give up their head and heart to set up a trap?”

“... the Black Dragon King cannot do that.”

“What...?”

“Ahh.”

For a moment, Beop Gye let out an exclamation as he understood Venerable Heo Do’s words. His face turned pale.

“T-trap...”

It was then.

“Uhahahahaahah!”

A loud laugh, which seemed to tear into their ears, exploded through the air.

The loud laughter, filled with tremendous qi, struck the cliffs surrounding them and echoed as if it would last forever.

It was the moment when everyone finally realized that something was wrong.

“This is...”

Venerable Heo Do’s face contorted viciously. As a Taoist, he was not one to show such an expression to others. The fact that his emotions were visible on his face meant the situation was dire.

Tat!

Venerable Heo Do dashed out as if he had nothing more to say.

The other sect leaders, understanding the seriousness, quickly followed his lead.

The battlefield’s scenery had changed. The fighting had already stopped as if the life-and-death struggle from moments ago had been a mere illusion.

Venerable Heo Do’s gaze moved forward. Everyone in the basin was looking in their direction.

On the cliff.

A man stood looking down from a height so great it felt as if one’s neck would break to see him.

Black clothes covered his entire body.

A long, rough black beard reached down to his navel.

His face was as red as hell, and his hair resembled a lion’s mane.

In addition to this unusual appearance, the image of a Black Dragon was engraved on his chest. They knew who it was without needing to say his name.

“Kuahahahaha! What did you bastards of the Justice Faction come all the way to the Yangtze River for?”

Namgung Hwang’s face turned cold.

The tremendous qi in the voice seemed to cut Namgung Hwang’s skin.

‘Is the Black Dragon King really this powerful?’

He had heard of his reputation.

But he thought the man was just another Evil Faction thug. Compared to the Justice Faction, which lives in fierce competition with sahyungs and even their brothers, the Evil Faction seemed nothing more than a group of fools with no trace of sincerity.

He wondered how impressive the head of the Pirates could truly be...

The Black Dragon King, seen with his own eyes, immediately shocked Namgung Hwang.

It felt like a wave of strength was flowing through his body, so intense it sent a shiver down his spine.

“Not only did you dare invade the Yangtze River, but you also crawled into the Black Dragon pirates’ territory. The scum of the Justice Faction has lost their fear!”

Namgung Hwang, who was listening, shouted.

“You coward, hiding in such a distant place, yet you talk so boldly. Do you have anything left to say as someone who abandoned his subordinates and turned his back on them?”

Namgung Hwang’s words made sense. They were effective in calming down the agitated warriors of the Namgung family.

“I turned my back....”

The Black Dragon King sneered as he repeated the words.

“Say whatever you want. I don’t care for the words of someone who will end up in a grave soon.”

“You!”

At that moment, Beop Gye spoke.

“Amitabha. Theravada is said to be the dharma of Shaolin.”

“Huh! How dare an elder who isn’t even the leader of the sect try to speak with me! Shaolin’s arrogance has reached the heavens!”

As the Black Dragon King openly mocked, Beop Gye frowned slightly.

“Lord, I heard your argument well, but what are you going to do about us in this situation? You cannot handle us all by yourself, right?”

“There is nothing I cannot do!”

The Black Dragon King laughed loudly.

“Do I really need anyone else to handle fools like you? My blade is enough.”

The Black Dragon King grabbed the sword at his waist. The handle changed and became longer, soon forming a long scimitar.

Paaat!

The Black Dragon King, who shook the blade in the air, had an arrogant smile.

“Who can handle me?”

Before Namgung Hwang could shout, Beop Gye stepped forward.

“Amitabha. If you wish, Beop Gye can handle you.”

“You? Hahahaha!”

The Black Dragon King’s roar rang loudly.

The Black Dragon King, who burst into laughter as if he couldn’t contain it, looked at Beop Gye with eyes of scorn.

“Get out of my way! It’s like a child speaking when adults are in discussion. If you want to handle me, at least bring your abbot in!”

Beop Gye’s face turned red.

Who on earth would treat him, an elder of Shaolin, like this?

“Amitabha. Amitabha.”

Beop Gye tried to soothe the boiling anger by constantly chanting.

“If you want a worthy partner, I will take you. You aren’t trying to say the Emperor’s Sword, Namgung Hwang, isn’t an opponent enough for you, right?”

“Emperor’s Sword...”

The Black Dragon King’s lips twitched.

“You people who were comfortably grazing in the fence called the Namgung family sure act like thugs!”

“What?”

“Why? Did it hit the nail on your head?”

The Black Dragon King glared at everyone with wide eyes.

“Of course, I can take you all down by myself. But... if you think I am not enough on my own, it wouldn’t be a bad idea to meet your expectations.”

“...What?”

At that time, two people slowly walked to the side of the Black Dragon King.

“It’s definitely not bad.”

“If one is of the Nine Great Sects, it would be a shame to handle them alone.”

The face of Venerable Heo Do hardened as he saw it.

‘They are...’

Surely, he had never seen them with his own eyes.

But he could tell. There were really only a few people in the world who were qualified to stand next to the Black Dragon King. And as it happened, the appearance of one of them matched that of someone who has the qualification.

“...Desolate Gold Elder.”

“It is an honor. I cannot believe Venerable Heo Do of Wudang recognizes me.”

Desolate Gold Elder, standing to the left of the Black Dragon King, smiled coldly and looked down at Venerable Heo Do.

“Even if I take just the head of Venerable Heo Do, there is nothing left for burial. So is there any way not to come? A chance to gain this much doesn’t come easily.”

On the other hand, the person standing to the right of the Black Dragon King was a man with an ordinary face and nothing special.

But Venerable Heo Do knew if that person could be identified as Desolate Gold Elder, then the other one was also known.

A person whose identity could never be identified by appearance.

“Thousand Faced Scholar.”

“Thousand Faced Scholar? That man.”

Byeok Hyun’s face turned pale.

Desolate Gold Elder and Thousand Face Scholar.

They were the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Sects, gathered there now.

‘Three of the Five?’

It was a truly formidable force. Even if three of the Nine Sects and Five Great Families united, victory was not guaranteed.

“S-sect leader!”

Byeok Hyun looked at Venerable Heo Do without realizing it. But Venerable Heo Do was equally shocked by this unexpected situation.

‘Why are they gathered in one place?’

This shouldn’t happen. It had to be that way.

The Evil Factions gathered there didn’t just rush in without thought. There was one reason they went to war with the Yangtze River pirates.

Because Evil’s Factions never united.

Even during the war against the Demonic Sect in the past, the Evil Factions were at odds with each other until the end. The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families only took advantage and didn’t fight by stabbing each other in the back or gritting their teeth.

But didn’t the Evil Factions attack and growl at each other until the moment the Demonic Sect’s sword was stuck in their necks?

Suppose the Yangtze River pirates were attacked by a Justice Faction. In that case, they shouldn’t come to help but rush in like wild cats to prey on the area where the pirates’ influence had vanished, to be an Evil Faction in its true nature.

“Then how...”

At that moment.

A group of warriors appeared at the top of the cliff. They walked at a leisurely pace and stood at the edge, glaring down at those in the canyons with cold eyes.

At a glance, they were warriors who had reached their peak.

‘They even brought their subordinates with them.’

When they realized that their heads were occupied by warriors of such caliber, they felt an indescribable pressure.

But that was bearable.

What really took Heo Do’s breath away was the very situation in which they appeared. It was difficult to accept the reality that someone had dragged them into this dock.

“Who are you....”

There must be someone who brought these people together, people who could never come together like this.

“Who...”

It was just as Venerable Heo Do was about to yell.

Clank.

A small sound passed through the air.

A clear metallic sound, as if a woman’s jewelry were clinking together.

It was nothing more than a small sound that ordinary people couldn’t even hear from a distance, and that even Venerable Heo Do could only hear if he listened closely.

But if Venerable Heo Do’s ears were working, this small sound felt like thunder.

“Hmm.”

A slightly high-pitched snort came down from the cliff.

At that moment, Venerable Heo Do felt the hair on his entire body stand on end. With eyes wide open, he stared at the top of the cliff as if he were being consumed.

Euk.

A man appeared on top of the cliff.

Venerable Heo Do bit his lip tightly.

He saw it.

A scene where the Black Dragon King, Desolate Gold Elder, and Thousand Face Scholar made way for the man who appeared.

Who in the world could make them step back like that?

Step. Step.

The color of his form, which revealed itself on the edge of the cliff, was too vivid.

A blood-red robe with a Black Dragon engraved on it.

Pure white skin, which looked like a woman’s after being powdered.

A pure white crown atop an ebony head.

And countless accessories wrapped around his body and arms.

Venerable Heo Do knew the man. No, he had no choice but to know.

Although countless people were living in the world, it would be rare to find a man who behaved as such. Especially one who dared to stand there.

If there is just one.

“... Hegemony Lord.”

Jang Ilso.

The moment he appeared, the air in the canyon began to cool down.

So different.

The blade of the Black Dragon King was powerful enough to stop the battle there.

The coldness emanating from Desolate Gold’s body was reminiscent of ice, and the presence of the Thousand Face Scholar, who was both normal and odd, irritated the nerves.

However, Jang Ilso was different.

He didn’t exude a great presence; he simply walked slowly and looked down from a high place.

Still, they felt out of breath, as if the air was blocked.

It wasn’t his strength as a warrior, but his presence as a human being that was on a different level from others. Even his loud and ridiculous outfit didn’t make him look funny; instead, it made him unapproachable.

“It will be the first time greeting you, people of Kangho.”

Jang Ilso put both hands together and slowly extended them towards them. His waist bent naturally.

It was a very common thing. For those who live in Kangho, this bowing was as familiar as breathing.

Yet, even with that familiar gesture, everyone there felt goosebumps running through their bodies and a sense of suffocation, even with that common greeting.

“I am Jang Ilso, the leader of the Ten Thousand People Clan and the Four Precepts Alliance.”

A white smile appeared on Jang Ilso’s bright face.

At that moment, Venerable Heo Do could see.

Jang Ilso, who was smiling kindly, didn’t have the slightest hint of laughter in his eyes.

‘Snake? No... that is an imoogi.’

An imoogi with fangs coiled up on the large cliff, slowly looking down at the prey that had fallen into deviation.

With those cold and eerie eyes.

“Four Precepts Alliance?”

Namgung Hwang spoke with a stern expression.

“What do you mean by that?”

Jang Ilso nodded with a relaxed demeanor.

“It is a name that hadn’t existed before. But now, it will be a name everyone knows. The 18 River Families of the Yangtze River, Black Ghost Fort, Lower District Sect, and Ten Thousand People Clan will be under this name.”

“... the leaders of the Evil Faction formed an alliance?”

A hundred years ago, during the war against the Demonic Sect, the Evil Faction formed an alliance. However, it was a temporary alliance for survival, and even then, it was only a façade and not perfect.

Did it mean that their clans, who were at odds with each other, had come together under one alliance?

But there was no way to believe it.

It was imaginable for the sect leaders of the Nine Great Sects to gather in one place. No one would find it odd even if the Five Great Families came together.

‘But who would have imagined the sight of the Evil Faction uniting with each other?’

That dream-like event was now unfolding before their eyes.

“Huh!”

Namgung Hwang snorted. It seemed he didn’t want to show weakness to them.

“How can it be such a big deal for the people of the Evil Faction to come together!”

“Indeed.”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“It might not be such a big deal in the eyes of the family head. But since we did come together with a decision, wouldn’t you like to congratulate us?”

“...”

Namgung Hwang glared at him with eyes that seemed to want to devour him.

“Hmm. You don’t seem to want to do that. Too bad.”

But those eyes didn’t make Jang Ilso flinch at all.

Even the tone of his voice and gentle hand gestures spoke of composure.

As he looked at Jang Ilso like that, he began to feel anxious for some reason.

Venerable Heo Do took a glance behind.

‘Too narrow.’

When they entered the narrow valley, they expected it to be a natural fortress. However, as things turned out, getting out seemed more difficult than getting in.

‘Hell will break out as the ships start to get entangled with each other.’

But there was no way he could wait for his turn because the pirates would come to target them.

‘A retreat would have no meaning then.’

So, even if they had secured an escape route and entered the narrow place, nothing would have changed. A way out was meaningful only when it could be used.

Venerable Heo Do, who understood the situation, looked at Jang Ilso.

“Four Precepts Alliance... no matter what, unity is a good thing. Can I congratulate you?”

Jang Ilso nodded his head.

“Congratulations from the sect leader of Wudang. It is a great honor.”

“I am glad you are happy. But, Hegemony Lord.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes seemed to pierce Jang Ilso.

“You aren’t in the position to receive congratulations. If you do want to be congratulated, shouldn’t you come down here instead of standing there and looking down on us?”

“Hahahaha.”

Jang Ilso laughed loudly as he shook his head.

“Right, that is right. Yes.”

“...”

“That is the right thing to do. But what can I do? I am an Evil Faction person who doesn’t know etiquette. Wouldn’t it be funny if we showed that, though?”

“Could it be that the person called a lord of the Evil Faction is scared?”

“Hahaha. Saying such an obvious thing. Of course, I am scared.”

The lips of Jang Ilso twisted into a smile.

“Shaolin, Wudang, Namgung, and Qingcheng sects. How can we not be scared when it comes to dealing with them? Even now, my spine is trembling, and I am having a hard time standing. Hahahah!”

Venerable Heo Do’s face turned cold.

‘That guy...’

It was something he said to scratch the pride, but it didn’t work. Usually, a person who is new to a high position is expected to consider pride first, regardless of their natural inclinations.

But Jang Ilso didn’t feel any such thing.

Venerable Heo Do knew this well. This kind of person was the most difficult and tough to handle.

“Then...”

He glared at Jang Ilso with cold eyes.

“I guess you just came to greet us.”

His tone turned rougher. It was proof that his calmness was being shaken, and he was becoming anxious.

“Greet....”

Jang Ilso slowly touched his chin with his pale hand.

“Is this what people who invade other lands and wield a sword without permission would say?”

“This is the first time I have heard about needing permission to clear out pirates.”

“Haha. Right. You don’t need to ask permission. Right, true. Until now.”

There was a hint of arrogance in Jang Ilso’s eyes as he looked down on Venerable Heo Do.

“But now you will have to ask permission. For this, it is Jang Ilso and not someone else.”

Clench.

Venerable Heo Do changed.

Who had he heard this from before?

“Seek permission?”

He tightly clenched the sword at his waist.

“What if we don’t do that?”

“Hahaha! You ask such obvious things. Of course, you will have to pay the price, right?”

At that time, Namgung Hwang, who was listening, let out a loud shout, unable to endure it any longer.

“You bastard with a big mouth! If you are so confident, then come down and take my sword!”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso looked around with odd eyes and a strange look.

“It seems like everyone doesn’t have the slightest understanding of the situation they are in right now.”

And then he nodded.

“It could be possible. Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung with Qingcheng. With the strength of those four factions, no matter what force they face in the world, they wouldn’t be defeated. Moreover, it is tough to discuss the advantages of the locations of such high cliffs.”

There was no escape.

But no attack would reach the Evil Faction either.

Even if they shot arrows, rolled stones, or poured boiling oil on them, there was no way they would suffer significant damage since they were the best warriors of each sect.

‘Furthermore, there are remnants of the Black Dragon Pirates in here.’

Quite a few of them had died, but more than two-thirds were still alive. Ignoring them and launching a wide-scale attack shouldn’t be an option, even for the Evil Faction.

Ultimately, this meant they would also have to descend the cliff.

If they were to face them head-on, it would be worth confronting them, no matter how many there are. Since they must have gathered in a hurry, they couldn’t have brought many troops.

Jang Ilso also knew what the opponents were thinking.

“But...”

His white sneer twisted horribly.

“That is what you think when you look down at Jang Ilso. I think you need to understand your situation.”

For a moment, Venerable Heo Do’s heart turned cold.

Jang Ilso lightly flicked his finger.

Snap.

A sharp sound echoed through the valley.

‘...what?’

Venerable Heo Do looked at the top of the cliff with nervous eyes. It was clear that Jang Ilso sent some signal, but no movement could be seen. It was just silence.

‘Bluff?’

No, it cannot be...

It was then.

Rumble.

“...”

...a terrifying sound came as if something was cutting through the heart. It wasn’t a loud sound but one that could be clearly heard in the silent valley.

“What...”

Kwaaang!

And then the roar to bring down the sky erupted.

And the most unbelievable sight unfolded.

One side of the cliff they occupied exploded. Clouds of dust rose up, and fragments shot out like firecrackers.

Rumble!

Rumble! Rumble!

“That...!”

Even in the experienced world of Venerable Heo Do, he had never seen this.

Soon, one side of the cliff where the explosion occurred slowly fell away and began to tilt.

In an instant, his mind went blank.

Rumble!

This enormous rock cast a shadow over the heads of the Four Precepts and began to fall with increasing speed.

“Uh, uh... UACKKKK!”

“RUNNNNNNNN!”

The best of the best, who were not afraid of death, screamed at the top of their lungs and ran in despair.

These were people who were willing to risk their lives for their colleagues. But the moment they saw this humongous rock falling above them, their hands instinctively grabbed their sahyungs and pushed them.

It was a terrifying sight.

It was something beyond logic and common sense. Even Venerable Heo Do was overwhelmed by the sight and could only watch it.

“UGHHHHHHH!”

Surprisingly, it was Namgung Hwang who acted first.

Formidable sword Qi emerged from his sword. It was more than twice as large and intimidating as the Enhanced Sword that had destroyed the entire ship a moment ago. He roared at the top of his lungs and swung the sword.

Kwakwakwakwang!

The sword qi he unleashed struck the falling rock. It was indeed a formidable sword, and although it left scars as large as five feet deep, the large rock didn’t break or crack.

“Amitabha!”

Namgung Hwang didn’t give up and once again brought out all his internal qi and unleashed the sword.

“Amitabha!”

Beop Gye, too, seemed to know what had to be done and shouted.

This enormous power of the golden fist was added to the sword qi of Namgung Hwang and embedded in the rock.

Crumble!

Finally, the rock, which was the size of a shrine, broke into two. But that wasn’t enough.

“What are you doing! Break it! Crush it!”

As Beop Gye’s scream rang out, all those who came to their senses drew out their internal qi and attacked the rocks in unison.

“UGHHHHH!”

“ACKKKKKK!”

It was a sight of struggle.

The colorful rock was hit, split, and pulverized. The rock gradually split, cracked, and got crushed.

“More! More! Use all your strength! Or we all die here!”

“WAHHHH!”

The best of the Nine Great Sects, whose eyes were red with fury, attacked with all their might. Yet, removing the enormous rock was impossible from the start.

The rock, as large as a grand shrine, broke and cracked, forming hundreds of rocks the size of people.

Bang! Bang! Kwang! Kwang!

The rocks fell from afar, smashing loudly into the mud.

Kwang! Kwang!

One after another, the rocks continued to fall for a long time.

The world shook as the cliff deformed.

How can this place, where the screams of humans and the land mix, be explained to the other world as anything but hell?

Thud! Thuddd!

Finally, the last rock fell to the ground.

A thick layer of dust filled the air.

As if the world wouldn’t witness this terrible sight.

It was only when the wind blew from somewhere, pushing the dust away, that the miserable scene was revealed.

Elder Heo looked at the tragic sight before his eyes.

“Eukk....”

“M-My leg is... my... uhh....”

Those crushed by the rocks.

Those who left, shedding blood.

Blood that flowed down and was covered by the dust, and blood flowed endlessly.

Venerable Heo Do looked at the scene blankly and then turned his gaze to the cliff.

“HAHAHAHAH!”

“...”

“UHAHAHAHAHAHAH!”
