While everyone was climbing the cliff to save themselves, he stood below with confidence.

The contorted eyes of Beop Gye looked at Hae Yeon, and he noticed the gentle look on the disciple’s face.

“Amitabha. Elder, please head up. Leave the bottom to....”

“Ah, if you have time to talk, fight well, you bastard!”

“Ahoo! That bastard... ahh. Amitabha! Amitabha!”

Hae Yeon, who frowned for a moment, was startled and continued to chant. He rushed at the pirates as if he had nothing more to say.

The pirates holding the bows couldn’t fight properly and were swept away.

‘Did it!’

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone again.

Of course, the numbers below are so high that one person cannot handle them all at once.

But just drawing their attention is effective. Hasn’t the momentum of arrows flying to the cliff already decreased?

‘A bit more...’

“AHOOO! So fucking frustrating that I might die! What are you looking at?”

At that moment, Chung Myung, who was clinging to the cliff, shouted.

“Leave the disciples behind and jump up first! Be it Shaolin, Wudang, or Namgung! I have to fucking feed things into your mouth!”

“That damned bastard!”

The first person to respond to those words was none other than Namgung Hwang.

“Fine! Here I come!”

He soared onto the cliff in one jump. Namgung Hwang, using the Wudang disciples, moved and took the help of Venerable Heo Do’s waist, rising up like a dragon ascending a cliff.

“Tsk!”

Venerable Heo Do gritted his teeth.

Chung Myung’s words were right. If they just blocked it there, they would lose strength and die. Even if they did take damage, they had to occupy the top of the cliff.

“Those who can follow me, follow!”

“Yes!”

Some of the elders retrieved their swords and joined Venerable Heo Do.

“OHH!”

Venerable Heo kicked off the cliff with an odd spirit. And he soared up and up.

“You should have done this a long time ago, morons!”

Pat!

Chung Myung ran up the cliff. The place where he was moving to was the place Jang Ilso was standing.

“I am pointing right at it, you bastard!”

Chung Myung’s loud voice spread over the cliff and into the sky.

Oil rained from the sky, and arrows shot out endlessly from the ground.

The qi from Shaolin monks moving up and down the cliff was like a golden dragon, and the Sword Shield drawn by Wudang covered the cliff in white.

‘What is this?’

Bake Cheon’s eyes trembled.

Even though they were in the middle of a battle, he had to calm his mind. Yet, anyone who witnessed this scene would understand why Baek Cheon was distracted.

Not just Baek Cheon and Mount Hua’s disciples, but even the warriors of the Ten Thousand People Clan who were facing him couldn’t take their eyes off it.

A sight that made “overwhelming” feel like an understatement.

Strength and stratagem.

Justice and Evil.

Everything that constituted the battlefield of Kangho clashed fiercely there. As was said in Kangho, there was no law or agreement there.

The only thing that existed was a terrible intent to kill another person and a strong desire to survive at all costs.

The smell of boiling oil and gunpowder pierced the nose, adding to a thick stench that defied explanation.

‘This is a battle...!’

It felt like something sticky and heavy was in his body. He couldn’t breathe properly and felt dazed the entire time as if a fog clouded his mind.

Baek Cheon, who had followed Chung Myung to various battles, knew that his skills might be first-rate, but his experience wasn’t.

But this was different from any battlefield he had experienced.

The scale of the clashing forces was different, and the will behind the conflict was different. Above all, the level of malevolence directed at each other was on a different level.

“Go up! Climb!”

Shaolin and Wudang disciples, with their eyes red and gritted teeth, climbed up. Even as the boiling oil touched and grazed their skin, they continued to move upward.

And the Evil Faction members, who saw that scene, began to pour more oil.

No, it wasn’t just oil anymore.

Seeing the disciples rise higher and higher, they grew impatient. They threw rocks without even being ordered, pouring all their qi into the pile of stones they moved.

Puaak!

The stone that the sword couldn’t completely cut struck a monk who was climbing the cliff.

His head was injured, with blood flowing down. The monk, who stumbled once, gritted his teeth and glared up.

“You... demons!”

His face was close to a demon’s. He grabbed a stone sticking out of the cliff and climbed up. There was no mercy or gentleness in his face anymore. It was just filled with malice towards the other person.

‘This is a real battle!’

When people’s lives were at stake, their true nature came out.

This battlefield was a place where human nature was shown for what it was. And here, there was no sign of the degree or agreement they had been shouting loudly about.

‘No, no!’

Not yet. This could not be the true battlefield of warriors.

“Sasuk!”

“Right.”

Yoon Jong seemed to feel that way, too, and shouted in an angry tone.

How many times had they not seen this far? This was just the start. When the true battle of warriors began...

“They are moving!”

With Jo Gul’s shout, Namgung Hwang and Venerable Heo Do began to climb up the cliff at a blinding speed. A step behind, Beop Gye also led the elders of Shaolin and jumped up the cliff like a dragon ascending to heaven.

Baek Cheon looked far above and searched for one person with his eyes.

“I will handle it all! You bastards!”

With a loud voice, Chung Myung rushed down the cliff like a snake. He was finally making his move.

‘This is it!’

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth.

Until then, it had been closer to a war than a battlefield of warriors. However, this was the most decisive factor that differentiated both.

An absolute warrior.

Absolute warriors who could turn the battlefield’s flow with a single sword or change the land with a single slash.

Their movements determined the direction of the battle.

“Chung Myung!”

Baek Cheon watched Chung Myung sprint along the cliff, while the Ten Thousand People Clan aimed for him from below.

As soon as he saw this, Baek Cheon’s mouth moved before his mind could catch up.

“Sasuk! Senior Sasuk!”

“Yes!”

“We’ll handle it here!”

Before Un Geom and Hyun Sang could fully respond, Baek Cheon swung his sword sharply at the Ten Thousand People Clan in front of him and roared.

“Yiseol! Yoon Jong! Jo Gul!”

“Yes.”

“Yes, Sasuk!”

“I’m ready!”

“M-Me!”

Baek Cheon responded to Tang Soso’s urgent call without delay.

“Soso, support our warriors! We can’t let a single person die!”

“Yes!”

“Let’s go! Chung Myung must be protected!”

“Yes!”

Without hesitation, Baek Cheon and four others leaped down the cliff.

Baek Cheon descended a few steps and touched his toes against the cliff.

Chikkk!

The moment his speed slowed down a bit!

Tat!

Baek Cheon kicked off the cliff, rushing ahead at lightning speed.

“S-Sahyung!”

“You bastards, this is what Mount Hua is!”

At the sight of the Five Swords running in perfect order, cheers erupted one after another from the Mount Hua disciples.

According to the natural order, many people could be superior to them. But when it came to navigating a cliff, everything changed. Which sect in the world would train to walk on a cliff?

“This cliff is nothing compared to Mount Hua!”

Baek Cheon soon ran past the slippery cliff as if it were flat land, chasing after Chung Myung.

He saw the warriors of the Ten Thousand People Clan moving towards Chung Myung, who was already engaged with another person. It was too late for him to intervene directly.

But there was nothing to worry about. Because there was one person here who possessed footwork techniques incomparable to his own.

“Samae!”

“Yes.”

At that moment, Yu Yiseol, who was running behind Baek Cheon, climbed up the cliff. Baek Cheon maintained his running speed and held the sword ahead.

Yu Yiseol rotated her body and fell onto Baek Cheon’s sword. The moment her foot touched the surface of the sword, Baek Cheon swung the sword at once and quickly propelled Yu Yiseol’s body.

Swish!

The surging Yu Yiseol’s body moved like a bird flying off the cliff.

“Die!”

Meanwhile, the Ten Thousand People Clan warriors, who had almost reached Chung Myung, swung their blades with all their might. They were determined to somehow attack Chung Myung and make him fall!

But, rather than even turning to look at them, Chung Myung only rushed ahead.

“You!”

It was the moment when feeling ignored, they pushed even harder into the province.

Paaat!

Yu Yiseol flew in at once and spun around with her sword unleashed. The bright red sword qi that erupted from the tip of the sword swept away the warriors aiming for Chung Myung in an instant.

“Oh!”

The sword qi, which suddenly flew in, frightened the Ten Thousand People Clan and slashed at their bodies. One person did succeed in blocking the flying sword qi with a hastily raised sword, but Chung Myung had already run far ahead of them.

“This woman!”

The eyes of the enemies were all red as they swung their swords at Yu Yiseol. Still, after hitting the cliff a couple of times, she instantly rose above their heads and shot forward again.

“W-what?”

The Ten Thousand People Clan were all wide-eyed.

‘On this cliff?’

Kicking off the ground and jumping over the heads of people wasn’t a big deal. It was acceptable to admit that she moved at a speed invisible to the naked eye. Still, in any case, it would be more difficult to find someone among those gathered here who couldn’t show such a level of footwork.

But wasn’t this place on a cliff?

Kakakakang!

Falling without any gain, they urgently drove a blade into the cliff. Somehow, they succeeded in standing up, but by that time, Chung Myung and Yu Yiseol had already distanced themselves.

“Cha-chase...”

“Who are you chasing, you damn bastard!”

At that moment, Yoon Jong and Jo Gul, who had followed them from the cliff, rushed at them with frightening momentum.

The Ten Thousand People Clan warriors was shocked.

‘No, NO....’

Slash!

There was no way they could properly respond to the attack coming from behind, as they barely managed to stay upright by sticking their swords into the cliff. Those in the front were instantly pierced and fell to the ground, splashing blood, while those in the back gave up fighting and threw themselves down.

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul, who had cleared out a dozen warriors in an instant, had already passed by and shouted at Baek Cheon, who was running ahead.

“I will follow right after you!”

“Okay!”

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth and continued to run. In his eyes, the image of Chung Myung running up the cliff and Yu Yiseol running on top of it as if guarding Chung Myung came into view.

“Don’t let Chung Myung stop!”

As if in response to the shouts, power surged from below and flew straight to the top of Chung Myung’s head.

Hae Yeon was also perfectly assisting in moving.

“T-that...”

The disciples of the Nine Great Sects, who gritted their teeth, climbed the cliff and couldn’t hide their astonishment at the sight.

They had heard of the reputation of Mount Hua’s Five Swords. However, they thought they were only second and third-class disciples.

It was ridiculous that these people could just run along the cliff as if it was flat and easily jump over the rocks like flying squirrels, but to think they did such tricks on the cliff?

How was it possible unless they had ten lives?

Jin Hyun gritted his teeth.

‘Damn it, Righteous Sword of Hua...!’

When he first saw them in the Martial Arts Tournament, the difference between him and Baek Cheon wasn’t so significant.

But now, he couldn’t even dream of imitating the way Baek Cheon was moving. At this moment, he was barely crawling up the cliff, while Baek Cheon was soaring like a bird.

‘What could possibly cause such a difference?’

His eyes were red with frustration.

Yet the anger he felt was nothing compared to what Namgung Do-wei was experiencing.

“This...”

Namgung Do-wei, watching Chung Myung from below, felt his blood boiling, unaware that his clenched lips were bleeding.

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

Chung Myung was truly clinging to the cliff like a Divine Dragon. But instead of splitting the mountain with his Great Sword, he was struggling and groaning?

“Ugh!”

In a fit of rage, Namgung Do-wei began climbing the cliff with intense movements.

“Do-wei, calm down!”

“It is dangerous!”

The elders of the family shouted urgently, but Namgung Do-wei wanted to argue back loudly.

‘Dangerous?’

Was it not dangerous for them?

The warriors and disciples of another small sect, which couldn’t compare to the Namgung family, were now dominating the battle. How could he remain silent and worry about his own safety?

“The sword troops of the Namgung sect will follow! I will protect the lord!”

“Yes!”

The young warriors of the Namgung family responded with loud cheers. Although they didn’t voice it, their eyes were also burning with fighting spirit. It was clear they shared Namgung Do-wei’s determination.

“We will never lose to Mount Hua! Be prepared to take risks and speed up!”

As the Wudang and Namgung family increased their pace, the two groups began to entangle with one another.

And then.

Taat!

“Ohhhh!”

Namgung Hwang kicked off the cliff and rose like a dragon.

‘Young bastards!’

The activities of Mount Hua were clearly visible to his eyes.

People who were less than half his age were risking their lives, and it was impossible for him, a member of a family clan, to prioritize his safety.

“Jang Ilsoo!”

Namgung Hwang, who struck down the pot of oil pouring onto his head with a single sword slash, leaped above the cliff.

“You little bastard!”

The cliff, which seemed to touch the sky, was suddenly covered in a shadow.

It was like a black sun had risen.

‘B-Black Dragon...’

“Take this!”

The Black Dragon King’s scimitar was swung in an instant, and the black-colored blade qi came like a waterfall.

Kwaaaak!

The Black Dragon King’s blade qi poured down the cliff like the spear of a god, flying straight for Namgung Hwang.

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were filled with shock.

An enormous amount of blade qi was pouring down over their heads. It was as if the entire field of vision was turning black.

“Tsk!”

Namgung Hwang leaped towards the blade qi, flying at him with a strike like lightning.

“I am Namgung Hwang!”

Namgung Hwang’s sword, accompanied by a mighty roar, cut through the blade qi in front of him. A sword as white as the sun sliced through the black darkness.

“Ahhhh!”

Chaakkk!

The sight of Namgung Hwang, surrounded by white sword qi, soaring through the enemy’s blade qi made the hearts of those watching him boil with excitement.

But.

“Hey!”

As the Black Dragon King’s scimitar descended once again, the blade qi, now with even more force, began to gush out.

Chak!

“Ahhhh!”

Namgung Hwang charged like a lion and split the blade qi.

The blade qi clearly demonstrated why the Black Dragon King was called the God of the Yangtze River. Still, Namgung Hwang proved his strength, earning his title as the Emperor’s Sword.

Unfortunately, this wasn’t a battle to prove an individual’s skills. That point divided the joy and sorrow between the two.

Kwaaaakk!

The Black Dragon King’s blade qi, which was split to the left and right by Namgung Hwang’s sword, rained down below without losing force.

“A-avoid it!”

“ACKKKK!”

Suddenly, the blade qi rained down on their heads, and the Shaolin and Wudang disciples flew in all directions, their faces turning pale.

But this place was on a cliff. Attacks that could have been easily avoided on flat ground were much harder to dodge here.

“ACKKK!”

“ACkkkkkk!”

Those who were swept away by the blade qi, unable to avoid it, fell helplessly, bleeding.

“Jin Heo!”

“Hae Myung! Hae Myung!”

Shaolin and Wudang.

The sects were recognized all over the world just by their names alone.

But even for them, it wasn’t easy to handle the qi of the Black Dragon King, a leader in the Shenzhou Five Sects. People climbing the cliff fell, with blood spilling all around.

What even the pouring oil and rain of arrows couldn’t accomplish, the blade qi of the Black Dragon King achieved.

“This!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes shone.

“Black Dragon Kingggg!”

“Kuahahahaha!”

The Black Dragon King laughed loudly.

“It was worth seeing your expression, Emperor’s Sword!”

Namgung Hwang clung to the cliff again and looked down, biting his lip hard enough to bleed. Not just Shaolin and Wudang, but even the Namgung family suffered because of what he did.

When he saw the people groaning down the cliff, his stomach turned hot with anger.

But Namgung Hwang couldn’t vent his anger either.

The Evil Faction.

The Yangtze River 18 Families.

It was difficult to say that the pirates he had been dealing with so far were strong, and those weren’t empty words.

The level of their opponents wasn’t comparable to that of the Namgung family. The warriors of the pirates weren’t even strong enough to match against the elders of the Namgung family. They were inferior to the normal elders.

And so, it was true that he developed a feeling of disdain for them.

But the Black Dragon King’s blade qi was enough to shatter the pride of Namgung Hwang in one swipe.

‘Black Dragon King.’

The King of the Yangtze River, a God.

He was of a different class. At least he was completely different from the other pirates.

Namgung Hwang’s sword skills could cut through the mountain with one slash and cut through the river. But only the Black Dragon King’s blade, qi, couldn’t be cut down.

‘How?’

How could such a person emerge from among those crude pirates?

A Justice Faction nurtured all of its affiliated clans, but in a sect, people with different standards stepped in. This was the moment when one painfully realized what that meant.

“Lord Namgung!”

At that moment, Venerable Heo Do shouted loudly and passed by Namgung Hwang. Thanks to that, Namgung Hwang, who had flinched, suddenly came to his senses and gritted his teeth again.

‘Does this mean I was scared?’

The moment he saw Venerable Heo Do passing by, the blood in his body surged high.

“HEO DOOOOO!”

Namgung Hwang shouted once again and climbed up the cliff.

Beop Gye quickly followed right next to him.

Venerable Heo Do, Namgung Hwang, and Beop Gye.

Absolute warriors representing the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families climbed up the cliff in one movement to sever the heads of the leaders of their enemies.

“HAHAHAHAHAH! You little brats!”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes filled with fire.

“If you want, I will deal with all of you!”

The Black Dragon King kicked the ground and flew down the cliff. The sight of the massive human falling from the sky felt more intimidating than could be imagined.

“How dare you!”

At the moment when Venerable Heo Do gritted his teeth and was about to slash his sword.

“I will be your opponent!”

A sharp sword slash moved to the neck of Venerable Heo Do, along with a cold and creepy voice.

Kakakng!

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone as he gently swung the sword and pushed the sword away.

“Desolate Gold Elder!”

The Desolate Gold Elder, who had come down the cliff, stood perpendicular to the cliff as if his feet were glued to the ground and looked at Venerable Heo Do.

“If it is Venerable Heo Do of Wudang, it would be worth risking my life for. No matter how I think about it, this is a profitable business.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes darkened.

A man without blood or tears.

A merchant from hell who dealt with everything.

A peddler walking through the darkest places.

There were so many words to describe this man, but there was just one name that Venerable Heo Do could think of.

“First Sword of the Evil Faction.”

“All a façade. Not worth a penny.”

Venerable Heo Do adjusted the sword and held it.

‘Damn it.’

And then he glanced left and right.

In the aftermath of the fierce battle and thanks to Chung Myung’s actions, most of the flames were extinguished, but those that hadn’t been extinguished were burning at the bottom of the cliff.

There was no way to take a step back.

The qi flowing from Desolate Gold Elder was telling. The moment he turned his back, his spine would be severed. An eerie killing intent and sticky force grabbed hold of his ankles and wouldn’t let go.

No matter how skilled he was as part of Wudang, he must risk his life if the opponent was the Desolate Gold Elder, the First Sword of the Evil Faction.

“Wudang’s Heo Do! I will take your life!”

“Nice! Let us have a go.”

The two of them ran down the cliff as if it were flat land and collided.

Kwaaang!

Through the qi waves of Black Dragon King’s scimitar and Namgung Hwang’s sword collision, Beop Gye soared into the sky. His elegant steps actually surpassed those of Venerable Heo Do and Namgung Hwang.

‘We must occupy the top part! I cannot make any more sacrifices!’

It was at that moment when Beop Gye gritted his teeth and soared up.

“It is the time of Shaolin. A true masterpiece.”

At that moment, dozens of hand shadows appeared above his head.

For a moment, Beop Gye was shocked.

It wasn’t a big deal to release spear attacks at the same time. Even if he went against Wudang’s first disciple, he could handle as much.

But if such forms of shadows all took different forms, the story was different.

“Ha!”

Beop Gye waved his hand in the air. At the same time, dozens of arms appeared from his fingertips like the hands of a Thousand-Armed Buddha.

Kwaaang!

The two forces intertwined and collided.

Beop Gye, unable to overcome the aftermath, retreated a couple of steps.

“....”

A light of astonishment appeared in the eyes of Beop Gye, who saw a person leisurely walking down the cliff with his back turned.

“But when it comes to footwork, I am not too far behind.”

“... Thousand Faced Scholar.”

The clan leader of the Lower District sect, the Thousand Faced Scholar, blocked his path.

Beop Gye massaged his hands with a stern look.

The Fist of Shaolin was known to be the best in the world. Needless to say, the abbot of Shaolin was the best. But the aftermath of the collision moments earlier left pain in his wrist.

“...the famous Thousand Faced Scholar?”

“An honor. It is such an honor. Beop Gye of Shaolin recognizes my skills.”

The Thousand Faced Scholar giggled as if he was having fun.

“But it seems that the Thousand Arms Buddha Shaolin’s technique wasn’t as good as I thought. I am not sure if it lacks the skill or understanding or is weak.”

“... Amitabha.”

The eyes of Beop Gye darkened.

“You will find out it is neither, lord.”

“Hahahaha. So, you people have a passion for winning, too? Then come at me!”

The Thousand Faced Scholar lightly jumped up and spread his arms wide.

And then Beop Gye rose with a stern look on his face.

Shadow and palm arts.

As the shadows of human hands covered the blue sky above the cliff, a black shadow was cast onto the cliff.

The absolute warriors representing each sect were facing their enemies on the steep cliff.

Kwaaang!

Kwaaang!

The top of the cliff soon began to be covered with large clashes and explosions.

“That...”

“What is that...”

Everyone who was climbing the cliff opened their mouths, watching the sight.

They had heard a lot of stories about the skilled warriors. Still, no one had ever seen with their own eyes the sight of such warriors fighting sincerely and doing their best.

That had to be it.

Kangho has enjoyed peace for over a hundred years. It had been well over a hundred years since the Evil and Justice Factions had been at war.

The best warriors represented the honor of each sect. These people had so much to lose in one defeat. Therefore, it wasn’t easy for them to clash.

But it was different now.

In order to survive and protect each other’s sects, they had to kill the enemy.

Grrrrng!

Enhanced sword and enhanced blade collided, and Palm arts and Fist arts clashed.

In the aftermath of the collision between the two, the entire cliff shook as if it would collapse at any moment, even though the explosives hadn’t detonated.

“That... the realm of absolute warriors.”

A laugh escaped from the mouth of Mu Jin, the first class disciple of Wudang.

The moment he witnessed it with his own eyes, the sight of the best warriors colliding in the air, his feelings of inadequacy washed over him like a wave.

‘So the sect leader was that strong?’

When the swords collided, they expelled qi waves that dug into the cliffs, leaving scars. A scene that seemed impossible for him to create even with all his might was being etched across the length and breadth of it simply with their spare qi.

Venerable Heo Do’s sword was astounding.

However, the sword of the Desolate Gold elder was not inferior to that of Venerable Heo Do.

The same was true for the others.

It was a historic clash of sorts.

The battle between the Black Dragon King and Namgung Hwang and the spar between the monk and the Thousand Faced Scholar were all close battles with no room for error.

Grrrr!

The cliff shook as if it would collapse at any moment, and fragments began to fall like a landslide.

“Hold on tight!”

“I am falling!”

Mu Jin gritted his teeth and clung to the cliff.

Breaking through the path and heading up? Not ten, even a thousand lives would be enough!

‘What do we do in this situation....’

And then it happened.

“Haha!”

A loud laugh, which didn’t suit the situation at all, caught Mu Jin’s attention.

Mu Jin turned his head and saw it.

Chung Myung, and the Five Swords surrounding him, like guards, ran to the cliff towards Jang Ilso at once.

“Did you clean that damn neck well? You bastard!”

Mu Jin’s mouth opened wide in shock.

While everyone’s eyes were frozen to the bones at the sight of the best warriors in the sect colliding, only one group, the Five Swords of Mount Hua, responded with fluidity.

As if they already belonged in that place.

“Mount... Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

At the same time as Mu Jin shouted without realizing it, Chung Myung rose from the cliff.

“Jang Ilso!”

Chung Myung’s sword flew like a flash and struck Jang Ilso’s head like lightning.

“Haha!”

Jang Ilso burst into laughter and raised his hand to block Chung Myung’s sword.

Kwaaaaang!

The rings on each finger collided with Chung Myung’s sword, creating a loud roar.

Chung Myung’s face contorted.

And Jang Ilso’s face wore a sneer.

The eyes of the two clashed along with their swords and hands.

Kaak! Kakakaka!

The sword, infused with internal qi and Jang Ilso’s ring rubbed against each other, creating a goosebumps-inducing sound.

When Chung Myung glared at him with eyes contorted in murderous intent, Jang Ilso responded with a sneer.

“Such a pity.”

“...”

Crack!

Jang Ilso grabbed Chung Myung’s sword and swung it away. Chung Myung spun in the air and landed on a cliff.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso shook off the hand that had blocked the black sword and looked down at Chung Myung.

“Do you know?”

“... what?”

“There is nothing as disgusting as an actor who doesn’t leave the stage when his role is over.”

Jang Ilso twisted his red lips.

“Your role would have been completed. Then why do you stay back being so ugly?”

“Role?”

Chung Myung looked at Jang Ilso and smiled. Their smiles seemed strangely similar, even though they were different. Above all, those eyes were condescending.

“Ah, I know very well. What is my role? It is my role to cut off your head and throw it to the dogs.”

“Oh my.”

Jang Ilso shook his head as if he couldn’t stop Chung Myung.

“You are such a bad actor. Very bad actor. You don’t even know what your role is. An actor like that not only ruins himself but also ruins the stage.”

Jang Ilso slowly stepped forward.

“It is heartbreaking, but an actor like that has to be pulled off the stage, by force at least.”

“Ahh. Is that so?”

Chung Myung turned the sword and held it again, revealing his teeth. His expression was creepy as if he was smiling or snarling, and he said,

“My personality has improved a lot, you know.”

“....”

“I am watching a little useless bastard like you act out. If it was in the past, I would have stuck the sword down your throat before you even spoke.”

“Hahahahahaha!”

Jang Ilso burst into laughter.

Actually, this was an odd sight.

Below the cliff where they stood, a thrilling battle was unfolding. The disciples of the Justice Faction were groaning, dodging falling rocks, and clinging to the cliff.

It was a scene where the fate of Kangho was likely being twisted.

However, these two people were leisurely chatting as if none of this concerned them. It was as if they were the only people in the world.

“Nice. Really nice. Did you know, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon? No, Chung Myung of Mount Hua. I really like you.”

“...”

“Which is why it is so disappointing. Truly. So much. The people I like don’t really like me. Of course, you feel the same, right?”

“Speaking of it will hurt my mouth.”

“Right, I guess so. Unfortunately. And so... there is nothing you can do.”

Jang Ilso covered his face with a large white hand. It was a gesture as if he didn’t want to show Chung Myung his expression.

But the crazy look in his eyes, peeking through his fingers, gave away what kind of expression Jang Ilso was making. A low murmur escaped from the lips beneath the palm.

“I have no choice but to rip off your neck and kill you.”

Chung Myung chuckled at those words.

“I agree with the last part. Except for that, everything else is the opposite. I hate you that much.”

Chung Myung extended his sword straight and aimed it at Jang Ilso.

“So, I will kill you right now, you damned bastard!”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso lowered his hand. He suddenly returned a neat smile to his face and tilted his head.

“So strange. You are a very smart kid. Excessively, sometimes creepily smart. You probably know a guy like you can’t defeat me on your own.”

Kakakak!

The ring tightly fitted on Jang Ilso’s hand rubbed against the sword, making an eerie sound.

“But why do you insist on inviting death?”

“You speak like an idiot.”

Chung Myung chuckled and looked at Jang Ilso with sharp eyes.

“You know the answer, right?”

Chung Myung’s cold eyes met Jang Ilso’s soft gaze, creating a tense atmosphere.

“Yes, I know. I know so well.”

Jang Ilso nodded.

“I asked such a stupid question. I was foolish. That is what it means to rise. It isn’t about defeating an opponent you can beat; it is about bringing down an opponent you cannot beat. Even if it means biting their ankles, sticking poisoned daggers, or pouring oil onto them.”

An eerie voice emerged from his lips.

“That is how one goes up. There is no other way but that.”

“You know me so well.”

Chung Myung thought while smiling.

This was why he hated Jang Ilso so much.

He was so easy to talk to, yet at the same time, incomprehensible. It would be even more amazing if one could like someone with both qualities in them.

“It wouldn’t be a bad idea for me to be your mountain. Welcome. Let’s have fun.”

“First.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung held up two fingers.

“Let me correct two things.”

“...”

“First. A mountain? Don’t speak so arrogantly. There’s no way a bastard like you can be a mountain in my path.”

“Hahaha. Haha. Right, right!”

“And second.”

Chung Myung smiled.

“Who says I am alone?”

“...”

At that moment, four people leaped from under the cliff simultaneously.

Tak!

Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, and Jo Gul positioned themselves beside Chung Myung without delay, exuding killing intent toward Jang Ilso.

Crack. Crack.

Chung Myung cracked his neck on both sides.

“It might be hard for you to understand, but the fights I have been fighting so far have been my fights. They have nothing to do with them.”

“...”

“But if the other person is you, if I try to take you down alone, these bastards will come for my neck.”

“That won’t happen.”

“I will kill him.”

“... cut that arm too.”

“I will drink your blood.”

Jang Ilso burst into laughter as he saw the disciples of Mount Hua gritting their teeth at him.

“Still, I thought you were a tiger cub, but now that I see you, you are just another bastard. Doesn’t it seem like courage that comes from being in a group of dogs?”

Chung Myung chuckled, his eyes shining.

“No matter how many dogs gather together, they are still dogs. Even if you struggle, you won’t turn into a wolf.”

It was a mockery aimed at them, but instead of getting angry, Chung Myung laughed again after hearing those words.

“That isn’t bad either.”

“Hmm?”

On the other hand, Jang Ilso frowned. Chung Myung smiled, baring his teeth.

“... is there any other death that suits you better than being bitten by a dog?”

“...”

Jo Gul, who was listening next to him, chuckled and said.

“It will be a dog’s death.”

“And even that is a luxury.”

“I will cut off his head.”

A strange expression flashed across Jang Ilso’s face as he saw the Five Swords talking so loudly in front of him, the Hegemony Lord.

‘These people are strange.’

Jang Ilso’s presence came not just from his strength. No matter how famous they were, if one met him, they would inevitably shudder.

But, in the face of Jang Ilso and receiving his hostility, they didn’t even flinch.

A bluff?

No, it wasn’t a bluff, but even if it was, it was amazing.

The bravado of sticking one’s head into the tiger’s mouth wasn’t something anyone could pull off.

“Surely, it is a unique sect. Mount Hua...”

Jang Ilso glanced down at the cliff. Now time had dragged on enough.

“Then cut off my head. Don’t just speak with your mouth.”

“I was going to do that anyway!”

Chung Myung stepped ahead.

“Sasuk! Sago!”

“Yes!”

“Be careful. It is an instant death!”

“Right!”

“Sahyungs!”

“Uh!”

“Don’t jump in just because you see a gap! That is a snake with ten heads. If you jump in because you see a gap, you will get bitten by those fangs!”

“Okay!”

Even when dealing with the high priest, Chung Myung had never given them such instructions. In other words, this meant that Chung Myung was evaluating Jang Ilso as being above the high priest.

This implied that it was a battle that couldn’t guarantee victory even if he led them, and this was someone who had grown stronger than before.

The bodies of the Five Swords felt tense.

Hegemony Lord Jang Ilso. That exalted name was now before them. Not far above, but within reach of their swords!

“Here I come! Bite and kill me!”

Kwang!

The Five Swords kicked off the ground simultaneously. The mad dogs of Mount Hua charged toward the tiger before them.

To the wild beast that defied common sense, and for which it seemed more fitting to call a monster than a tiger. Without a single hesitation!

The sword of Chung Myung, which struck first, flew in a straight line toward Jang Ilso.

A sword that flashed like lightning. A sword without any finesse. Therefore, it was faster and swifter than any other sword.

The sword flew at incredible speed, aiming to cut off Jang Ilso’s head.

Kakang!

Jang Ilso, swinging his sword at once, struck down the flying sword, smiled brightly, and aimed for Chung Myung’s head. But instead of blocking or retreating, Chung Myung moved even closer into Jang Ilso’s arms.

At that moment, two swords came between Chung Myung’s head and Jang Ilso’s hands.

Kwaang!

Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol’s swords blocked Jang Ilso’s hand, which was targeting Chung Myung’s head.

Chung Myung leaped into Jang Ilso’s arms without the slightest hesitation, as if anticipating this, and unleashed his sword at Jang Ilso’s chest.

Pat!

Sword qi began to pour out like a shower.

“Hmm?”

Jang Ilso let out a small exclamation and leaned his body slightly backward.

Kakakakakang!

In the blink of an eye, Jang Ilso’s hand and Chung Myung’s sword clashed a dozen times in the air. Even Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol couldn’t see the flashes with their own eyes; all they could perceive was something gray moving.

Pat!

Paat!

The hem of Jang Ilso’s shoulder was cut off, and simultaneously, a handful of flesh was torn from Chung Myung’s side.

But at that moment, Yoon Jong and Jo Gul, who followed behind Chung Myung, thrust their swords under both of Chung Myung’s arms.

Jo Gul’s swift sword unfolded dozens of Sword Qi, while Yoon Jong’s sharp sword met with Jang Ilso’s hand aimed at Chung Myung.

Pat!

Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol didn’t wait and watch.

Although they didn’t even exchange glances, they moved simultaneously as one body.

Baek Cheon lowered his form and rushed down as if crawling on the floor, while Yu Yiseol jumped over Chung Myung’s head and quickly flew toward Jang Ilso.

Below and above.

Red Plum Blossoms spread like blood drops and swept over the body of Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso’s eyes changed entirely.

“Trivial!”

A dark blue qi appeared in both hands and shot up like a flame.

Paaat!

The plum blossom sword qi that flew in collided with Jang Ilso’s qi and shattered instantly. Jang Ilso’s eyes followed Yu Yiseol, who jumped in the air, like a hawk.

Just as Jang Ilso’s hand was about to swing, Chung Myung jumped to the floor and kicked Jang Ilso in the head with his knee.

Kwang!

Jang Ilso’s wrist and Chung Myung’s knee, which quickly blocked the attack, collided. Jang Ilso swiftly twisted his wrist and thrust ten qi movements at Chung Myung.

Kwang! Bang! Kwang!

Jang Ilso’s qi and Chung Myung’s sword constantly clashed. Every time a clash occurred, Chung Myung’s body was pushed back, helplessly.

At that moment, Jo Gul and Yoon Jong grabbed Chung Myung’s back and planted their feet firmly on the ground.

Kwang!

Tat!

The sword of Chung Myung stuck on the floor, was dragged back, leaving a scar like a worm on the ground. However, he managed to hold on without falling off the cliff.

At that moment, Yu Yiseol landed in front of Chung Myung and displayed her fierceness towards Jang Ilso, like a poisonous wild cat. Simultaneously, Baek Cheon flew right behind him to protect Chung Myung, aiming his sword at Jang Ilso.

“... Ha.”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

Drrr.

A trickle of blood flowed down his white cheek.

Jang Ilso lifted his finger to wipe the blood from his face and traced it to his lips.

“So...”

Blood-red lips, no, Jang Ilso’s lips, literally stained in blood, opened with his white teeth revealed.

“Amazing, Mount Hua.”

At that moment, a mountain-like pressure began to weigh on the Five Swords’ shoulders.

Before he knew it, Jo Gul’s back was covered in cold sweat.

Jo Gul, who had never once lost his spirit, even when dealing with the high priest of the Demonic Sect, now had an unprecedented expression on his face.

The fingertips holding the sword trembled.

Without realizing it, his tightly closed lips turned white.

It was as if a giant tiger was snarling with its mouth wide open in front of his face. The moment that mouth closed, it felt hard to breathe due to the pressure, as if his throat would be torn open and blood would rush out.

“Kuaak...”

He took a short, deep breath and gripped the sword tightly as if to prepare himself.

‘This is Jang Ilso...’

A venomous snake?

That wasn’t a way to describe him. That thing had to be an Imoogi, or rather, it might be something called an evil dragon.

And it wasn’t just Jo Gul who felt that way.

‘He is strong.’

Baek Cheon also watched Jang Ilso with eyes that were clearly tense.

‘High priest? No... more than that.’

Of course, the high priest was incredibly strong. It was difficult to deny that their win against the high priest at that time was just because of luck.

But it became clear after one clash: Jang Ilso was a lot stronger than the high priest.

The high priest was, to speak, a natural disaster.

He was a man who seemed ready to sweep away everything in the world with his unbelievably enormous power.

But, to use a crude comparison, it was no different from a 5-year-old randomly swinging a sword ten times their size. Although the size of his internal power reached the sky, the sophistication of his technique was not particularly remarkable.

But Jang Ilso was different.

‘That guy is just strong.’

He was fast, precise, and even skillful.

The words Chung Myung said when he first saw Jang Ilso flashed through Baek Cheon’s mind.

-That is a self-made monster.

Looking back, Chung Myung had never used such expressions with anyone. Although he dismissed it as being out of his mind, Chung Myung not only said everything he wanted to say in front of the Shaolin abbot but even pointed a finger at him.

They should have understood when such words came out of Chung Myung’s mouth.

‘Obviously stronger than the high priest.’

It wasn’t surprising that there was someone stronger in the world than the high priest of the Demonic Sect. Moreover, the comparison was against a high priest who was already injured.

But, when you face the person in front of you, understanding it becomes a separate problem.

Baek Cheon glanced at Chung Myung’s face without realizing it. For some reason, he felt like he needed to check what kind of expression that guy was making right now.

“...”

And soon, all the strength left Baek Cheon’s body.

Chung Myung was smiling, with a face that said he was going insane with the fun he was having.

And so... just like Jang Ilso.

“Pretty amazing.”

Chung Myung adjusted the sword he was holding and opened his mouth.

“That Evil Faction guy gets to be proud.”

“Hmm.”

“But I commend you for climbing up there.”

Jang Ilso snorted and smiled.

“This... to receive praise from the renowned Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, I am unsure of what to say.”

Jang Ilso chuckled softly. The accessories worn all over his body jingled sharply.

Chung Myung’s teeth were revealed.

As expected, Jang Ilso’s leisureliness was excessively annoying.

It wasn’t because Jang Ilso was taking his time with Chung Myung in front of him. But the leisure was clearly from being familiar.

This was familiar to Jang Ilso.

Fighting in the hellish battlefield.

Someone trying to take his life.

The people on the other side were trying to grab his ankles, threatening to kill him without anyone knowing.

He was used to it.

The relaxedness possessed by someone who had won a thousand battles, even ten thousand battles. A realm only those who risked their lives in battle could attain.

Right, like... the past Plum Blossom Sword Saint.

Clench.

After clenching his teeth, Chung Myung lowered his body. His eyes turned cold and eerie. He looked like a small, wild beast.

“You cocky bastard.”

His eyes stared straight at the prey, relentlessly chasing it.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso thought as he observed Chung Myung.

‘Strange. So strange.’

These people were strange.

To put it bluntly, they seemed like they weren’t even sane.

He has dealt with many people so far. Among them were those who had been working hard in Kangho for decades and those who honed their skills religiously.

But even those guys couldn’t make Jang Ilso feel this way.

A perfect connection?

Did they have the insight to accurately understand the roles and capabilities of themselves and their colleagues without even having to say it?

‘How trivial.’

He felt sick of such things.

What really baffled Jang Ilso was that these people entirely entrusted their lives to the person next to them.

Did they attack with the belief that someone would block the attack falling on them?

Right, if it were to be asked, it was a possible task.

But not leaving even the slightest doubt within their movements wasn’t just a level of trust, but of madness.

‘What on earth have they done to build such trust?’

And...

‘That guy.’

Jang Ilso’s eyes followed Chung Myung like a snake.

The strangest one was that guy.

‘I knew he was great, but...’

That damn sword was beyond the realm of understanding.

It could not be fully explained in words; it was bizarre and poignant.

The sword of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon was somehow twisted. If he had to compare it, it might be as if he were wielding a large ax while performing the most sophisticated swordsmanship in the world or as if he were wielding chopsticks while executing the best sword technique.

A tense sense of discomfort settled in, as if his swordsmanship, internal qi, and body were all running as they should.

‘What if it all merges into one?’

What would happen if, one day, all these swords ended up in harmony?

“... that’s creepy.”

His entire body shook at the thought.

Just imagining it made him feel scared as if a huge landslide were sweeping him away. A huge smile spread across his face, and soon he began to grin, showing his teeth.

That guy... no, these people were all getting stronger.

After clashing arms, it became clear. These people would always be at the top of the Justice Faction. There were as many as five in one sect. On the day they realized their full potential, no sect would dare raise their heads in front of Mount Hua.

So how could all this not be fun?

He was given the chance to tear and trample on this infinite potential with his own hands, turning it into trash.

This was a pleasure that could not be compared to anything else.

“I thought you were all morons, but you turned out to be at least as good as tiger cubs. But that won’t be any good for you. I am not one to keep tiger cubs alive.”

Clank. Clank.

As if to prove that a death wish had risen in his heart, the jewels all began to cry out. The accessories were shaking in response to the increased qi.

“So die here!”

Swish!

Jang Ilso’s body disappeared on the spot. And without any time to be surprised, he appeared right in front of Chung Myung.

Yoon Jong’s eyes opened wide.

The reason he was surprised wasn’t because of Jang Ilso’s footwork, but because Chung Myung’s sword was touching Jang Ilso’s neck. As if he had predicted in advance that Jang Ilso would appear there.

Kakang!

The bracelet on Jang Ilso’s wrist clashed with Chung Myung’s sword. Baek Cheon’s sword, swiftly aimed at Jang Ilso’s back, was also quickly caught by Jang Ilso’s other hand.

“Slow.”

With a light twist of his wrist, Jang Ilso made the swords bounce off each other like toys.

Kwang!

Jang Ilso’s fist then moved towards Baek Cheon. Baek Cheon quickly raised his sword to block, but it was futile. Baek Cheon’s body was sent flying like a cannonball.

Simultaneously, Jang Ilso, who lunged at Chung Myung like a tiger, lowered his hand, which was bent like a claw.

Chaak!

Chung Myung moved back without hesitation. Although he narrowly avoided a direct hit, his front robe was torn, and five streaks of red blood spread across his chest and stomach.

The attack that narrowly missed Chung Myung ended up crashing into the cliff they were standing on.

Kwaang!

“Kuak!”

Yu Yiseol rushed through the rising dust cloud, slashing her sword horizontally. The sword, loaded with internal qi and wielded with all her might, was more powerful than any she had shown before.

But.

Kakakang!

Her sword stopped midair with the sound of metal clashing.

Yu Yiseol’s eyes widened.

As the dust cloud cleared, she saw Jang Ilso standing calmly, his hand grasping her sword.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso smoothed his chin with one hand, carefully examining the Plum Blossom Sword in his grasp.

“A good sword.”

“Euk!”

Paak!

Before Yu Yiseol could react, Jang Ilso’s foot struck her stomach.

Crack.

Yu Yiseol, her intestines turned upside down by the hit, fell, coughing blood.

“It was too much of a waste for morons like you to use it.”

Slowly, the dust cloud settled.

It was a perfect demonstration of everything the Mount Hua disciples had learned until then.

But that success was so easily thwarted.

Jang Ilso, who had nullified Mount Hua’s acceptance just by wedging himself between them, smiled like a tiger leisurely strolling under the languid sun.

“There is just one person here who is suited to use a sword like that.”

Step. Step.

Chung Myung, with a scar on his chest as if scratched by a tiger’s claws, held the sword backward and walked towards Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso burst into laughter. The moment he saw Chung Myung’s eyes, sunken and low, it felt like a spark was flowing through his entire body.

‘One never knows.’

It was difficult for him to understand why he felt so nervous.

Possibilities were just possibilities. They were like illusions that didn’t exist until one grasped them with their own hands and made them their own.

So, at that moment, Chung Myung couldn’t reach him; it was worth nothing more than a single piece of chess to him.

But strangely, he couldn’t take his eyes off that kid.

The moment he turned his gaze away, his entire body trembled with a sense of crisis, as if that sword would soon find its way to his neck and his head would be ripped off.

“Spit!”

What tore Jang Ilso’s attention away, which had been focused on one person, was a low sound from behind.

Yu Yiseol.

She, who had been kicked and sent flying, was coming back, spitting blood from her mouth.

‘... her body should have been entirely messed up inside?’

The attack was such that it wouldn’t be surprising if her breathing had stopped. But she was calmly walking?

No, even if it did happen, she hadn’t lost her will to fight at all?

It wasn’t just Yu Yiseol and Chung Myung.

Step. Step.

Baek Cheon, who had fallen back after being hit by Jang Ilso’s fist, also walked back with an expression that said nothing much had happened. Yoon Jong and Jo Gul were also approaching him with determined eyes.

Like wolves surrounding a tiger, they began to close in on him.

“Haha... hahaha... Hahahahaha!”

In the end, Jang Ilso laughed loudly again.

He never thought he would feel his blood boiling against such kids in such a place.

Not to Wudang’s Heo Do or Namgung Hwang, but the brats of Mount Hua!

“Jang Ilso.”

“Hmm?”

Chung Myung had a bitter laugh.

“You need to be careful. My kids are a little rough.”

“Listening to that...”

Jang Ilso’s eyes held madness flaring.

“Makes it feel like good news.”

The qi exploded from Jang Ilso’s whole body. His red long sleeves bulged and fluttered, and the accessories all began to jingle like they would break.

Even in the face of this overwhelming sight, the Five Swords didn’t back down at all.

Paat!

Five streams of light rushed to Jang Ilso without giving up.

The sunset light that rose from their bodies bloomed like the five petals of a red plum blossom.

“Do not get overeager!”

Hyun Jong’s voice rang loudly.

Hyun Jong rarely raised his voice, but now it carried a tremendous strength unlike usual.

“Do not rush! Do not let your anger take away your reason! You are disciples of Mount Hua! Believe in your sahyungs and hold your position!”

“Yes!”

Hyun Jong bit his lip. His eyes followed Chung Myung on the other side of the cliff.

‘Those people...!’

Hegemony Lord, Jang Ilso.

The disciples were risking their lives fighting against this enormous person.

He was well aware that the disciples had gone against many enemies, but this was his first time seeing everyone fight together in front of his eyes.

It felt like his stomach was being scraped with a sharp knife.

He now clearly understood what it meant to feel the blood go dry. Hyun Jong’s entire body felt like it was lodged on the other side of the cliff, but here.

But...

“Un GEOMMMM!”

Hyun Jong let out a loud roar at the movement that caught his eye.

And Un Geom, who was about to strike ahead, flinched and stopped.

“Stay back!”

“...”

“Are you even going to say you would risk dying by trying to help! Bastard!”

Un Geom bit his lip, his shoulders shaking.

But he held his position. Disobeying the orders of the Sect leader wasn’t an option for him at all.

Why did he feel like he knew how Hyun Jong felt?

Not just Un Geom but also other disciples would want to go help the Five Swords by cutting down all the Ten Thousand People Clan warriors who stood in their way.

But that couldn’t happen.

The strength of Mount Hua hadn’t reached the level needed to face the Ten Thousand People Clan. If they tried to force their way through, they needed to be ready to get annihilated. Just by holding on and maintaining their place, the disciples were doing more than they could.

Fortunately, but also strangely, the Ten Thousand People Clan wasn’t attacking aggressively.

‘Well, this could be natural.’

There was no reason for them to hurry. Those who came up from below were blocked by the battle of the absolute warriors, and they couldn’t imagine their leader being defeated.

Crack.

In the end, a stream of red blood flowed from the lips where his teeth had dug in.

He also wanted to shout, to risk his life and breakthrough this place of warriors and help his disciples.

But this was why it could not be done.

He was the sect leader of Mount Hua. There was no way for him to sacrifice other disciples.

The people he must protect weren’t the talented ones of Mount Hua but the weakest disciples of Mount Hua.

‘Which is why!’

Hyun Jong held the sword tightly.

What he had to do now was to believe in them and hold off the warriors of the Ten Thousand People clan.

‘Don’t make me regret this, disciples, for the rest of my life, please.’

It was a wish that was extremely earnest but couldn’t be said loudly.

Grrrng!

The cliff shook as if it would collapse.

Mu Jin shouted as he hit the debris and rocks that rained down on his head.

“Take another path! Watch your head! Move now!”

His face gradually contorted more and more.

‘Damn it!’

Right now, a battle was literally happening above them that any warrior would want to witness.

One blow, one hit, collapsed the cliff and made the sky cry. No matter how much the Wudang disciples might try, attempting to climb through them was like pouring oil on someone and jumping into a flame.

But that didn’t mean they could just hang onto the cliff.

Shaolin, Wudang, Namgung, and Qingcheng below began to take a major detour to avoid the battle happening above.

“Endure it!”

“Ahhhh!”

At that moment, a sword qi the size of a house flew in from above.

The moment Mu Jin opened his eyes and saw the sword qi coming toward him, a green figure appeared and swung a sword.

Kwaang!

“Sect leader!”

It was Byeok Hyun.

He shouted as soon as the sword qi was deflected.

“Elders, protect the disciples! We need to climb the cliff!”

“Yes!”

There was nothing for them to do.

Those who could move on cliffs, especially those with good skills, blocked the fragments of rocks pouring from above.

‘Above, if only I could climb above...’

It was that moment.

Kwaaang!

One side of the cliff fell away from impact, accompanied by a loud noise that seemed to turn the sky upside down. A large chunk of rock began to fall slowly, with a gnawing sound.

“STOP IT!”

A shout came from somewhere, and a person jumped up.

“Elder Heo Gwang!”

A white sword and enhanced sword qi were combined into one at the same time. What he released was a clean circle.

“AHHHH!”

The blade of the Taiji Wisdom Sword struck the falling rock. At the same time, the power unleashed by the Shaolin elders broke the rock into small pieces and shattered it further.

“Spread out!”

And he cut it again and again. Attacks that could rightly be called disasters continued one after another, but the unity of disciplined humans overcame it.

But.

“SAJAEEEEEE!”

Not everyone succeeded in overcoming it.

Those who lost strength slipped, and those who were hit by broken rocks the size of a human head screamed and fell.

“Euk!”

Mu Jin bit his lip.

“Go up! Just climb up the cliff! Do not even look back!”

“Sahyung! But Sajae...!”

“Just shut up and climb up!”

...

Tears of blood flowed down from Mu Jin’s eyes.

‘These damned Evil Faction bastards!’

This wasn’t the mindset a Taoist should be embracing. But a killing intent, which none had never been seen before, was constantly flowing from his eyes.

“I will definitely kill them! For sure! I will kill them all!”

After shouting this, he gritted his teeth once again and climbed up the cliff.

The extended blade grazed his neck.

Pack!

Only after the sword passed through the throat did the sound follow.

It was a swift sword that couldn’t be described as anything other than amazing. But the true value of the sword wasn’t limited to its speed.

Venerable Heo Do, who had lifted his sword sharply, retreated.

Trickle.

Even though he carefully avoided it, he felt pain around his neck.

“... Intangible qi.”

An intangible qi, which couldn’t be discerned with just the eyes, was unfolding every time the blade moved, entangling Venerable Heo Do’s body.

He had heard of it.

A swordsmanship that causes death without the victim understanding what is happening. The name of the legendary swordsmanship was the Evil Blade, said to cause death in the hands of a grim reaper rather than a human being.

“...Is that the Ghost King’s Twelve Movements?”

“Hmm. I see you recognize it.”

The Desolate Gold Elder nodded with a blank expression.

“It is a technique not easily handled by the sect leader of Wudang, but you have unparalleled skills. And above all....”

Pat!

The Desolate Gold Elder’s sword pierced toward Venerable Heo Do as if it intended to stab him. When Venerable Heo Do quickly twisted his body, the black sword qi that passed by him pierced the cliff and dug in deeply.

“...”

How far did the cut go?

“I use it a lot because it is a fairly efficient sword technique. I don’t really like wasting my qi.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes became distorted.

That sword was definitely difficult to handle.

“What is it?”

“...”

“I don’t mind taking my time. But aren’t things different for you? The longer it takes, the more your cute disciples get hurt. Does that mean it is alright?”

Venerable Heo Do bit his lip.

A clean defeat?

There was no way such a thought existed in his head. He was the sect leader of Wudang. Even if he died with his tongue stuck in his throat, he would never let himself be killed by people of the Evil Faction.

But the problem was what came next.

His gaze turned to those below.

Gritting their teeth, the disciples of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families struggled to climb the cliff. Rocks tumbled down on their heads, and every time an attack flew their way, they did their best to block it.

Hanging desperately onto the cliff.

“...”

The disciples’ faces were so contorted that he couldn’t bear to watch.

Covered in oil, bleeding from arrows, groaning with their qi drained. Below, at the bottom of the cliff, which couldn’t be seen clearly, the fallen disciples groaned in pain.

‘Since when did it end up like this?’

Something was definitely wrong.

Climbing the cliff. Right, that was the only path Venerable Heo Do could see then. He thought that would be the way to survive.

But was that really the case?

‘Survive?’

The opponent was conserving their energy at the top of the cliff. At most, they only brought pots and arrows.

The Ten Thousand People Clan, Lower District, and Black Ghost Fort. And there were certainly pirates remaining.

On the other hand, even if the disciples of the sects managed to climb to the top, they had already gone through enough battle. Still, they had to fight against the Evil Faction after losing their energy and qi.

‘Why did it come to this?’

When he entered this canyon, he could never imagine such a scenario. What on earth did he do wrong to lose his precious life so vainly?

For what?

“You seem to have a lot on your mind.”

“...”

Venerable Heo Do grabbed his sword.

A little distance away, Namgung Hwang and the Black Dragon King were fighting. The Black Dragon King was constantly unleashing a huge amount of Blade Qi, but Namgung Hwang was not backing down an inch; the fight was amazing.

But.

Crack!

“AHHHH!”

Each time they swung their swords and blades, the cliff was breaking, and the qi was slashing through everything.

“This...”

And it was the Wudang and Shaolin disciples who were taking the damage firsthand.

He knew.

That was something that couldn’t be helped. For the sake of those below, if they conserved their strength and got defeated, the Black Dragon King would immediately go to kill the disciples.

Then, it would all be over.

But even though he knew this, the reason he felt resentment was that, in a corner of his mind, he wondered if the people climbing up at the front would be given the same treatment by him if they were Namgung and not Wudang. This was proof of how anxious Venerable Heo Do was feeling.

Byeop Gye was of little help.

There was no reason why an elder of Shaolin should be inferior to the elders of other sects. Still, there was also no reason why the Thousand Face Scholar would be inferior to him. No, the shoulders of Byeop Gye were already heavy with the responsibility of handling the one foe he had to.

...So what on earth should be done?

Clench.

Venerable Heo Do changed. His eyes were bloodshot and red.

‘Jang Ilso.’

There was just one name that popped into his head.

Kwak!

In an instant, the face of Venerable Heo Do turned cold as ice. As if he had made up his mind, the white and black sword qi flowed out like water from the sword.

Sword techniques, like lines drawn on a canvas with a thick brush, swirled around Venerable Heo Do’s treasured sword, creating a vortex.

“Hmm?”

The Desolate Gold Elder’s eyes narrowed at the changed atmosphere.

The hat placed on the top of Venerable Heo Do’s head, unable to withstand the force, was torn off. He looked like an evil spirit with his hair scattered around.

“Hmm... if I handle this wrong, there could be losses.”

The Desolate Gold Elder raised his sword with a disapproving look on his face.

Woooong!

At that moment, the two qi, white and black, emitted from Venerable Heo Do’s sword swirled around each other in the air, forming a huge circle.

‘Taiji?’

The Desolate Gold Elder’s face instantly tensed.

The Taiji Wisdom Sword.

The Taiji Wisdom Sword, said to be the best sword of Wudang, was unfolding in the hands of Venerable Heo Do.

“Hmm!”

The Desolate Gold Elder slowly adjusted his sword and held it.

“... this is a definite loss.”

A bright blue sword qi rose from the blade.

“Would you like to see how great the Wisdom Sword of Wudang is?”

A cold aura emanated from the Desolate Gold Elder’s eyes.

Red Plum Blossoms bloomed on the cliff.

The qi emanating from the tip of Baek Cheon’s sword made this red flower bloom in the wind flowing over the cliff.

Baek Cheon’s eyes contorted as he channeled his internal qi into it.

Had there ever been a time before when he put so much effort into drawing the plum blossoms? This plum blossom, infinitely elaborate and eerily vivid, contained everything that Baek Cheon had accumulated so far.

But.

The blue-enhanced qi soared above the Plum Blossoms of Baek Cheon. A blue so deep that it seemed to freeze the heart, it bit into the plum blossoms like a huge wave.

The plum blossoms, which finally bloomed after overcoming the cold winter, withstanding the scorching sun, and enduring the blowing strong winds, were helplessly and cruelly destroyed in the face of the oncoming waves.

Baek Cheon distorted his face.

Kwaang!

But that wasn’t everything. The green-blue qi that crushed all the plum blossoms fluttered like a snake’s tongue and then extended toward Baek Cheon.

“Tsk!”

It seemed like the will at play was lacking, and he shuddered at the deep malice lurking in that qi.

Kakakak!

The Plum Blossom Sword, which had been pushed in with all his qi, collided with this qi.

“Kuak!”

A groan that couldn’t be suppressed escaped Baek Cheon’s mouth.

It felt like his grip would be torn apart. The amount of qi in that enhanced qi far exceeded Baek Cheon’s expectations.

‘E-endure...’

But at that moment, the blue-enhanced qi covering the Plum Blossom Sword trembled and writhed as if it had life, rushing toward Baek Cheon, completely surrounding the sword.

‘What?’

A trained body moved faster than the mind. Baek Cheon rolled on the ground as if he had fallen. The enhanced qi passed above him.

Sweat poured down his face like rain.

‘I feel like the qi is alive!’

What terrifying skill was it? While clashing swords with internal qi, the enemy converted it into an attack.

Of course, not an impossible task.

But wasn’t Jang Ilso fighting not just Baek Cheon now?

Kuku!

Baek Cheon, who had stuck his sword in the ground, quickly stood up. Without a moment’s hesitation, he kicked off the ground and rushed straight towards Jang Ilso.

When the sword stopped, the qi meant for him would target the sahyung-sajaes. That was absolutely unacceptable.

He knew. He had just evaded death.

If he had been a moment late in rolling over, he would now be covered in blood and cooling down.

So, was he afraid?

‘It is ridiculous.’

Dying wasn’t scary. What was truly scary was witnessing those who survived and died with their own eyes.

‘I will die first!’

That was how Baek Cheon of Mount Hua lived.

At that moment, he could clearly see Chung Myung flying towards Jang Ilso like an arrow.

“Chung Myung!”

Paat!

The surge of enhanced qi enveloped Chung Myung. But at that moment, Chung Myung’s sword unfolded into plum blossoms. Each one was vivid, as if alive.

But more overwhelming than its beauty was its scale. It was difficult to follow a single flower in the forest of plum blossoms that bloomed like clouds in an instant.

Kakakakang!

The blue force and the clear plum blossoms, which soared like flames, intertwined in the air.

Tat!

At that moment, Chung Myung slightly lifted his body into the air. He lowered the plum blossom sword qi and Jang Ilso’s force, pushing them together.

Pat!

Having lost the qi, he rushed ahead naturally but swiftly, like flowing water.

But Jang Ilso was running towards him as if he had anticipated Chung Myung’s move.

“Haha!”

Jang Ilso unfolded his arms wide, and his wide sleeves flapped like red wings.

Paat!

A slash like a beam of light followed.

The moment Chung Myung’s sword, with its red qi, flew in, Jang Ilso’s floating sleeve sharpened like a sword.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

Jang Ilso’s sleeve and Chung Myung’s sword collided in the air. The sleeve and the sword touched, but strangely enough, it was like swords colliding with one another.

“Good...!”

Paat!

Jang Ilso stopped speaking and shook his head. The sword of Chung Myung passed right next to his face with terrifying force.

The extended sword was quick and strong, stopping in the air as if it were a lie, then gently turned around and aimed at Jang Ilso’s neck.

Jang Ilso’s waist tilted back. The sword of Chung Myung passed right in front of his nose. No, he thought it was passing by.

But at that moment.

Pat!

The sword of Chung Myung stopped again and changed direction in the air with the intent to strike down Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso was shocked. His body accelerated toward the ground.

Kwang!

Jang Ilso, who hit the ground with his hand, turned to the side and quickly spun in the air, dodging Chung Myung’s sword. At the same time, his feet fired dozens of kicks toward Chung Myung.

Kwang! Bang! Bang! Bang!

Chung Myung quickly raised his sword and blocked all the flying kicks. But every time they made contact, his body was pushed back one inch.

And Jo Gul, with Yoon Jong, didn’t miss the chance he created.

“EUHHHHH!”

Jo Gul flew in like a swift sword and slashed his sword at Jang Ilso, who was still in the air. At the same time, Yoon Jong rushed from the other side and covered Jang Ilso with plum blossoms.

“DIEE!”

Kaaang!

Jang Ilso’s bracelet and Jo Gul’s sword collided. Jo Gul bounced back faster than the speed he ran to attack. At the same time, the plum blossoms covering Jang Ilso from the other side became entangled with blue flames emitted by Jang Ilso.

Kwaang!

Yoon Jong was violently pushed back. Blood splashed from his mouth and trickled down his chin.

Tak!

The very moment Jang Ilso landed on the ground.

Wheik!

Yu Yiseol appeared behind Jang Ilso like a ghost and drew her sword out.

“Tsk!”

Jang Ilso’s body suddenly turned violently to the side.

Pat!

Yu Yiseol’s sword passed through Jang Ilso’s bent waist. However, Yu Yiseol seemed to have anticipated this, as she quickly retrieved her sword and swung it dozens of times in succession.

Kakakang! Kaang!

Jang Ilso’s hand blocked Yu Yiseol’s stabbing sword without missing a single point. The metallic sounds continued to ring like music.

Kwaang!

At the moment when the final thrust and Jang Ilso’s outstretched hand collided head-on, Yu Yiseol, unable to withstand the shock, was thrown back and rolled to the floor.

“...”

Jang Ilso slowly lowered his head and looked at his hands.

Both eyes were slightly distorted.

There was red blood.

Of course, it wasn’t a serious wound, but the fact that his hand was cut was significant.

The moment when Yu Yiseol was thrown back, she twisted her sword and cut Jang Ilso’s hand.

It was persistence that was truly ferocious.

This was something that could never be attempted unless one was willing to exchange a drop of Jang Ilso’s blood for their own flesh.

“... it isn’t about losing face.”

It wasn’t just his hand.

His clothes were cut and tattered here and there. His hair, which had been neatly organized, was also tangled.

People inherently had their own aesthetics.

It wasn’t the beautiful scene he had pictured, standing here looking like this, where he was supposed to be toying with them as if they were his prey.

Kukukuk.

That was when a sharp sound caught his attention.

When he turned his head, he saw Chung Myung coming towards him, scraping the floor with a dangling sword.

“How is it?”

“...”

“How does it feel to get bitten by a dog?”

Jang Ilso twitched the corner of his mouth and answered.

“Although you are a member of the Justice Factions, you work together and look proud. Is this even right?”

“Hearing something like that from a guy who pours oil and uses gunpowder makes me feel so embarrassed that I want to puke.”

“Hahaha.”

Jang Ilso showed his fangs.

Clearly, these were quite a dangerous pack of wild dogs. Tigers sometimes died from being bitten by wolves, and if they were unlucky, they could even get mauled by a pack of wild dogs.

But...

“Even if there weren’t five dogs but a thousand, they wouldn’t deal with me. Do you know what the reason is?”

“...”

“Because there is a very easy relative method.”

At that moment, an evil light poured from Jang Ilso’s eyes.

Pat!

Jang Ilso rushed towards Chung Myung with terrifying speed. The speed was so great that it made Jang Ilso’s body appear as just a single red line.

Jang Ilso, who rushed ahead in a single moment, struck Chung Myung with a bizarre smile on his face.

Wheik!

Blue flames spewed from Jang Ilso’s robe and attacked Chung Myung. Chung Myung threw his body back without delay, but Jang Ilso hit the ground and caught up faster.

Kwaang!

Behind him, the ground where Jang Ilso hit exploded. However, their bodies had moved a long time ago.

Chung Myung leaped back and thrust his sword at Jang Ilso, who was chasing him.

Dozens of blades of sword qi were released at once, aiming to pierce Jang Ilso’s body. But before the swords were fully extended, they collided with the enhanced qi created by Jang Ilso.

Kakakang! Kakang!

The sleeve that blocked the sword Qi made him reveal his teeth and twist his wrist. Chung Myung’s sword, which was aiming for Jang Ilso’s neck, scratched the bracelet and went off to the side.

Kakang!

Without missing that moment, Jang Ilso threw away his sword and unleashed three blows at Chung Myung’s wide-open chest.

Chung Myung, doing his best to use his left hand to employ the plum blossom technique, quickly blocked Jang Ilso’s incoming attack.

Kwang!

One punch twisted the wrist.

Kwaang!

The moment his fist collided with the sword, his left hand couldn’t withstand the force and was pushed back, striking his own chest.

And three punches!

Kwanng!

Jang Ilso’s fist exploded against the hand, blocking the path to Chung Myung’s chest causing him to cough up blood.

“Kuak!”

Chung Myung, who had been retreating constantly, suddenly kicked the ground, changed direction, and rushed forward.

But at that moment...

KWAAAK!

Chung Myung’s body rolled on the ground and bounced to the side. It was as if Jang Ilso anticipated Chung Myung’s reaction and jumped in that direction.

“So easy to understand. Really!”

Just as Jang Ilso was about to rush at Chung Myung, who hadn’t regained his balance, Yu Yiseol and Baek Cheon, who had been following him, leaped up and unleashed the plum blossoms towards Jang Ilso’s back.

However, instead of turning to defend, Jang Ilso continued his charge at Chung Myung, releasing enhanced qi strikes behind him. The lack of precision created an opening, and the plum blossom sword qi pierced through the strikes and lodged into his back.

It was an attack he could have avoided if he wanted to. But Jang Ilso didn’t even glance back. His eyes were fixed on one thing.

KWANG!

Jang Ilso hit the ground hard and swung his hand, attacking Chung Myung, who was barely able to stand. He clashed head-on with Chung Myung’s sword, ready for the confrontation.

Kakakakaka!

The Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword bent precariously as if it would break, and Chung Myung was pushed backward. The fingernails, enhanced with qi, extended in front of his face, drawing lines across it.

In an instant, streams of blood appeared on Chung Myung’s face.

“Hahahah!”

Jang Ilso burst into laughter and continued to push Chung Myung forward.

“Chung Myung!”

Chung Myung’s body, propelled by that force, was quickly pushed off the cliff. As if Jang Ilso wasn’t satisfied with that, he chased after and threw Chung Myung off the cliff.

The two individuals, one wielding a sword and the other using his hands, began to fall off the cliff simultaneously.

Kakakakang!

“Hahahahaha!”

Madness and hostility seethed like lava in the eyes of the two as they tangled together and plummeted downward.

The feet didn’t touch the ground. The body was falling at an eerie pace.

But neither of them paid any attention to that.

Swish!

Chung Myung’s sword split into a dozen forms. Each sword was filled with killing intent, all aimed at Jang Ilso.

“Ha!”

For a moment, Jang Ilso’s sneer was met with tremendous power.

Slash!

Jang Ilso’s hand swung in one motion and tore apart the flying sword qi. At the same time, he quickly rushed in, aiming for Chung Myung.

He couldn’t back down.

This wasn’t a place where feet touched the ground. No matter how great his abilities were, he couldn’t move freely in the air while falling.

The boldness of Chung Myung and the foundation of all courage were bound to be halved in this position.

But there was no hesitation in Chung Myung’s eyes.

He stretched out his sword towards Jang Ilso, who was flying at him as if he would take his breath away at any moment.

Crack!

The sword struck with a sound like it was piercing metal. But this alone couldn’t block Jang Ilso’s attacks.

But at that moment!

Prrrr!

Chung Myung’s sword began to tremble from the handle. The small vibration grew louder, and soon, the entire sword shook left and right.

Pat!

The sword swung in one swipe, expanding the force of Jang Ilso and eventually tearing it to pieces.

Jang Ilso was shocked.

This wasn’t something even skilled people would have ever seen before. Was it possible in reality to cut through enhanced qi like this?

But he didn’t have time to be surprised.

The tip of Chung Myung’s sword, which had deflected it, was drawing out small plum blossoms at that moment. As if time had sped up, the plum blossoms, which had bloomed at a blinding speed, rushed to Jang Ilso’s face.

A small, humble flower.

But what was inside was even more fatal. Jang Ilso didn’t dare to downplay this and waved his hand to block them.

Crackle!

Jang Ilso grabbed the plum blossoms and crushed them. In the face of overwhelming violence, the flowers seemed delicate and insignificant.

But at that moment, Jang Ilso saw three flowers flying again.

Some were fast and swift, others were soft and fluid, and still others were powerful.

They were all the same, yet they were never the same flower.

“Trick or treat!”

In this case, Jang Ilso swung his hand at once, and all three flowers were torn apart. His strength, like a blue flame, burned the crushed flowers to a pale blue color.

But that wasn’t all.

Immediately after that, Jang Ilso saw twelve flying flowers.

And after that, more flowers bloomed.

Dozens, hundreds of flowers. And in the end, thousands of flowers.

In the air of this terrible valley where not a single blade of grass could be found, this sword qi, like red plum blossoms, bloomed in abundance.

The Plum Blossom Sword Movement’s Plum Blossom Chaotic Nine States.

The Plum Blossoms created by Chung Myung spread around the world.

“Kuak!”

Even Jang Ilso couldn’t help but let out a voice of awe in the face of this unique swordsmanship. All he could see were the plum blossoms.

Jang Ilso, with a ferocious killing intent, looked with shining eyes, clenching both fists. Immediately after, these black punches came.

A series of them, so many that he couldn’t make out individual ones.

The fists were thrown hundreds or thousands of times, covering the entire sky in blackness.

Sura’s Killing Fist, Array of Heavenly Thunder.

“Haaap!”

“AHH!”

The distance was just five feet.

A distance one could reach if they just extended the sword. A distance where one could crush another with a punch.

At that distance, the cloud of flowers covering the sky and the clouds of lightning collided head-on.

Kwaaang!

Those who were desperately climbing the cliff also stopped their hands and feet and opened their mouths.

Those who were shooting arrows could no longer reach from below, and even the Mount Hua and Ten Thousand People Clan pushed each other away.

That scene even captured the attention of the sect leaders, who were fighting with everything they had.

‘What is that...?’

Namgung Hwang’s eyes trembled.

This wasn’t a spectacle of nature.

Where in the world would one see lightning and flowers clash with one another?

“... Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon?”

A groan-like voice escaped from Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

It wasn’t shocking for Jang Ilso to be so strong. Due to the nature of the Evils Faction, only those who are strong survive.

If the Ten Thousand People Clan and Jang Ilso had been a little weaker, the other sects would have already absorbed them.

Jang Ilso was the one who created the Ten Thousand People Clan from among those ghosts seeking fights and raised them as one of the Shenzhou Five Clans.

That is why he is called the Hegemony Lord.

Suppose the Ten Thousand People Clan’s strength had been even a little stronger than it was now. In that case, no one might have been able to deny the fact that Jang Ilso was the foremost among the Evil Faction leaders.

But Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon was different.

‘How did that guy match against the Hegemony Lord?’

It made no sense to say they were equal.

But there was no way he could deny what he was seeing. Right now, Chung Myung was clearly fighting on equal footing with Jang Ilso.

It seemed as if those countless flowers were sucking away Namgung Hwang’s soul.

‘What is that sword...’

How could he know?

This sword was the Plum Blossom Sword of Mount Hua, recreated in Kangho for the first time in 100 years.

Kwaaang!

The lightning and flowers caused a huge explosion. Torn lightning and crushed flowers flew all around, and Chung Myung and Jang Ilso were thrown back at the same time.

“Avoid it!”

“It is dangerous!”

The disciples of the Nine Great Sects screamed at the fearful fragments of qi that attacked them.

In the meantime, some people, unable to take their eyes off the fierce battle, saw it clearly.

Chung Myung and Jang Ilso were thrown back but then kicked the air as if it were solid ground and rushed toward one another faster than the speed at which they had bounced off.
