“I will get treatment. I will really get treatment. But not now. Let us organize the other things first.”

“Is it urgent?”

“Rather than urgent... because it is something I have to do.”

Seeing the look in Chung Myung’s eyes, Tang Gunak hesitated in displeasure but eventually reluctantly nodded.

“I understand. Just remember one thing. Your body isn’t just yours. That is what leading people is all about.”

“...”

“Why do you look at me like that?”

“No, well...”

Chung Myung scratched the back of his head.

“It is nothing.”

This was something Chung Myung heard quite often in the past.

-Yah! Do you think your body is yours alone!

‘Nagging...’

He never thought he would hear those obvious naggings again here. Perhaps because he had just experienced something urgent, the tip of his nose felt numb.

“Fine. How was it, Jang Ilso and all?”

“... he is a snake.”

“A big snake at that. The world calls giant snakes dragons.”

“That dragon can freeze to death. Right, he is a snake.”

Chung Myung glared at Jang Ilso’s retreating carriage, now just a faint dot in the distance.

‘Do as much as you want now.’

But the next time they met, that smile would vanish.

“Tsk.”

Chung Myung briefly clicked his tongue and turned around in frustration.

“Let us climb up. The sect leader is waiting there.”

“I understand. But what happened? Looking at your condition, it seems like a big fight. Why did Jang Ilso just retreat? He didn’t seem to be harmed enough to retreat.”

“That...”

Chung Myung searched for words, scratching the back of his head, and sighed.

“It is a bit complicated to explain.”

Tang Gunak’s eyes were filled with doubt.

“Chung Myung!”

The Five Swords, who had been waiting anxiously, were startled by the sight of Chung Myung climbing the cliff again and running towards him.

They had said they would come, but they had no choice but to wait there because Chung Myung insisted that if they all went, it would lead to another fight. And he wasn’t the type to be stopped, even if they went along.

“Is it alright? Did that bastard Jang Ilso try something again?”

“What the hell! If you don’t want to be fooled, at least keep your mouth shut!”

“... I really don’t get what you are.”

After examining Chung Myung’s physical condition, they all took a deep breath, relieved to find no new injuries.

“What are you even doing?”

“Nothing. I was upset to just let him go, so I decided to at least shout out.”

“... insane bastard, please...”

Baek Cheon held his throbbing head. At that moment, Hyun Jong approached from behind them.

Chung Myung spoke awkwardly as he looked at Hyun Jong, who was right in front of him.

“Sect leader...”

Hyun Jong bit his lip tightly.

Looking at Chung Myung’s face, he felt as if his insides were burning and he was being sliced into pieces.

He placed his trembling hands on both sides of Chung Myung’s shoulders.

“... you did great.”

Still, he bowed his head slightly. It was difficult to see Chung Myung’s face.

“Really... you really worked hard... I have no shame...”

He could do nothing because the Ten Thousand People Clan blocked them. He believed the best choice was to reduce the damage to the other disciples, but seeing Chung Myung in front of him, even those words felt useless.

Was it right to force this child to make sacrifices to protect others?

“I am...”

At that moment, Chung Myung held Hyun Jong’s hand, which was on his shoulder.

“You were great.”

“...”

“If the sect leader had been excited, everyone wouldn’t have been safe. Thanks to the sect leader being there, I could fight in peace.”

Hyun Jong couldn’t bear to say anything more and closed his eyes tightly.

‘Damned boy.’

Why did he think of others even in such a situation? The person who suffered the most here was none other than Chung Myung himself.

‘This kid has been through this hell every time.’

It made him think again.

The fact that Mount Hua might have been created by the blood shed by Chung Myung. Hyun Jong’s insides burned as he faced a reality that he couldn’t ignore.

“I...”

“Sect leader. Lord Tang has come.”

A deep sigh escaped Hyun Jong’s mouth as he stopped trying to say something.

When he raised his head, he saw Tang Gunak standing a little behind. As Chung Myung moved to the side to clear the way, Hyun Jong calmly greeted the Tang family head.

“It has been a while since I saw you, Lord Tang.”

“I greet you, sect leader.”

Tang Gunak politely bowed.

“Sorry. I arrived too late...”

“No. Thank you for coming here. But how did you know...”

“I received a message from Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

When Hyun Jong turned as if to check, Chung Myung scratched the back of his head with a flustered look on his face.

“Something was happening in a delicate situation. If you make a fuss, you will lose strength, but it is better than being trapped in a dangerous time. I am sorry for not telling you in advance.”

“No, no.”

Hyun Jong shook his head.

It never occurred to him that the situation was strange until he came here. So it would have been difficult to inform.

‘We are holding his ankles.’

Both he and the current Mount Hua were still insufficient for Chung Myung.

“I heard about the situation on the way here, Alliance leader.”

Hyun Jong sighed softly at those words. This was because he felt that Tang Gunak’s voice was colder and stiffer than usual.

“...where were you?”

Tang Gunak bit his lip and asked again.

“Where are the others now?”

“Ugh...”

“Sajae, you alright?”

Those who came back down were taking care of the injured. Every time they saw the corpses around, an indescribable look of devastation appeared on their faces.

“S-sahyung...”

One of the Wudang disciples, who was injured and writhing, grabbed the arm of the helper and squeezed out a voice.

“Evil... Evil Faction bastards...”

“...”

They couldn’t bear to answer. The person who closed their eyes tightly turned their head and answered briefly.

“First, handle your body state first.”

“...S-sahyung...”

“Move to the ship.”

“Yes.”

Those carrying the wounded quickly moved to the anchored ship.

Jin Hyun, who was watching this, closed his eyes without realizing it.

The agreement was based on sacrifice. Those who claim the name of righteousness and justice must know how to abandon their lives for the sake of justice. That is what they heard.

Then... what had all these people here died for?

They failed to do their job, and they failed to stay right.

So what else did the remaining people protect? In the end, all those alive at the end had pitifully saved their lives.

“Hurry!”

Namgung Hwang’s voice came out cold.

“We must finish the settlement before the sun sets!”

“Yes.”

The members of the Namgung family responded, but unlike when they first arrived, their voices lacked strength.

Of course, Namgung Hwang couldn’t blame them either.

Clench.

He clenched his fist and looked around. He saw the survivors of the Black Dragon pirates glaring at him as if watching.

His teeth ground together.

Those hateful bastards were laughing at them, but Namgung Hwang couldn’t do anything about it. They had already lost. In a situation like that, taking out his anger on them would only make him look more foolish.

‘Yangtze River.’

There might be no need for a treaty.

Because he would not step on the Yangtze River on his own again. If he saw the Yangtze River flowing with his eyes, it was obvious that the humiliation of the day would come back to him.

“Lord, the settlement is done.”

“And the bodies?”

“... respectfully done...”

Namgung Hwang nodded slowly.

If he thought about it, the damage wasn’t so severe. Although those who fell down the cliff sustained serious injuries, the actual number of those who died wasn’t large.

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were fixed on one place.

The back of a person who was busy pushing away fallen rocks and tending to the injured.

It wasn’t often for him to express gratitude to others, but at this moment, he truly felt grateful to the man. If he hadn’t kept the Black Dragon pirates in check from below, all those who had fallen and been injured would have been dead at the hands of those Black Dragon pirates.

“... was it Hae Yeon?”

“Yes. The one who walked with Mount Hua here...”

“Is it Mount Hua again?”

Why did it end up like this?

Clearly, they were all full of spirit when they entered. But now he felt like a dog with its tail between its legs, heading home helplessly.

Namgung Hwang suddenly looked at the culprit of the incident.

Venerable Heo Do.

He could clearly see him taking care of the Wudang disciples and putting them on the ship.

Clench.

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth as he spoke.

“Now, the entire world will point its fingers at us.”

“Lord...”

“How on this world do I bear such humiliation?”

“There will come a time for redemption. It is said that it isn’t too late for the right man to take revenge, even after ten years. One day, you will cut off the head of that wicked Four Precepts Alliance leader and repay the debt.”

“... damn it.”

It was then.

“Hmm?”

Namgung Hwang turned his head at the unfamiliar presence he felt for a moment. Several people were coming down from the top of the cliff.

‘Is it Mount Hua?’

His face contorted at that.

He didn’t want to see Mount Hua right now.

Since he was human, it was natural for him to be grateful, but this feeling was different from that. As he was a rather shameful person, it was tough for him to look up at Mount Hua.

But... Namgung Hwang’s face contorted even more as he looked ahead.

The first thing he saw was the familiar face of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, Chung Myung. And the one following...

Namgung Hwang’s face turned pale as he recognized the man.

Perhaps there was someone there at this moment that he almost didn’t want to see in the world.

Tak.

He descended to the bottom of the cliff and slowly walked ahead.

A cold, cold face as if covered in armor.

Deer skin gloves exposed beneath and the wide green sleeves of the robes.

“Tang... Gunak...”

A voice like a groan came out from Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

Tang Gunak, the Sichuan Tang family lord, walked straight to him.

Step. Step. Step.

Tang Gunak, who was standing in front, slowly looked around and then looked at Namgung Hwang.

And his mouth slowly opened.

“... is...”

“...”

“Is this the choice of the Five Great Families?”

Tang Gunak’s face contorted like a demon.

“This... you morons who don’t even know what pirates are!”

Namgung Hwang closed his eyes tightly, overwhelmed by the unfiltered anger pouring out from Tang Gunak.

Tang Gunak’s icy eyes seemed to pierce through Namgung Hwang.

‘Why here...’

Why did Tang Gunak, who was supposed to be in distant Sichuan, appear on the Yangtze River? And why now, of all times?

Namgung Hwang’s fingertips began to tremble slightly.

Of course, everyone here probably didn’t want to face anyone else. They knew very well what they had done.

However, facing Tang Gunak and not someone else in this situation was, for Namgung Hwang, a terrible experience incomparable to anything else.

The Namgung family of Anhui and the Sichuan Tang family of Sichuan.

The two sects were the two keystones that led the Five Great Families for a long time. Naturally, the heads of both families had competed, cooperated, and even fought with each other for a long time.

From Namgung Hwang’s point of view, the person he didn’t want to lose to even more than the Nine Great Sects was Tang Gunak.

“Answer me, Namgung Hwang.”

Tang Gunak scolded in a cold tone.

“What on earth is the agreement you spoke so loudly about?”

“...”

“And yet you are the head of the Blue Heaven Namgung family? After you beg for your life from the Evil Faction, can you still talk about your people being the Blue Heaven with your own mouth?”

Namgung Hwang bit down on his lip until he bled.

What caused him the most pain wasn’t the rebuke of Tang Gunak. Even after hearing that reprimand, he couldn’t make a single excuse.

“Answer me.”

“...”

‘Was that life so important? To the extent of throwing the value of justice to the ground?’

“... Tang Gunak.”

Tang Gunak had changed. He wasn’t mocking Namgung Hwang. He was genuinely angry.

Although the Tang family had become a member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and their ties had become complicated, the Sichuan Tang family was still one of the Five Great Families. The indecency shown by the Namgung family, which was the head of the five families, made his blood boil.

No, it wasn’t just the Five Great Families.

“Answer me, monk.”

“... Amitabha.”

Byeop Gye, who received the gaze of Tang Gunak, couldn’t bear to face him and just chanted with his eyes closed.

Even after witnessing this helpless reaction, the intensity of Tang Gunak’s force didn’t diminish one bit.

“Ridiculous.”

A cold cynicism flowed through everyone’s hearts.

“I never would have thought that those who repeatedly emphasized that justice is more important than life would throw away their justice and act as if they weren’t devoted partners the moment they got the chance. Are you all still qualified to call yourselves just and righteous? And be still?”

Byeop Gye’s face turned red.

Even if he had ten mouths, what could he have said?

Byeok Hyun, the sect leader of the Qingcheng sect, which was located in Sichuan and had some contact with Tang Gunak, spoke with a bitter tone.

“... Please stop, Lord Tang. All of this was done by Venerable Heo Do. Namgung Hwang and Monk Byeop Gye are no different from victims of this incident.”

“Victims?”

However, there was a sneer on the lips of Tang Gunak.

“They are innocent because they didn’t come forward and shout?”

“That...”

“Let me ask you something.”

Tang Gunak glared at everyone with eyes filled with killing intent.

“Then why are you alive?”

“...”

Those words left Byeok Hyun silent.

“Save your disciples? Fine. That is totally understandable. But then, why are you alive with your feet on the ground? And without injuries at all!”

The strong voice of Tang Gunak burst out loud.

“What on earth did you people do while the young people of Mount Hua were bleeding and fighting? Who is it that should have been shedding their blood and fighting here?”

Namgung Hwang closed his eyes and took a deep breath through his nose.

Saying that he was unable to focus on winning because he was watching the situation was nothing more than a poor excuse.

He had lived this long and didn’t know it.

What it meant to fight for your life and how difficult it was.

Of course, he might have to force himself to make an excuse.

However, the scars that covered Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon had made his tongue stiff and weighed heavily on his lips.

“The reason the world tolerates your violence is because it believes there is justice at the tip of your sword. The moment you negotiate for your own benefit....”

Tang Gunak chewed over the words as if spitting them out.

“It is no different from being a thief. You moronic bastards.”

“...”

There was silence. Tang Gunak bit his lip, holding back a sigh.

No matter how much he cursed, he could not undo what had happened. Knowing that made him even more frustrated.

After barely calming down, he gritted his teeth and said,

“I declare this as Lord of the Sichuan Tang family. From today onwards, the Sichuan Tang family will never be called one of the Five Great Families.”

“Tang Gunak!”

“Shut up, you damn bastard.”

When Namgung Hwang shouted in shock, Tang Gunak snarled back like a tiger.

“What you started out of envy and jealousy has ended in fear and cowardice.”

Tang Gunak glared at everyone around with cold eyes and then turned.

“Get out of here. Now, the Yangtze River is a place you cannot step on.”

Namgung Hwang’s body trembled.

A terrible sense of humiliation and unbearable shame took over his entire being.

But what was even bigger than that was the disgust with himself.

“...Get on board.”

“... Yes.”

In the end, when Namgung Hwang spoke weakly, the members of the Namgung family answered softly and headed to the ship anchored nearby.

“Amitabha.”

Byeop Gye also shook his head and sighed deeply.

“... Let’s go back.”

The Shaolin monks nodded slightly towards the ship. However, among those turning around, just one person was standing with his feet on the ground.

“...What are you doing?”

“Elder.”

Hae Yeon opened his mouth with a calm face.

“I will not come back.”

“...”

Anger surged onto the face of Byeop Gye.

He was now the acting abbot of the clan. Disobeying his orders was disobedience to the abbot and to all of Shaolin. There was no way Hae Yeon didn’t realize it.

But Hae Yeon gazed at Byeop Gye with unwavering eyes. His anger was short-lived, and Byeop Gye instantly lost strength at the sight of his calm face.

Everyone quickly climbed up the cliff to survive.

At that time, Hae Yeon was the only one who stayed below to watch over those climbing up.

In this terrible hell, Hae Yeon was the only one who put into practice the Shaolin teachings of compassion and altruism. By what right did Byeop Gye have to criticize him?

“... Do whatever you want.”

“Amitabha.”

Hae Yeon calmly chanted. Byeop Gye headed to the ship without looking back any further.

Byeok Hyun, who was left alone, opened his mouth towards Tang Gunak.

“Lord Tang...”

“Now.”

But Tang Gunak didn’t even glance at him and scolded him coldly.

“I do not want to talk now, so please head back, sect leader.”

“...”

A deep exhale escaped from Byeok Hyun’s mouth.

‘It must hurt.’

Tang Gunak was a person who understood the heavy pressure of having to take responsibility for the lives of countless disciples. Even such a person as Tang Gunak reacted with such contempt. How cold and abandoned will the reaction of the people around the world be?

Just thinking about it made his fingers go cold.

“... till the next time. Do take care.”

Byeok Hyun, who left only those words in a miserable mood, led the disciples to the ship.

Those walking to the ship, their legs held no strength. It looked quite different from when they came. It was natural. They had lost so much here.

Shaolin’s ship was the first to enter the narrow valley, followed by Namgung’s ship leaving the canyon. Eventually, even the ship of Qingcheng turned its bow, but the ship of Wudang, which was the first to be anchored, didn’t move and stayed anchored.

A person who was not on board was standing in front of Wudang’s ship, looking their way.

“That man...”

When Tang Gunak tried to move ahead, Chung Myung grabbed his arm and stopped him.

“Wait.”

Then he approached the person standing in front of Wudang’s ship. Venerable Heo Do, who stood alone on the land, glanced at Chung Myung approaching and smiled.

“Don’t be so upset, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

“...”

“Nothing bad has happened to the Heavenly Friends Alliance. You wouldn’t want to miss a time when the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families are criticized. How about being honest and happy instead?”

Chung Myung stared at Heo Do without answering.

“... why? Do you still have anything left to say?”

When Heo Do asked, Chung Myung looked at him with dark eyes and then spoke after a pause.

“Idiot.”

It was a cold day.

“If you have made a choice, grit your teeth and stick with it. There is nothing more foolish than doubting your own choices. And if you use an evil move, no one will criticize you more than needed, and hurling insults at the face won’t make you feel any ease.”

“...”

“Right, you did the dumbest thing in the world. You will live the rest of your life regretting the choices you made. And not only you but the entire Wudang and Nine Great Sects will be held responsible for that.”

Venerable Heo Do listened to Chung Myung’s words without changing his expression. A face whose thoughts couldn’t be understood.

“But at least...”

It was Chung Myung who twisted his face again and bit his lip. He paused for a moment and then spoke with a pained face.

“I might call you stupid, but I will not laugh at you for being a coward.”

“...”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes wavered for the first time.

Chung Myung sighed.

‘Shit.’

He felt so complicated now. He knew it was wrong. He definitely knew it was wrong. But... he could not help but understand the feeling of having to make such a mistake.

Because it was something he had regretted countless times.

If only he was a bit more selfish if only his sahyung was a bit more sly...

“If you’re comfortable, get out now.”

“Ha...”

Venerable Heo Do let out a low laugh.

His face was expressionless. But that expressionless face somehow looked different from before.

“See you again.”

“Will that happen?”

“We will see each other again. One day...”

Venerable Heo Do turned and jumped onto the ship. Once he got on the ship, it turned the bow and sailed out of the canyon. Chung Myung, who watched the Wudang’s ship move away, leaving long ripples, looked up at the sky as if he had a bitter taste in his mouth.

He couldn’t criticize that man.

If it had been Chung Mun of the past, Venerable Do would have been scolded without hesitation. But it wasn’t Chung Mun there, and Chung Mun could no longer shout now.

‘I still have a long way to go.’

It was the moment when Chung Myung, who shrugged his shoulders, smiled and was about to say something to the sky.

Stumble.

“...Uh?”

‘What?’

Why was the world spinning...

No, why was the floor rising?

“Chung Myung!”

“Bastard!”

Loud shouts from all around. But for a moment, all the noise turned dull as if listening underwater. And soon, all sound vanished from the world.

‘Dark.’

He couldn’t see anything.

His body was sinking endlessly.

‘Sect leader sahyung....’

The disciples of Mount Hua, who held Chung Myung, who was unconscious, screamed and called for his name, but Chung Myung couldn’t hear a thing.

The desires of countless people and the true intentions of countless people.

Those who tried to achieve everything even if it was risky, and those who didn’t want to let go even if they lost it all.

It was the moment when the great war, in which so many things mixed together, had come to an end.

But no one thought this was the real end.

This was just the start.

It was just a small spark that would one day grow into a fire that would engulf and burn the world.

The consciousness sank deeper and deeper.

Sinking down endlessly, the body and consciousness became separated from each other, growing increasingly distant.

The dream-like consciousness, blurred but not completely severed, was constantly searching for something.

‘Sect leader...’

Tak!

“Ackkk!”

Chung Myung opened his eyes and grabbed his head at the throbbing pain.

“Euk! Who’s the bastard that did this!”

“It is me.”

“...”

He rolled his eyes like he was losing it, but when he saw who was sitting in front of him, he quietly closed his mouth.

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“As people live, they might fall asleep. It’s not that I don’t understand that.”

“Yes, that’s right. It isn’t anything special.”

“Yes. But...”

The one sitting at the front.

An indescribable feeling of emptiness fell on the face of Chung Mun, the sect leader of Mount Hua.

“It’s not that I did much, but this is the first time in my life that I’ve heard of someone dozing off while being disciplined. You ruined man!”

“... it’s all your fault! Because Sect leader sahyung keeps saying the obvious things...”

“What now, brat?”

As Chung Mun began to lose it, his sajae Chung Jin, who was watching the situation, became shocked and shouted loudly,

“Calm down, sect leader sahyung!”

“You cannot lose yourself!”

Chung Myung smiled happily at that heartwarming sight.

He didn’t usually talk to them, but those bastards still respected him a lot...

“Then it means we will be hit later!”

“Don’t hit me if you don’t intend to stop! We are being hit twice now!”

“I would rather get kicked out of Mount Hua! But no one is doing that!”

“...”

He looked at the last guy. Even if no one realized it, that guy was upset.

Chung Mun held the back of his neck and sighed heavily.

“You a Taoist? Uh? Are you a Taoist?”

“Has our sahyung’s eyesight deteriorated significantly? I am proudly wearing the robe here.”

“You call yourself a Taoist just because of that robe, you idiot!?”

Chung Mun screamed.

“I sent you to subdue the bandits who are harassing the civilians!”

“We subjugated them. I destroyed everything just like you told me to. Then, instead of acting like this, you should praise me!”

“Right. You did great, my sajae.”

Chung Mun let out a chuckle.

“So, what did you receive from the people who said they were grateful?”

“Uh?”

“What do you think you got?”

“Ah. That?”

Chung Myung smiled shyly and scratched the back of his head.

“No, I was going to leave, but they kept saying thank you... they said if it wasn’t for me, many people would have lost their lives to those demons.”

“So?”

“So, they wanted to say thank you in return, but I thought it wasn’t polite to ignore their sincerity, so...”

Chung Myung had this awkward smile and glanced around.

“To thank me for my hard work, I received something.... Ack!”

Chung Myung fell flat on the spot, avoiding the hits that flew like lightning from the inkstone. The inkstone barely grazed his head and got stuck in the wall.

“A-are you trying to kill me now?”

“If you die from that, I wouldn’t have to go through this trouble, you damned bastard!”

Chung Mun now picked up the table in front of him and threw it.

As Chung Myung quickly rolled his body to the side to avoid it, both eyes of Chung Mun shone.

“You avoided it? Avoided it? Come on, today, either you die or I die! Chung Jin! Go and get me the whip!”

“Yes!”

“You. Go and try bringing it.”

“... yes.”

“I told you to bring the whip!”

“Yes.”

“Think carefully. Really carefully.”

Two people... No, Chung Jin, caught between a giant and an evil spirit, suffered, torn between this and that, unable to do this or that. Then he said while looking at Chung Mun.

“Sa... sect leader sahyung.”

“What?”

“I think... I think I got cramps in my legs from kneeling for so long, so send another....”

Chung Mun glared at him, but Chung Jin just lowered his head and looked away.

‘Authority is far; fists are close.’

If Chung Myung’s calf was caught from afar, it was obvious that Chung Jin’s chin would catch fire. Even if he got hit, it would be better to get hit by Chung Mun. That person beat him without mercy, so the situation would be more dire.

“Eukk.”

Seeing the sajaes all avoiding his gaze, Chung Mun groaned.

“They are already devastated civilians from the attacks of the demons! You couldn’t give the money and come back. Instead, you take their money?”

“Then do you want me to not accept their sincerity? No way. Ignoring their sincerity is not polite. Hahaha.”

“Kuak.”

Chung Mun grabbed the back of his head, which was hurting.

“S-sect leader sahyung!”

“Calm down! And then you can move!”

“This hasn’t happened for just a day or two, so why are you getting so angry?”

That bastard has been wanting a hit for a while now!

He would see him now.

Chung Myung glanced at his sajaes. Unable to control his anger, Chung Mun stretched out his hand, but when he realized that he had thrown the inkstone a moment ago, he shouted at Chung Myung.

“Water! Refreshing cold water!”

“Yes!”

Chung Myung quickly answered and turned his head.

“What are you doing? Run, you bastards!”

“You go, go! You bastard! You... euk...”

“Ehhh, I told you to calm down.”

“Now. Take a deep breath. Slowly. Slowly. That is right.”

When Chung Mun began to fall back with a clatter, Chung Myung quickly ran out.

He scooped up the water in an instant and held out a glass. Chung Mun took it and tried to gulp it down at once but then flinched.

“...”

He looked down with a blank face and saw steam rising from the glass.

“... Chung Myung.”

“Yes?”

“... why is this hot?”

“Uh... I was afraid that if you eat something cold in a hurry, you will get sick. Did I do well?”

“...”

“...”

There was an emptiness in Chung Mun’s eyes.

He could not kill this one.

Oh, Heavenly Father, why must he suffer through this?

He moved his lips as if he wanted to say something but soon stopped and shook his head helplessly.

“Chung Myung.”

“Yes, sahyung?”

“Why did you take the money?”

“No, well... they said they would give...”

“Speak honestly!”

“Ugh.”

Chung Myung stuck out his mouth in disapproval and made a loud noise.

“Honestly, I don’t really get it.”

“What?”

“To put it bluntly, that’s something which should have been done by the officials. But we take our precious time to go there and help.”

“Yes.”

“Then isn’t it true that there is a price to be paid?”

“...”

“It’s also a problem to think that someone else will always come to help whenever something happens. In the world, if there is something that gives, then something can be received. But it isn’t like I received anything special. I just made a couple of pennies.”

A sigh naturally escaped from Chung Mun’s mouth.

“Was it that big of a deal for you to beat up those demons?”

“... that is true.”

“Right. Wouldn’t it be over in just one minute?”

“It is true; be it one form or one move, they would have been done.”

Chung Mun, who was looking at Chung Myung, who seemed not to get it, asked.

“Even if I get attacked by the demons, are you going to save me and then ask to be paid?”

“Eh. Can sahyung and those people be the same? Don’t worry. If sahyung is attacked by them, I will cut those demons into pieces. Those demons would die, you know?”

Chung Myung made a serious decision. Chung Mun sighed and looked at him quietly.

‘What do I do...?’

It wasn’t that difficult to consider Chung Myung’s actions as wrong. It might be a bit lame, but Chung Myung always followed his words.

But that shouldn’t be the case.

This was a Taoist sect. It was a place where he had to follow his heart. So what Chung Mun needed to do wasn’t to control Chung Myung’s actions, but to change his mind.

‘I sent him to feel how powerless it is.’

There was nothing more to ask for if such outstanding talent was accompanied by cooperation. However, the heavens didn’t give everything to one person.

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“Do you know why Mount Hua emphasizes justice?”

“Because we are a justice faction sect.”

“Then why do the justice faction sects emphasize justice?”

“... well, because it looks like it suits us?”

Chung Mun shook his head.

“Because we are people with swords.”

“...”

“We are people who try to get stronger through training. Of course, the training is also a way to realize the Tao, but the essence of those who increase their strength doesn’t change.”

“Of course.”

“And... when people hold a sword, they want to swing it, and when they have strength, it means to use it. It means that those who decide to wear a sword at their waist will inevitably pull it out one day.”

Chung Myung nodded.

What Chung Mun said was certainly right. There were very few people who repeated training within their own sect throughout their lives and then simply died quietly. One way or another, accumulated power was bound to be expressed.

“Who are those who receive that strength on the other side?”

“...”

“He is also a person, Chung Myung.”

Chung Mun’s eyes sank.

“Strength without direction becomes oppression and evil. The reason why an agreement must be established isn’t out of pity. It is to establish one’s own path correctly. The power which forgets righteousness will one day destroy itself.”

“Eh... so...”

Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders.

“You just need to defeat the bad guys.”

“...”

“Right, right. It’s alright if you don’t pay for the trouble. How much does that cost? I will be more careful from now on.”

Chung Mun sighed in response to Chung Myung’s answer.

As expected, Chung Myung did as told. But that wasn’t because he understood it, but because Chung Mun wanted it.

And this wasn’t what Chung Mun wanted.

If Chung Myung wanted to live with the sword of Mount Hua, that would have been enough, but what Chung Mun wanted from him was to stand upright as a Taoist.

Even if it was an incredibly difficult task.

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“Right, you can forget it all. But remember this one thing.”

“Yes.”

“You cannot do everything on your own.”

“...”

“No matter how strong you become, no matter how great you reach, there are some things you cannot do alone. If you find yourself in such a situation someday, you too might desperately need someone’s help.”

“Me?”

Chung Myung tilted his head as if he didn’t understand.

It might be difficult to understand for the current Chung Myung.

Those who had never lost, those who had not experienced despair due to a keen sense of inadequacy, could not understand the value of the warmth contained in someone’s selflessly extended hand.

“Someday, when you feel helpless, when you despair over what you cannot do, then you will understand. How did the people you helped feel?”

“...”

“If that time comes, can you promise that your sword will continue with justice?”

“Euk.”

Chung Myung still scratched his head with an uncertain look.

“Honestly, I don’t know, sahyung.”

“Right. It isn’t easy for you.”

“But well...”

But he nodded obediently.

“Yes. I feel it... if I realize that, I will do as sahyung says. But I cannot guarantee it.”

“Right. That is good enough.”

Chung Mun smiled and nodded.

“Now go and return the money you got.”

“... uh-that...”

“I know you spent it all on drinks. Take the money you saved and give it back without a single penny missing.”

“...”

“Answer?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung pouted, but Chung Mun smiled.

“Right.”

“Then, I will come back.”

Chung Myung turned. Seeing that, Chung Mun added softly.

“Ah, and...”

“Uh?”

“Please distribute to the civilians all the property that was stolen while robbing the demons.”

“...”

Chung Myung’s body froze like a stone statue.

After a while, he slowly turned around, despair on his face.

“H-how did you know...”

“Chung Jin.”

“... Yes.”

“Follow and keep an eye on this guy to make sure not a single penny is missing. Chung Myung, if you steal again, I will hang you upside down, so behave yourself now!”

“...”

“Think about which is better, hanging or saving money. Steal money, and you get hung up.”

“... ghost.”

“Tsk.”

When Chung Myung, now sullen, left the room, Chung Jin sighed and asked.

“Sect leader sahyung, aren’t you expecting too much from Chung Myung sahyung? That sahyung is different from us, right?”

“I know. It won’t be easy. But...”

Chung Mun looked silently at the door that Chung Myung closed and left.

‘But one day...’

The day would come when Chung Myung would also understand the meaning of his words.

That guy was Chung Myung, after all.

A warm smile slowly appeared on Chung Mun’s lips.

Chung Myung slowly opened his eyes.

What he could see was an unfamiliar ceiling with a faint light flickering from the lamp.

He lay there blankly, looking at the ceiling with a frown.

‘I am dreaming all the old memories again.’

He burst into laughter and got up with a groan.

Throb. Throb.

“Aiyaa...”

At that moment, a familiar yet unfamiliar pain surged through his body. It felt as though his entire body, including the upper body, was completely wrapped in bandages.

“No, what...”

When he touched his face, he realized that it was even covered in bandages. If the intention wasn’t to turn him into a jiangshi, it meant the wounds he suffered were that serious.

Chung Myung, who was laughing out loud, was about to take off the bandages.

“Hmm...”

“Eh?”

Chung Myung turned his head when he heard sleep talk coming from somewhere.

In a room lit by a small lamp, several people were curled up and asleep.

Jo Gul was on the ground, while Baek Cheon and Yoon Jong were leaning against the wall in the corner. Tang Soso and Yu Yiseol were sleeping side by side, sitting in front of his bed.

‘This is a sight I have seen before...’

Chung Myung scratched the back of his head.

It seemed like they couldn’t overcome the fatigue in their bodies and fell asleep after sticking around to take care of him.

It would be natural. Tang Soso must have been exhausted from treating the wounded along with Chung Myung while the others fought with him against Jang Ilso. The bodies of the Five Swords were also covered with bandages.

‘Foolish.’

Since they received the attacks of Jang Ilso from the front, they probably weren’t in the best health. Because that was what it meant to face such a warrior.

And yet, they were foolishly sleeping on cold floors.

Chung Myung glanced at the sleeping people, then got up. Just in case they woke up, he had to be careful.

“Kik.”

“Shh.”

Baek Ah raised her head at the sight of Chung Myung opening his mouth, and Chung Myung quickly put his index finger to his lips. Baek Ah understood, nodded a couple of times, ran over to him, and then jumped on top of his head.

Chung Myung quietly exited with Baek Ah perched on his head, closed the door, and looked outside.

‘It doesn’t seem to be a camp; it looks like they found some accommodation nearby.’

He glanced around the pavilion and headed towards the bushes ahead. After muffling his footsteps and venturing deep into the forest for a while, he found a suitable spot. Chung Myung sat down and gazed at the night sky.

The moon was unusually bright.

“Sigh...”

As he took a long breath and stood still, the face of Chung Mun he had seen in the dream came to mind. Chung Mun, overlapping with the moon, was looking down at Chung Myung, smiling.

“Don’t look at me like that,”

Chung Myung grumbled and pouted his lips.

“Ah, don’t look.”

However, seeing Chung Mun’s face still smiling, Chung Myung lay down on his back.

‘Euk! Aho... It hurts so bad.’

Chung Myung, whose face was distorted, ripped off the bandage wrapped around his face.

“This is embarrassing...”

A one-sided blow.

Of course, in his defense, there were many factors. But Chung Myung knew that nothing could be a proper excuse.

There was only one reason why all this happened.

Because he was weak.

Doing the best he could in the current situation?

That was just a weak thought. The important thing in becoming a warrior wasn’t about doing the best according to the situation but creating a situation in which one could win from the beginning.

‘Jang Ilso.’

Clack.

Chung Myung ground his teeth.

He did give him a blow in the end, but that wouldn’t relieve his anger. To put it bluntly, everything that happened in that canyon was under the planning of Jang Ilso.

‘If Chung Jin had seen me, he would have laughed endlessly.’

What he had to say was clear.

-Sahyung, I’ve told you repeatedly that war isn’t fought with just swords, right? Uh?

‘You are right.’

Chung Myung covered his face with one hand.

‘It isn’t enough.’

Everything was lacking.

If it had been Mount Hua of the past, it would have been possible to wipe out the Ten Thousand People Clan in one go.

But now Mount Hua was lacking everything. No, it wasn’t Mount Hua; it was something lacking in Chung Myung. In every way.

Unlike Chung Mun, he didn’t have the ability to lead people and unite them.

He didn’t have a brain as sharp as Chung Jin.

Even his strength was trivial compared to the Plum Blossom Sword Saint of the past.

If he had regained even half of his past strength, the aspect of war would have been completely different. And if his past strength had been entirely restored, the war would have been completely different.

The leadership ability to lead a sect.

The intellect to seize victory.

And the power to make the victory a reality.

The three most important elements in waging war are all lacking. Excuses, like he fell for a trick or was betrayed, were nothing more than excuses for his shortcomings.

No matter how foolish Heo Do was, if Chung Myung had brought the Mount Hua of the past, he would have overcome it without a problem. No, if this were a place like Mount Hua of the past, Venerable Heo Do wouldn’t have dared to do anything like this in front of them.

In the end, his shortcomings led to the defeat.

Chung Myung’s eyes were dark as they looked at the moon in the sky.

This wasn’t his first defeat.

Rather, they suffered countless defeats. Because that’s what war was. It was no exaggeration to say that the war against the Demonic Sect was the history of his defeat.

But the defeat he experienced this time was different from before.

Right now...

“Don’t laugh.”

Chung Myung, who was lost in thought, simply pouted as he looked at the smiling Chung Mun looking down at him.

He was getting wound up for no reason as he looked at Chung Mun, who was smiling, unaware of what others were thinking.

But what could they do?

The image of Chung Mun that lingered most strongly in his mind was that smiling face.

Chung Myung slowly closed his eyes.

“... it isn’t that he is lonely or anything. These idiots over there don’t give people a chance to be lonely. So it’s not like that...”

Chung Myung’s low mumble permeated the dark night landscape.

“It’s just like that. What if I hadn’t come back alone? Sahyung... and if Chung Jin and the sajaes had come back, it wouldn’t be the same now. No... even if not that...”

Chung Myung closed his mouth.

After hesitating for a long time, he parted his lips, unable to separate them.

“If sahyung had come instead of me, Mount Hua would have been better off than now. I didn’t know anything at first... I think I understand it now. I was being stupid, sahyung.”

When he opened his eyes again, Chung Mun’s face was still smiling.

“... for real.”

Chung Myung’s face suddenly contorted.

“People are harsh.”

When you looked at that face, you could not say anything weak.

Of course, Chung Myung also knew.

This was just an illusion. If they were watching Chung Myung from heaven, they would be lamenting that he couldn’t lead Mount Hua on his own.

“Just... I made some noise. If there was sahyung, he would have cried and cried, and if Chung Jin had been there, I would have at least scolded him. But... by myself. Right. Because I am alone, I just have nowhere to talk about this.”

The sound of insects crying came from somewhere.

“Kik.”

Baek Ah, clinging to his head the whole time, looked at him worriedly. Looking at those black eyes, Chung Myung chuckled. Then he tapped Baek Ah’s head with his index finger.

“I am not worried about you.”

A sigh escaped from Chung Myung’s mouth.

This was difficult.

It wasn’t that difficult to get much stronger than he was now. He just had to follow the path he took in the past, but that couldn’t be the case.

That was why he could not defeat the Heavenly Demon.

He must become much stronger than before. He had to reach a point where no comparison could be made. To do that, they had to take each step with certainty, even if it was slow.

So he felt nervous.

He was afraid that Mount Hua would collapse before he was done. He was afraid that the Heavenly Demon would show up before he was perfect.

As the days went by, instead of getting lighter, his shoulders felt heavier. Although the kids had grown compared to before, the pressure was becoming more intense each day.

“So strange.”

Chung Myung, who was lost in thought, scratched his head.

“Now that I think about it, I haven’t been like that lately, but I don’t know why I am thinking about the Sect Leader a lot these days. I guess it is because I got beaten up badly... despite not being a kid.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“I understand. So don’t look at me like that. I am Chung Myung. I whine for today and will be fine tomorrow. Just... people need this kind of time.”

It was clear throughout the day, and the night sky looked clear, too, making the moon even clearer.

Chung Mun was a really strong person.

But at the same time, he was also a frustrating person. In the past, Chung Myung sometimes didn’t understand and found it frustrating that Chung Mun was so stubborn and upright in keeping to the standards.

But... he thought he understood it to some extent now.

‘Sahyung was so lonely, right?’

That was what it meant to be in the position of a leader.

No one could tell you whether you were walking on the right path. Since there was no one in front of you, you had to constantly ask, doubt, and agonize on your own.

And to Chung Mun, doubt and consultation must have been like a light that kept him and Mount Hua from heading down the wrong path.

He must have wanted to walk down the wrong path several times, but he must have suppressed his own desires. Not for himself but to lead those who believe in him and follow him on the right path.

It was only when he was in the same position as Chung Mun that he understood the difficulty and loneliness of the time.

That meant that even the past Chung Myung couldn’t fully understand Chung Mun.

How lonely his sahyung must have been?

Chung Myung, who was vaguely trying to understand what was happening, closed his eyes.

‘I am craving a drink.’

Where could he get alcohol...

It was then.

“Chung Myung!”

“Where did this bastard go again?”

“Find him, now! He might be drinking!”

“I will kill him!”

“...”

A loud noise broke the silence. Chung Myung sighed, looking in the direction of the sound.

‘Those fucking ghosts.’

It seemed like they discovered he was gone and came out in a hurry. Chung Myung flinched from all the curses and looked at the sky.

-See. Didn’t I tell you? I won’t be given a chance to be lonely. They are much better kids than I was.

Chung Myung got up.

“Tsk.”

After rubbing his butt, he grabbed Baek Ah, who was climbing up and put her on his shoulder, mumbling.

‘Don’t worry. Because I am strong, you can relax and watch. I will beat those bastards.’

It was the moment when he smiled to take a step ahead.

-Strength...

Chung Myung cast his gaze to the sky again.

-It isn’t strength if one doesn’t fall, but the strength to get up again even after falling is the best, Chung Myung.

That was what Chung Mun once told him.

Chung Mun’s face, which was looking down at Chung Myung along with the moon, began to disappear slowly. Chung Myung, who was looking at the scene blankly, soon had a calm smile on his lips.

“Such nagging.”

He straightened his back and looked ahead. His eyes showed much clearer determination than before.

It was okay if he wasn’t enough.

It was okay if it was not enough.

One could no longer fill a full bowl with water. Being lacking meant that one had more space to move forward.

Therefore...

Chung Myung walked toward the sahyungs, who were calling out his name at the top of their voices.

There was no further hesitation in his legs moving forward.
